# Handover

> Owns the project's memory: what was built and why, the technical decisions
> behind it, known issues, pitfalls, unfinished work, and what the next agent
> needs to know before touching anything.
>
> This file is **history and state, not rules**. Where a version entry below
> describes a colour, size or icon, that is a record of what happened in that
> round — the binding rule lives in `UI_UX_DISCIPLINE.md`, and the discipline
> doc wins on any disagreement. Repo mechanics live in `REPO_RULES.md`;
> universal agent conduct lives in `STANDING_RULES.md`.

---

## Cold start — the 60-second version

**CrewAssist** is a single-file PWA chat assistant for Singapore Airlines cabin crew: a COP/IFA/LMA allowance calculator, roster-PDF import, an Earnings page, live inflight menus + a print sheet, and a guided feature tour — all inside `index.html`, no build step. The owner is SQ crew; every change ships to their phone as an installed PWA.

The loop every round follows: **implement → extend the matching test block → bump `APP_VERSION` + `CACHE_NAME` together → `node tests/run-all.js` all green — AFTER the bump, never before: the release-stamp pins must validate the state that ships (1.45.8 ran it pre-bump and shipped four stale suites) → stamp README → HANDOVER → tests/README (one file at a time) → commit → push the session branch → owner reviews on the phone** (the onboarding stamp must read the new version — a mismatch means cache, not code).

Where everything lives:

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| UI + engine + CSS | `index.html` (one file; 4 `<script>` tags — 3 inline + 1 external lucide) |
| Formulas / behaviour rules | `docs/LOGIC.md` |
| SQ menu API quirks | `docs/API_REFERENCE.md` |
| Design log, version history, traps, ship checklist | this file |
| Test-suite contracts | `tests/README.md` |
| Browser smoke rig (real Chromium) | `tests/smoke/` — inside the repo since 2026-10-07 (owner order) so restores can't wipe it; also carries the `tailwindcss` CLI for `tw.css` rebuilds |
| Feature-tour beat script | `docs/TOUR-BREAKDOWN.md` — **sync + commit it after every tour change** |
| The owner's real roster/menu PDFs | `docs/_reference_rosters/` — committed reference fixtures (owner order 2026-10-07; formerly the gitignored-untracked `_inbox/`); each new file goes through the suite loop (parse clean or record honest flags) |
| Rates data | `rates.json` (network-first; devices with dev overrides keep them) |

Non-negotiables (each one has cost a regression — details in the sections below): work only on this session's `arena/<id>-crewassist` branch; **fetch-compare before every commit** (sandbox drift is real — see the next section); both version stamps bump together; every feature ships with its test block; every show/hide animates; **Lucide icons only, never emoji**; all `:hover` behind `@media (hover:hover)`; never fabricate data a source did not provide (unfetched flight times, TVL sectors, offline numbers); never derive calendar dates from `toISOString()`; never edit two markdown files in parallel.

**Standing rule (owner, 2026-09-16): any content that appears, hides, changes, transitions, expands or collapses in the app must be animated — never an instant show/hide. New UI (archive overlays, cards, trip bars) follows the max-height/opacity `transition-all` pattern; migrate remaining instant `hidden` toggles to it on touch.**

**Product status (owner, 2026-09-15):** v1.5.9 was **signed off** — its issues fixed (LMA out-date = the typed sector date; calendar weekday / Today button / fixed height). The v1.5.10 → v1.19.11 run that followed (calculator interface modes, Lucide icons, pill selectors, trip bars + Fetch all, the earnings archive, the refetch gate, scroll choreography) shipped release-by-release and was accepted; every feature through v1.19.22 was signed off 2026-09-19 and the app is **essentially complete**. What each version added lives in `APP_CHANGELOG` (v1.0.0 → current, in `index.html`) and the version-history sections below — this paragraph no longer repeats it. Two rulings from those rounds still bind: **Fetch may pre-tick Shuttle only when the transit is under 3 hours from scheduled arrival to report time (v1.40.0; the v1.5.9-era same-calendar-day-or-under-6h rule is superseded)**, and **never hardcode flight numbers** (no SQ478/479 or any other).

---

## In-Progress / Known Gaps

Owner considers the app **essentially complete**. Do not start a new slice without a request.

Honest leftovers (do not “fix” unless asked):

- **Publish rates to all installs** is still: export or edit `rates.json`, commit, merge via GitHub Desktop. No one-tap GitHub write from the PWA (rejected).
- **Icons, not emoji (v1.9.0):** the app carries **no emoji**. Two glyphs stay on purpose — `◆` in the elegant print-sheet header (the sheet takes **no icons**; the compact export draws and measures text on a canvas) and the typographic minus in the `A−` / `A+` type-size buttons. Where markup never gets a `lucide.createIcons()` pass (the menu viewer) or lands in a `textContent` sink, use the inlined constants `LUCIDE_PLANE_SVG` / `LUCIDE_X_SVG` (declared just above `lmaMealBadge`) instead of `<i data-lucide>` — a stray `<i>` there renders as an empty tag. If you add icons to `renderIfaSectors`, note that it calls `lucide.createIcons()` right after `container.innerHTML = html` for exactly this reason.
- **Toggle switches (`.ca-switch`):** every on/off control is a switch now — **Direct US** (`flag`, `ULR flight — {directUSMultiplier}×`), **Paxing** (`armchair`, `Deadheading — {paxingMultiplier}×`, **its own row**, not beside Direct US) and **Shuttle**. Multipliers are read live from `IFA_CONFIG`, never hardcoded. The switch is CSS-only: a visually hidden `<input type="checkbox" class="ca-switch-input sr-only">` followed by `<span class="ca-switch">`, styled by the `.ca-switch-input:checked ~ .ca-switch` rules at the end of the `<style>` block. **Keep the real checkbox** — `validateInputs`, `executeCalculation`, the Fetch auto-tick and `suggestShuttle` all read `.checked` by id (`{id}-ifa-us{i}`, `{id}-ifa-px{i}`, `{id}-lma-sh{idx}`), so replacing it with a `<div>` would silently break the engine.
- **tw.css trailing newline (audit FIX 7 — closed, won't fix, owner ruling 2026-10-15):** the audit's last fix would add a trailing newline to `tw.css`, but its premise was wrong — the file is **tracked** and rebuilt with the CLI (`tests/smoke/node_modules/.bin/tailwindcss -c tw.config.cjs -o tw.css --minify`), which emits no trailing newline; a hand-added one is reverted by the next rebuild. Ruled won't-fix: the file is machine-stamped generated output — one minified line, never hand-edited — and no browser, test or tool reads the final byte; making the newline stick would bolt a permanent extra step onto the rebuild command, reintroducing the exact fragility the hold existed to avoid. The CLI's output is canonical. Do not hand-edit the file to "fix" this.

## Behaviour contracts by subsystem (built Step 1 → Step 6; all still current)

- **Step 1:** PWA scaffold, animated starry sky canvas, theme toggles, gold paper-plane (origami dart) logo.
- **Step 2:** Onboarding UI, settings bottom sheet, developer mode. Subtle SemVer `v1.6.0` at the bottom of onboarding (`#app-semver`). Under the CrewAssist title: a hairline **the width of `CrewAssist™`**, then `AN UNOFFICIAL CREW TOOL` on **one row** (`whitespace-nowrap`), SemVer colour. **10 taps** on `#header-brand` (400ms) toggles developer mode. Settings reopen: scroll top, collapse rate accordions. Settings sections in order: Profile, Feedback and Support, **Allowance calculator interface**, Developer Mode (hidden unless dev), Data Management. What’s new is a **glass overlay** (`#whatsnew-backdrop`) after `showMain` (after onboarding for new crew; on the chat page for returning crew). X closes. “Do not show again” sits **outside** the glass card, **bottom-left** of the window (`pl-5` just before the card’s corner arc), checkbox + label on one row. **v1.35.0 delta rule (owner ruling, hotfixed same day):** What’s New shows ONLY the versions the crew has not seen — `APP_CHANGELOG` entries newer than `crewAssist.wnSeen` — and the sheet RETURNS ON EVERY LAUNCH until the crew taps “Do not show again” (merely opening or closing it never stamps anything; the checkbox is the only “seen” and writes BOTH `wnSeen` and `hideWhatsNew = APP_VERSION` — per version, so a future release’s news shows again even after this one was dismissed). The list is grouped by version headers and scrolls when long; Settings → Changelog shows the whole history since v1.0.0 (hotfix-3 redesign: filter chips, sort toggle, a What’s-new-for-you summary card, sticky version headers with NEW/CURRENT badges and relative dates, categorised rows). There is **no** chat What’s new bubble.
- **Step 3:** Chatbot, greeting, quick-action chips, regex intent parser (`menu`, `print`, `IFA`, `LMA`, `COP` / `total`, `tour`).
- **Step 4:** COP / IFA / LMA calculators with nested sectors, cascading dates, glassmorphic summary overlay. Coefficients live in `rates.json` (network-first fetch, last-good `localStorage`, `DEFAULT_RATES` fallback baked from `IFA_CONFIG` / `REGION_RATES`). Device overrides in `crewAssist.rates`. Developer editor (animated `.dev-rate-fold` accordions, collapsed by default):
  - **Base hourly IFA rate ($/h)** — paired ranks share one field: Jr. FS / Jr. FSS, FS / FSS, LS / LSS, CS / CSS, IFM. Save writes both keys.
  - **SDP buffers (hours)** — Out of Singapore (`sgBuffer`), Out of Station (`stationBuffer`).
  - **Overrides & bonus** — Paxing Multiplier, Direct US Multiplier, Turnaround Bonus.
  - **Layover modifiers** — bands `SDP ≤ t0 → m0`, `SDP > t0 AND ≤ t1 → m1`, `SDP > t1 → m2`. Editing `t0` live-echoes into the next row’s “> …”. Stored as `ifa.layoverBrackets` (`maxHours` + `multiplier`; last row `maxHours: null`).
  - **Turnaround modifiers** — same pattern with **TSDP** and four bands (12 / 14 / 18 / rest).
  - **LMA meal windows** — B/L/D start–end (`lma.windows` in `rates.json`; `LMA_WINDOWS` in JS). Defaults 07:30–08:30 / 12:30–13:30 / 19:30–20:30.
  - **LMA region rates** — B / L / D per region.
  - **Save** = this device. **Reset** = shipped `rates.json`. **Export / Import** JSON. **Do not write to GitHub from the app.** Publish to everyone = GitHub Desktop replace `rates.json`.
  - **Turnaround dates:** 2-sector = date above sector 1 and 2; 4-sector = date above sector 1 only. Layover still uses LMA dates. Defaults/cascade use `todayLocalYMD` / `addLocalDays`.
  - **Bubble-height scroll (v1.11.0, replaces the COP-only rule):** `scrollChatToBubble(el)` measures the new bubble against `chatContainer.clientHeight` — **taller than the visible area → put its top on screen** (`scrollChatToElNow`), **otherwise → `scrollToBottomNow`**. Strictly greater: a bubble exactly screen height still scrolls to the bottom. It re-measures on all three passes (10 / 150 / 500 ms) because cards are still animating open when they land. Wired into `addMessage`, `renderCalculatorCard` and the flight-verification card. A COP card is long, so it takes the top branch exactly as the old `mode === 'both'` special case did. `scrollToBottom` / `scrollChatToElNow` are the one-pass primitives; `scrollToBottom` keeps its own three passes. **Adding an LMA station still calls `scrollToBottom`** — that grows an existing bubble and the crew needs to see the station they just added (`mode !== 'both'` and `__caLmaQuietAdd` still guard it).
  - **Calculator interface mode (setting v1.6.0, behaviour v1.7.0):** Settings → *Allowance calculator interface* is one segmented pill (`#calcui-segmented`): **Default** (`sparkles`) / **Manual** (`pen-line`) / **Advanced** (`sliders-horizontal`), gold fill on the selected pill. Under it a header row — `#calcui-desc-icon` + `#calcui-desc-title` + `#calcui-desc-badge` — then `#calcui-desc` (the old `#calcui-helper` line was removed in v1.8.0). All copy lives in `CALC_UI_META` (`icon` / `title` / `badge` / `desc`): Default = “Smart automation” + a **green-pill badge** (filled star + “Recommended”, `.ca-badge-green` — owner ruling: gold-on-gold had no contrast); Manual = “Full manual control”, no badge; Advanced = “Advanced breakdowns”, no badge. The badge renders its star icon in `syncCalcUiPills` (`innerHTML`, never `textContent`). **Advanced exists only in developer mode** — hidden pill, `setCalcUiMode` clamps a stray `advanced` back to `default`, and turning developer mode **off** while on Advanced drops to Default and rewrites storage (`syncCalcUiDevMode`). State is `calcUiMode` (`'default'` | `'manual'` | `'advanced'`), persisted as `crewAssist.calcUi`, loaded by `loadCalcUiMode` (unknown value → `default`), painted by `syncCalcUiPills` from `initUI`.
    **What each mode shows** — `syncCalcUiCard` / `syncCalcUiAll`, applied to every `[data-calc-card]`, re-run after each sector re-render, each added LMA station, and on every mode change (so cards already in the thread repaint live): **advanced** = everything, unchanged. **default** = hide `.ifa-time-input` and `.ifa-us-field` (Direct US only — Paxing stays), and hide the LMA `.calc-lma-section` **only on cards that also carry `[data-calc-ifa-sectors]`** — the LMA-only calculator keeps its section (it would otherwise be an empty card) but loses `.lma-iata-field` and both `.lma-time-field` instead; Turnaround keeps hiding LMA independently. **Calculate gate:** `validateInputs` normally skips `ifa-fn-input` / `lma-fn-input`, but when `calcUiMode === 'default'` the flight number is required, so the button stays greyed until the crew types it (without this, every visible field was hidden and Calculate fired on an empty card → `$0.00` summary). `syncCalcUiCard` dispatches a non-bubbling `input` on the card so the gate re-evaluates on every mode change. **manual** = hide every `.ifa-fetch-btn` / `.lma-fetch-btn` **and** `.ifa-trip-bar` / `.calc-hint` (v1.12.0 — there is no Fetch in Manual, so a trip bar built from fetched airports and its progress hint would only mislead); flight and date are both `flex-1 min-w-0`, so they share the freed width evenly with no extra CSS. Hide with the **`hidden` class, never raw `display:none`** — `validateInputs` skips inputs inside a `.hidden` ancestor, and that is what stops hidden fields from holding Calculate hostage.
  - **CrewAssist calendar:** `#cadate-panel` is **opaque** cream `#F6F1E8` / navy `#0B1A3A`. Days outside **today−2 … today+42** get `.is-out` strikethrough (number still visible). COP/IFA/LMA can still select them; Fetch then `appAlert`s past vs too-far-future and does not call `/api/sq`. Inflight menu Pick Date opens the same picker with `caDateStrict` — crossed days are not tappable. Do not restore `input type=date` on COP/IFA/LMA. `showDatePickerUI` must not call `showPicker()`. Header is `Sat, 26 Sept 2026` plus gold hairline. Fixed 6-row grid (`#cadate-grid-wrap` 13.5rem). `#cadate-today` jumps to today. Month slide ±18%. Cancel muted gray; OK gold. Legend: Today ring / Selected fill. **Two** gold hairlines (1px `rgba(201,162,39,0.35)`, full width): `#cadate-hairline` under the header date, `#cadate-hairline-foot` between the legend and the Today / Cancel / OK row (0.75rem both sides of the foot line; the action row is `mt-3`).
  - **LMA out date:** next sector’s **typed** `#ifa-d` (not `data-arr-ymd` / destination arrival). Overnight NRT→SIN must not +1 the layover out date. In-date stays previous sector arrival local.
  - **Fetch date:** do **not** write `leg.depLocal` back onto the sector date field (that +1’d last-sector SQ11). Keep the typed date. A dead-code sweep (single-occurrence scan of every declared function) left exactly one unused symbol, `printStrikeAllergens` (the print sheet only ever used the `invert`/highlight path), removed in v1.16.1. `setFetchFace` / `finishFetchBtn` and the `.fetch-face` / `.fetch-btn-spin` / `.fetch-btn-tick` markup were **deleted in v1.14.0** — no per-sector Fetch button remains to animate. Loading is now a pulsing gold `.ifa-status-dot` / `.lma-status-dot`. If a spinner is ever reintroduced, rotate `.fetch-spin-ring` **inside** `.fetch-face`, never `transform` on the face (that made the ring walk diagonally).
  - **LMA dates:** `parseSqLocal` (wall time) vs `parseSqUtc`. Do not `new Date('YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM')` for local fields — Chrome treats that as UTC and LMA +1s in Singapore.
  - **Inflight Pick Date:** after OK, the Pick Date pill shows `DD/MM/YYYY` and stays selected until Today or Tomorrow.
  - **Flight/date row:** both `flex-1 min-w-0`. Labels and SQ prefix `text-[10px]`; values `text-sm`.
  - **Summary:** `showResultsOverlay` sets `#results-content.scrollTop = 0`.
  - **Trip bar + one Fetch all (v1.12.0):** under the pills, `.ifa-trip-bar` holds `#{id}-ifa-trip-route` (IATA nodes joined by `→`, `···` until fetched, first node from sector 1's `data-dep-airport` else `SIN`), `#{id}-ifa-trip-progress` (`2 of 4 fetched`), `#{id}-ifa-expand-all` and `#{id}-ifa-fetch-all`. **There are no per-sector IFA Fetch buttons any more** — the only per-sector button is `.ifa-retry-btn`, shown solely when that sector's fetch failed. Per-sector state lives on the card as `data-ifa-state` = `idle` | `loading` | `done` | `error`; `.ifa-status-dot` is gold + `animate-pulse` while loading, green on done, red on error. `done` sets `data-ifa-collapsed`, which hides `.ifa-edit-area` and shows `.ifa-summary-row` (flight / date / time / green check). Tapping the row expands; **any `input` inside a `done` sector resets it to `idle`** and re-expands it, guarded by `ifaApplyingFetch` so the fetch write-back does not count as an edit. `ifaKeepFirstOpen` keeps the first not-yet-fetched sector expanded. The helpers are `function` declarations at the top of `bindCalculatorEvents` (not const arrows) because `renderIfaSectors` is defined and first run further down and it repaints the bar; they read the DOM by id rather than closing over `setVal` / `syncLmaFromIfa`, which are declared later. `ifaFetchOneSector(idx, quiet)` is the old per-sector handler with `quiet` suppressing the alerts, and `ifaFetchAll` awaits it in a plain `for` loop — **strictly sequential, never `Promise.all`**. `#{id}-calc-hint` under Calculate reads `Fetch all sectors to continue (2/4)` or `All sectors fetched · Ready`. The collapsed row hides its inputs with `.hidden`, which `validateInputs` skips — safe because a `done` sector always has a fetched time, and editing clears `done` first.
  - **Fetch all arming + the date window (v1.13.0):** `ifaPaintTripBar` computes `fnReady` (every `.ifa-fn-input` non-empty) and arms the button only while `fnReady && done < count && !ifaFetchingAll`, toggling `cta-gold` on and the outline classes off — the same treatment as Calculate. Once every sector is fetched the button goes inert; any `input` in the sectors container or any date `change` repaints and re-arms it. Before the loop, `ifaFetchAll` walks every sector date and raises **one** `appAlert` with `Live flight information isn’t available for the selected dates.` if `menuDateSide` rejects any of them, making no request at all.
  - **The calendar floor (v1.13.0):** `caMinDateFor(inp)` derives the earliest allowed day from the field id (`-ifa-d<n>` → the sector above; `-lma-a<n>` → the previous station’s departure; `-lma-d<n>` → its own arrival). `openCaDatePicker` takes it as a 4th argument into `caDateMin`; the grid marks those days `is-out` and the day click, Today and OK handlers all reject them. Empty means unrestricted.
  - **Durations as `2h 15m` (v1.13.0):** `formatDurationHm(hm)` sits next to `hoursToHm` and is **display only** — the inputs and the engine still use `HH:MM`. Hours carry no leading zero, minutes do. The collapsed IFA row prefixes it with a lucide `clock` icon.
  - **The flight-time gate (v1.13.0):** `validateInputs` checks every `.ifa-time-input` for `length >= 4` **regardless of visibility**, after the normal visible-input pass. In Default mode the field is hidden, so Fetch is the only way to satisfy it.
  - **Sector pills (v1.13.0):** the count pills read `2` and `4`, and every `.calc-pill` is `whitespace-nowrap`, so the pill row never wraps.
  - **Animated collapse + pill/button fit (v1.16.0):** `ifaPaintSector` no longer toggles `hidden` on `.ifa-edit-area`; it swaps `max-h-0 opacity-0` ↔ `max-h-[1200px] opacity-100` with `overflow-hidden transition-all duration-500 ease-in-out`, the same pattern the LMA stations use on add — so the fold visibly animates, and `validateInputs` keeps skipping a folded card via the existing `!inp.closest('.max-h-0')` check. `.ifa-summary-row` still toggles `hidden` (it holds no inputs). `fitCalcPills()` shortens the `data-value="Turnaround"` label to `Turn` when `scrollWidth > clientWidth`, and is called after every `renderCalculatorCard` and on `resize`; in a layout-less test runner `scrollWidth===clientWidth===0`, so it never shortens there. v1.16.1 found why v1.16.0 still overflowed on small screens: the pills are `flex-1` and flex items default to `min-width:auto`, so a pill grew to fit the word and overflowed the *card* instead of itself — making `scrollWidth===clientWidth` and the check never fire. The pills now carry `min-w-0 overflow-hidden` so the pill is constrained and the overflow is measurable, and `fitCalcPills` also runs on `load`, `document.fonts.ready`, `fonts loadingdone` and three post-render timeouts so a late webfont can't ship the wrong label. The IFA/LMA Fetch all buttons drop to `px-3`, `text-[9px]`/`text-[10px]`, `tracking-normal`, `gap-1` + `whitespace-nowrap` so the bottom row stays inside the 85%-wide bubble.
  - **Trip bar panel shape (v1.15.0):** both bars are now **two rows inside one panel**, never one horizontal row. Top row is the route only — `#{id}-ifa-trip-route` / `#{id}-lma-trip-route` are direct children of the panel, `flex flex-nowrap overflow-x-auto`, so five chips get the full width and a route that still overflows **scrolls sideways rather than wrapping or squashing**. Bottom row is `flex items-center` holding progress, Expand all and Fetch all, with `ml-auto` on Fetch all to push it right and `shrink-0` on all three. Chips and arrows already carry `shrink-0`, so nothing compresses. `.ifa-trip-route, .lma-trip-route { scrollbar-width: none }` plus the `::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none }` pair keeps the scroll affordance out of the way — that is the only CSS added, and it touches no colour or chip style. The old IFA panel was `flex items-start justify-between` with a `flex-wrap` route inside the left column, which is what made chips wrap onto two lines and collide with the progress text.
  - **LMA Calculate gate (v1.15.0):** after the IFA flight-time check, `validateInputs` also requires a valid `^[A-Za-z]{3}$` `.lma-iata-input` and `.lma-time-input` of `length >= 4`, **regardless of visibility**, but **only on the LMA-only card** (`isLmaOnlyCard` = has `.calc-lma-section` and no `[data-calc-ifa-sectors]`). That scoping is deliberate and load-bearing: on a COP card these fields are fed by `syncLmaFromIfa`, which intentionally leaves a departure time empty when the next sector departs somewhere else, and in Default mode the crew cannot type them — a blanket gate would lock Calculate permanently. A station with Shuttle ticked is exempt via `lmaExempt`, mirroring the `if (sh && sh.checked) return;` in the visible-input pass above it.
  - **LMA trip bar (v1.14.0):** `.lma-trip-bar` in `lmaHtml` mirrors the IFA one — `#{id}-lma-trip-route` (first node `SIN`, then each typed `.lma-iata-input`, `···` when empty), `#{id}-lma-trip-progress` counting **stations**, `#{id}-lma-expand-all`, `#{id}-lma-fetch-all`, and `#{id}-lma-calc-hint` (`Fetch all layovers to continue (1/3)` / `All layovers fetched · Ready`). It renders on both the LMA calculator and the COP card, and `syncCalcUiCard` hides `.lma-trip-bar` / `.lma-calc-hint` in Manual.
  - **LMA leg state (v1.14.0):** each station carries `data-idx`, `data-arr-state` and `data-dep-state` (`idle|loading|done|error`) plus `data-lma-collapsed`. “Fetched” is **explicit state, not a non-empty time** — that is what lets editing a flight number re-arm Fetch all, matching the IFA rule. `lmaStationDone` = both legs `done`; `lmaKeepFirstOpen` keeps the first unfinished station expanded; a station folds only when both legs are done. `.lma-summary-row` shows `SQ 265 / 266` plus the IATA in a card. Any `input` inside a station resets **both** legs to `idle` and expands it (guarded by `lmaApplyingFetch`). `lmaFetchAll` checks every leg date first (same single-window error), then walks stations 1..n × `['arr', 'dep']`, skipping `done` legs, **strictly sequential**. `syncLmaFromIfa` marks a leg `done` once the IFA fetch has filled its time, so a COP card does not report `0 of 1 fetched` over freshly filled LMA times.
  - **Do not reintroduce a `data-idx`-less station.** `lmaStationEl(idx)` selects `.lma-station[data-idx="n"]`; the station `div` is built with `className` only, so `data-idx` is set explicitly alongside the leg-state attributes. Dropping it makes every LMA fetch return at its first line with no error and no alert — this shipped briefly and cost a debugging round.
  - **Fetch (optional):** each IFA sector has flight + date on **one row** (both `flex-1`, calculator card `w-[85%]` like inflight menu). **LMA** stations have no per-leg Fetch button either (v1.14.0) — only `.lma-retry-btn`, shown solely when that leg failed. Fetch writes the same hours / LMA IATA / in-out fields the crew can type. Uses `/api/getcabin` then `/api/menu` (first published cabin, JCL preferred). Block time = UTC arr − dep. Multi-leg menus pick the **unused** dep→arr pair (`pickScheduleLeg` + usedPairs), preferring previous sector arr as this dep; last layover sector prefers SIN. LMA dep only if next sector dep airport matches the station. Unpublished (101) may retry a sibling sector’s date. Cabin class is not required. Empty flight number does **not** disable Calculate (`ifa-fn-input` / `lma-fn-input` skipped in `validateInputs`) — **except in Default interface mode**, where the flight number is the required field. Do not auto-Calculate. A failed fetch must not wipe typed 101/net hours.
  - **Flight-number placeholders follow the pills** (v1.10.0): `fnHints` in `renderIfaSectors` — Layover+2 = `632` / `633`, Turnaround+2 = `852` / `853`, Layover+4 = `12` / `12` / `11` / `11`, Turnaround+4 = `136` / `135` / `994` / `995`. They are placeholders only (the `SQ` prefix is a separate absolutely-positioned span); nothing reads them. LMA equivalents: station IATA `e.g. BNE`, both LMA flight fields `265`.
  - **Sector dates cascade like LMA** (v1.12.0 supersedes the v1.10.0 rule): sector *i* defaults to `addLocalDays(todayLocalYMD(), i - 1)`. Each hidden date input carries `ifa-date-cascade` + `data-idx`; a `change` on the IFA sectors container runs `cascadeIfaDates(fromIdx)`, which walks `fromIdx + 1 … count` and **rewrites every date below to `previous + 1` unconditionally** — not only the ones that were out of order. The sector that was typed, and everything above it, is untouched. That is the owner's chosen behaviour ("use the same cascading logic from LMA"); the earlier "only bump a date that sits before the one above" version is gone. LMA keeps its own arrival handler plus `cascadeLmaFloor` over `arr1, dep1, arr2, dep2, …`, reached from a hand-typed departure date via the `date-cascade-dep` branch. Dates are `type="hidden"`, so none of this touches the Calculate gate.
  - **Direct US auto:** on 2-sector layover Fetch, tick Direct US if **either** airport is US (`isUsAirport`). Crew can untick. Direct US UI is still layover + 2-sector only.
  - **LMA Fetch:** on the LMA calculator **and** COP, inbound and outbound each have flight + date + Fetch; times/IATA stay editable. COP also still fills LMA from IFA Fetch.
  - **Shuttle:** a **toggle switch row**, rendered **only on the second layover station** (`idx === 2` in `addLmaSector`, so a 1-station card has none and stations 1 and 3 have no checkbox at all). Left cluster is `repeat` + bold **Shuttle** + muted `1-stop connection — *no LMA*`; the switch is on the right, row is `justify-between`. It sits **last in the station, below Departure Time** (v1.8.0). Default off, hidden with the LMA wrap on Turnaround. On → that station’s LMA is **$0** (no B/L/D); IFA unchanged. Not IFA flight type Turnaround. Fetch may pre-tick when same local YMD or ground &lt; 6h; crew can untick. Shuttle stations are skipped in Calculate validation. Do **not** hardcode flight numbers.
  - **Flight Type / Sector Count are pill selectors** (v1.9.0, replacing the glass-overlay dropdowns; `selectDropdown` and every `-btn` / `-menu` / `-icon` element are gone). Each group is `#{id}-{type}-pills` with `.calc-pill[data-value]` buttons; the selected pill gets `bg-sia-gold text-black`. **Layover** and **2 Sectors** are selected in the initial markup, matching the hidden inputs. A pill writes the still-present hidden input (`#{id}-flight-type` = `Layover` | `Turnaround`, `#{id}-sector-count` = `2` | `4`) and dispatches `change`, so **either pill still resets the card** (v1.8.0): `renderIfaSectors` rebuilds the IFA sectors and `resetLmaFields` (assigned by the LMA block, called through `onIfaDropdownChange`) clears the stations back to one empty one. Read the value from the hidden input — nothing downstream changed.
  - Summary overlay: `formatMoney` (`$1,457.38`). IFA sectors First/Second/Third/Fourth Sector, with ` (Paxing)` when that sector is paxing. LMA day rows: `formatLmaDay` (`DD MMM YY`), three fixed-width B/L/D badges (the inlined lucide `x` when missing), `$` per day, **no station total**; then Breakfast/Lunch/Dinner totals with counts (`BREAKFAST (3x)`).
  - IFA hours display is `XH YM`, never a decimal like `10.17`.
- **Step 5:** Inflight menu viewer. Chat flight verification → `api/sq.js` → overlay.
  - Dropdowns: one row; menus overlay content (do **not** restore `overflow-x: auto` on `#menu-dropdown-row` — it clips overlay dropdowns).
  - Category control: single segmented bar (Meals / Drinks / Snacks / Amenities), no wrapping pills.
  - `menuViewState` remembers category and meal tab across cabin / sector / cuisine changes; those changes and meal-service tabs scroll the overlay to top.
  - Centered meal-service title under the route hero. Meal-service tabs centered.
  - Meal photos: `imagePathIfeHigh` if SQ linked it, else construct IFE URLs from dish `id` (and from a same-name dish’s id if this row has none). Linked/working URLs and ids merge into session `imageByName` / `imageIdByName` across legs, cabins, and later fetches — **not** reset per payload. Name keys also match singular/plural and `and`-splits (**no dish-name hardcoding**). Missing CDN = HTTP 200 HTML → `onerror` then **gold bullet**. No placeholders, no `/assets` prefix, no iOS `data-src` / `revealMenuImages`. Light Bites under Delectables **keep photos**. Other snacks / snack drinks stay bullets. Amenities bullets (never photos).
  - Each course is its own card. Course labels between thin gold hairlines; 2+ dishes → italic `Choose one of N` (never `(CHOOSE N)`).
  - Tap a thumbnail for `#menu-lightbox`. More/Less if copy > 2 lines (animate); imaged items stay horizontal.
- **Chat jump-to-latest:** `#btn-scroll-bottom`, same size as send, just above chips; only when not at bottom. **Hide while typing.** Do not use `opacity: 1 !important` under `html.chat-busy` (it fights the hide).
- **Flight lookup:** Card title Inflight Menu (plane icon). Last 5 successful fetches as chips under the flight field (`crewAssist.recentFlights`; horizontal scroll + `.mask-edge` like chat chips). Overlay `currentMenuSearch.selectedSector` is kept when switching cabin (`renderMenu` re-picks that leg). Fetch menu CTA: **book-open** for viewer, **printer** for print. After Fetch: typing → “Fetching menu from seat pocket.” → typing → overlay → “Here is the menu.” After a date: sector pills (if multi) + cabin pills; CTA grey until ≥1 sector (if shown) and ≥1 cabin. Date tap fetches immediately (500ms debounce is only for typing the flight number). `executeFetchCabins` shows `#fv-loading` then `Promise.all`s `getcabin` **and** a speculative JCL `menu` before painting pills. FCL label is Suites vs First from aircraft type (`380`/`388`).
- **Chat motion:** `.chat-anim-in` (320ms) / `animateChatRemove`. Typing before every bot line, calculator, and lookup. Welcome is two bubbles (`{greeting}, {rank} {name}!` then `How can I help you today?`). Send, chips, and in-chat buttons locked (`html.chat-busy`) until the current bubble + gap finish. **Fast lane (v1.28.4, owner ruling — universal, NOT returning-gated):** in normal operation the queue speaks briskly — typing dots 150–250ms and a **250ms** gap (`CHAT_FAST_GAP_MS`) between bubbles, animation 320ms unchanged; the greeting + next-flight card land in ~1.5–2s instead of ~4.5s. **The feature tour is exempt:** while `ca-tour-on` is set, `caTourPace()` keeps the classic 550–900ms dots and 500ms `CHAT_GAP_MS` — the tour's hand-tuned pacing is locked and must not speed up. The pace is decided per-slot/per-bubble at render time, so a straggler bubble that renders while the tour is on also goes classic. **Calculator Enter-advance (v1.29.0):** inside a `[data-calc-card]`, Enter on any `ui-input` text field focuses (and selects) the next one — a delegated document keydown, so it survives card rebuilds; the last field is a no-op. Every numeric calculator field already carries `inputmode` (station-code fields are letters by design). Enter-to-send in the CHAT input is rain-checked by the owner — do not add it.
- **Onboarding gender:** Fixed. `#toggle-autoscroll` was removed; do not re-read it in `initUI()`.
- **CTAs:** Gold-bordered pills. Calculate stays disabled until required fields are filled. Native `appAlert` / `appConfirm` — **never** `alert()` / `confirm()`. Send icon centered.
- **Step 6 inflight menu printer:** Same glass overlay. View ↔ print (`#btn-menu-to-print` / `#print-btn-view`) reuses `currentMenuSearch`; close the other overlay with `immediate === true`. Overlay print control must have a **visible circle** like `#print-btn-view`. Printer loading: “Preparing the menu” (menu/chat still “seat pocket”).
  - Sector tabs: circled number + `LAX → NRT`; selected = gold **outline**.
  - Overlay `overflow-hidden`; scroll `#print-paper-scroll`. Sheet + Edit scroll independently; both replay `.menu-anim-in`. Changing sector / layout / paper jumps preview to top.
  - Preview = real A4/A6 **mm** page scaled to overlay width, centered. **No A3** unless explicitly asked. A−/A+ change `--print-type` only (not the page box); **disable** at 70% and 140%.
  - Elegant auto-selects A4, Compact auto-selects A6; size chips stay toggleable. Zoom and hide flags are **independent per layout** (`hiddenElegant` / `hiddenCompact`).
  - Elegant pills, left to right: **Bounds**, **Descriptions** (on by default), **Greyscale** rightmost. Compact **hides** Greyscale + Descriptions. Bounds = live A4/A6 page-height guide; **never** in PNG/JPEG/Word even if selected.
  - Hidden-by-default: snacks except Light Bites under Delectables; drinks except Champagne / red / white — **not fortified**. Compact also hides From The Bakery and Hot Beverage. Compact SCL hides Chocolate and Cheese and Crackers; compact YCL hides Cheese and Crackers. Still available in Edit → Show. Hidden rows grey; the Show control stays fully usable. Edit: cabin classes are **accordions, collapsed by default, animated** (`.print-edit-fold`).
  - Compact sheet: per-cabin/service header same size as body, all type black: `SQ478 (→JNB) JCL (SUPPER) 160926` — date **after** the service. **No line under the header. No rule above the first meal.** Full-width `.pc-svc-rule` **after each service including the last**. Gap from last text row to that rule = gap between text rows. 100% type = former 80% (A4 `6.8px`, A6 `6px`). First item after `APP:` colon, later items hang under that circled number; Light Bites comma-separated run-on wrap; wine groups not all labelled CHAMPAGNE; meat/seafood proteins black fill + white text on the **same baseline** as the row; invert box must **not overrun below** the letters.
  - Compact PNG/JPEG: **custom 300 dpi canvas** (`exportCompactImage`). Do **not** send compact through html2canvas (failed through 1.4.19–1.4.21: grid circles, `text-transform`, inline `.pc-hl`, nested invert, sibling header rules, baked `<img>`s). Elegant html2canvas scale 3 / JPEG 0.95 is OK.
  - Print footer: `Menu content © Singapore Airlines · Compiled with CrewAssist — an unofficial crew tool`. Caps only: masthead, cabin band, route block, beverage group heads. Elegant type = weight / italic / navy — not extra sheet typefaces. No photos/icons/veg leaf/timeline through rings.

## Subsystem decisions and constraints

These have already caused regressions. Treat them as locks.

### SQ inflight menu (vital)

- Reverse-engineered map: `https://inflightmenu.singaporeair.com` → unauthenticated `POST https://cifp.auto.prod.c0.singaporeair.com/api/{getcabin|menu}`. No key; `sessionId` is local. CORS: preflight `*`, POST allowlist is the **official origin only**.
- **JSON must stay on `/api/sq`.** Do **not** call cifp from the browser. Direct `cifp…/api/getcabin` from this sandbox: TLS EOF — do not retry expecting JSON. Keep `/api/sq`. `api/getcabin.ts`, `api/menu.ts`, `api/cabins.ts` were removed; do not re-add as a replacement for `/api/sq`. Proxy timeout is **12s**.
- **Images are `<img src>`** to `https://inflightmenu.singaporeair.com/…` (not CORS-gated). Join origin + relative path with `encodeURI` (spaces in amenity paths). **Never** prefix SQ relative images with `/assets/` (404s).
- Dish photos: `imagePathIfeHigh` on ~9% of items (relative `fabs/IFE/INFM/<cabin>/HIGH|LOW/<id>_<cabin>.png`); otherwise construct from item `id` (`DH021259-v3-FCL` → strip `-vN` → `DH021259_FCL.png`; `DH026749-001-SCL` **keeps** `-001`). Missing CDN objects return **HTTP 200 + `index.html`** (~4664 B, `text/html`) — **never trust status**; `onerror` / decode fail is the miss. Then gold bullet. **No placeholder images.** Skip `API_REFERENCE.md` cascade **step 4** (app placeholder). Amenities stay bullets even if `imagePath` exists.
- Session `imageByName` / `imageIdByName` + same-name id reuse across sectors/cabins/fetches. **Do not hardcode dish names.** Do not use iOS `data-src` / `revealMenuImages` / forced delay / parent key-scan for junk `url`/`path`/`src`.
- Light Bites under Delectables **keep images**. Meal **courses** keep photos. Other Delectables muffins/snacks = gold bullets.

### Calculator / rates

**Refetch gate (v1.19.0, owner).** Where the Fetch button exists (Default and Advanced - Manual hides it), editing a flight number (`.ifa-fn-input` / `.lma-fn-input`) or a date (`.ca-date-input`) puts that sector in `staleSectors` (keys `ifa:N` / `lma:N`, declared at the top of `bindCalculatorEvents`) and `validateInputs` greys Calculate while the set is non-empty. A successful fetch clears the key (`ifaFetchOneSector`; for LMA only once `lmaStationDone(idx)`, i.e. both legs). Typing a time by hand (`.ifa-time-input` / `.lma-time-input`) also clears it, so a failed fetch is never a dead end - Default hides the time field, so there only a refetch will do. Sectors that no longer exist and LMA stations with Shuttle ticked are pruned from the set during validation. **Re-run the gate after a fetch** - `validateInputs()` is called at the end of `ifaFetchOneSector` and in `lmaFetchOneLeg`'s `finally`. It has to be: the write-back fires `input` while the sector is *still* marked stale, so that pass greys Calculate, and clearing the key afterwards re-validates nothing. v1.19.0 shipped without it and Calculate stayed grey after a successful fetch (it only looked fine in tests whose fetch stub resolved instantly, before the `click`-scheduled `setTimeout(validateInputs, 100)` - always give the stub a real delay). The `ifaApplyingFetch` / `lmaApplyingFetch` guards stop a fetch write-back from reading as a crew edit; note `syncLmaFromIfa` fires `input` on LMA *times* (dates use `setVal(..., false)`), which deliberately clears LMA staleness on a COP card because those times came from the fresh IFA fetch.

- `LOGIC.md` wins on **behaviour**. Live `rates.json` / `crewAssist.rates` wins on **coefficients**. Formulas stay in LOGIC + `executeCalculation`; numbers are not the source of truth in JS once `rates.json` has loaded.
- Critical asymmetry: **layover** multiplier from **per-sector SDP**; **turnaround** multiplier from **summed** SDP, applied to every non-paxing sector. Paxing (0.75×, SDP ignored) beats Direct US (3.5×) if both were true. Direct US UI is layover + 2-sector only. LMA is **hidden / zero** on turnaround. **Shuttle** is a per-station LMA skip ($0 that station only), not Turnaround; do not hardcode flight numbers (`LOGIC.md` §4.3a).
- Rank pairs are the same rate (Jr. FS = Jr. FSS, etc.).
- Bracket editor: one editable cutoff per boundary; the next band’s “> X” is a live echo, not a second field. Last band has no cutoff (`maxHours: null`).
- Save = device only. Reset = shipped file. Export/Import JSON. **No GitHub write from the PWA. No one-tap publish. No non-dev crew editing.**
- Service worker: network-first for app shell **and** `rates.json`. If rates miss and there is no cache, return **503 JSON** (`{}`), **not** `index.html` (that would parse as HTML and poison the engine).
- **Never use `toISOString()` for calendar dates** without compensating timezone (`LOGIC.md` §6.7). Prefer `new Date(y, m-1, d)` + local getters, or `YYYY-MM-DDT00:00:00`.
- Display money with `formatMoney` (thousands separators). Internal math unrounded; round at display only.
- IFA duration display: `XH YM`, never decimal hours.

### Printer (locks through 1.4.24)

- Compact export = `exportCompactImage` 300 dpi canvas. **Never html2canvas for compact** (invert/header failed 1.4.19–1.4.21). Do not restore CSS `text-transform`, inline/table invert backgrounds, sibling header rules, or baked invert `<img>`s for compact export.
- Insert needle for export work was `async function exportPrintImage(type)` — do not miss it.
- Bounds never in export. No A3. Print zoom must not change the A4/A6 page box.
- View↔print: `currentMenuCabins()` / `currentMenuSearch`; `closeMenuPrinter(true)` / `closeMenuViewer(true)`.
- Compact wine hide keys order: fortif / white / red before champagne. SCL compact hide + YCL cheese/crackers: `printHideCompactScl`.

## Feature history

Dated, round-by-round record. Archaeology — consult it when the question is
"why is this the way it is?"

### Earnings archive (v1.19.13 redesign; earnings page v1.24.0; save-dedupe v1.28.1; corrections + broad search + save honesty v1.29.0)

**v1.24.0 (owner spec, round 14 — the Earnings page redesign):** the Earnings overlay is rebuilt as a scrolling earnings page (iOS-style sheet with a 44×4px handle) on the same `#ca-arch-*` ids and the same entry schema. (1) **Gold summary card** with a **Month / Year / All-time segmented control** (`.ca-arch-seg`, active `.ca-arch-segon`): total, flight count and average-per-flight follow the scope; the context line reads "On track for $X · $Y left" (target icon) on the partial current month, "+/−X% vs LY" (trending-up/down) for the year scope (vs the *whole* prior year) and month scopes with a prior-year record, and Context (current-month YoY + proj.) on All-time — averages over complete months only. (2) **Twelve-month bar chart** (`.ca-arch-barcol`, oldest left; the window ends at the later of the newest entry month and the current real month): actual totals as gold bars scaled to the highest actual month on a 120px track, the current partial month stacking a hatched **projection segment** (`.ca-arch-barproj`, total/days-elapsed × month-length, only when partial and > $0.01), empty months as 2px stubs, a `Peak: $X.Xk` label, a tooltip reading `Sep 2026 · $500.00 → $625.00`, and tapping a bar selecting it (`.ca-arch-baron`) and expanding + scrolling to that month group. (3) **Insights panel** behind a View insights toggle (label flips to Hide insights, `aria-expanded`, chevron rotates): exactly twelve `.ca-arch-inscard` cards — Top/Lowest earning month (complete months only), Monthly average, Busiest month, Current streak (months ≥ average), Longest flight streak, Longest break, Highest single flight, Per flying day, Nights away YTD (station days−1, fallback one per layover), Flying days this year, Busiest weekday — no annual-goal / YTD-allowances / long-haul leftovers. (4) **Month list**: newest-first groups with **Best / Low / In progress badges** (Lucide trophy / trending-down), a YoY arrow (`.ca-arch-yoy-up/-down`) on complete months with a prior-year same-month record, a hollow-dot **projected row** inside the expanded current month ("Projected · N flights", gold italic total), and an End-of-records footer ("End of records" + "Showing earnings since {Month YYYY}"). **Search narrows the list only** — summary, chart and insights always read the whole archive for their scope, a filtered month head reads "$X of $Y", and **trash deletes exactly what the search leaves visible** (the list is never scope-narrowed). Theme: `--arch-*` variables in `:root` (dark) and `html:not(.dark)` (light), every `:hover` behind `@media (hover:hover)`, Lucide icons only (never Font Awesome). An empty archive keeps the gold card and the exact empty-state wording but hides the trend card and the insights toggle. Import/export, storage schema and the reopen sub-sheet are untouched; the old `#ca-arch-bar` scope pill and `.ca-arch-pill` CSS are gone. Suite `tests/earnings-archive.test.js` 32→**118 asserts** (seeded two-year archive pinning every figure; date-derived projections computed from the run date).

Dedicated overlay (`#ca-arch-*`), not the shared results sheet: pinned gold summary bar (scope pill cycles ALL-TIME -> year -> month of the newest entry; count + total follow), search (route text, stacks with scope), collapsible month groups **newest first** (this supersedes the older oldest-first reading order for the Earnings page), 4-part rows (route / date / amount / grey x), 6s Undo toast for delete/import/clear-all, header import (JSON only, validate-then-preview, merge-only, dup skip) + export (JSON/CSV, share-or-download, `crewassist-earnings-<scope>.<ext>`) + gated trash (respects filters). Storage schema unchanged: `{id, savedAt, monthKey, sectorDate, flightType, stationDisplay, amount}`. v1.19.14 polish: search icon + gold focus + clear(refocus); collapsed month headers show a muted count; a filtered month's subtotal reads "$X of $Y" (never understates); rows render-sorted flight-date desc with insertion-order ties (storage stays append-only); list scroll position is preserved across re-renders; headers are buttons with `aria-expanded`, toast is `role=status`; icons limited to search/chevron-down/x. v1.19.15: rows show first station code only and are indented under the month; month subtotals white; months default-collapsed except the newest; delete toast "Deleted <code>"; clear-all reads "Delete N entries? / This can be undone."; dev Save/Reset/Export/Import are icon buttons; COP save shows a settings-style "<route> earnings saved." pop-up. v1.19.16: archRoute drops only the SIN segment (keeps JNB/CPT); row amounts white, month subtotals gold; dev rates split footer (full-width gold Save + 3 labelled secondaries save/rotate-ccw/file-down/file-up) with snapshot-based dirty state (disabled dim Save + UNSAVED EDITS dot/label + per-row EDITED tags + gold left border), saved flash (#1D2B1F/#6EE7A0) + toast, reset-to-defaults marks dirty, export warns when dirty, import fills+dirty never saves; unsaved edits kept in-session, dropped on restart. v1.19.17: archive expands the real current calendar month on open (future months, e.g. an early-saved October, start collapsed — previously the newest month won); each dev rates section header carries a gold dot while any of its rows is edited. v1.19.18: all exports (dev rates, earnings JSON/CSV, print images, Word) route through `exportFile(blob, filename)` — iPhone/iPad use the Web Share sheet (Save to Files; AbortError = cancel, no toast), PC/Android fall back to an anchor download into Downloads; dev rates import picker `accept` now `.json,application/json` matching the archive import; archive export no longer triggers the share sheet on desktop. v1.19.19: section dot sits immediately after the title (wrapper flex child, margin-left 6px, chevron stays far right); export share gate is now ANY mobile UA (`exportIsMobile`, incl. Android) — PC/Mac always direct-download; archive delete uses the settings-style `appConfirm` pop-up instead of the sub-sheet, undo toast unchanged. v1.19.20: exportFile retries the share as text/plain when canShare rejects the real MIME (Android whitelist rejects application/json — CSV shared, JSON silently downloaded); dev footer secondaries are now two equal one-line buttons Export(download)/Import(upload) matching the Save button anatomy; Reset moved out of the rates card into Data Management as a dev-gated gold pill (`#btn-dev-reset`, hidden unless dev mode, 280ms reveal animation) above the restyled transparent red-bordered Clear All Data pill. v1.19.21: Android gates Web Share by filename EXTENSION (not MIME) and rejects .json - the v1.19.20 text/plain retry kept the .json name so it still failed; JSON exports now retry as a .txt twin (same bytes, type text/plain), scoped to application/json only (Word keeps .doc and downloads); exportFile resolves the delivered filename and toasts it; both import pickers accept `.json,.txt,application/json,text/plain` so Android .txt backups round-trip (import validators read content, not extension). v1.19.22: canShare proved unreliable on the owner's Android (refused even valid .txt), so exportFile now only uses canShare to PRE-SELECT a form (real name, else .txt twin for JSON) and attempts navigator.share directly when canShare refuses everything - a genuine rejection falls back to the download, AbortError (user cancel) delivers nothing; IFA/LMA trip bar sector boxes turn emerald green when their sector/station is fetched (data-ifa-state=done / arr+dep done), updated in place with transition-colors so the flip animates.

**Round 12b (owner data):** the owner completed 2026 — Jan/Feb/Mar/Apr + Nov PDFs added and September 2026 replaced (commits `b918cd1`/`3f00f9f`/`314922a`; `_inbox/` now holds 35 rosters, 2026 Jan–Nov). The real-roster suite flagged exactly the five new files, all honest: Jan/Apr/Nov 2026 begin mid-trip (completing sectors in Dec 2025 / Mar / Oct), Mar 2026 ends mid-trip (completes in Apr), and Feb 2026's CHC-SIN return is a **TVL row** (verified raw: `TVL@211`, PN pickup, duty/FDP totals but no Flight Time cell — the crew flies out to Christchurch, travels home as passengers). All five recorded in EXPECTED_FLAGS; the consecutive-month stitch invariants verify every new boundary trip resolves. September 2026's update parses fully clean. Suite now 317 asserts in roster-real (574 total, 8/8). No app change — no version bump.

**v1.23.12 (owner data, round 12 — the 2024/2025 rosters):** the owner uploaded every month of 2024 and 2025 to `_inbox/` via github.com (commit `a11fb45`, 24 PDFs, 30 total). Running them through the REAL pipeline (pdfjs-dist text extraction → the booted app's `rosterParse`) exposed a genuine parser bug: **long-haul duties are echoed across three day-rows** — op row (STD+FT, no STA), an EMPTY echo row (running totals only), then the STA row up to two days later (seen on Jun 2025 SQ33 SFO-SIN and Oct 2024 SQ37 LAX-SIN). The empty row used to spawn a phantom sector AND steal the echo-merge key, splitting one real sector into two broken ones. Fix: an all-empty fn+sector row is now skipped (`if (!r.vals.std && !r.vals.sta && !r.vals.ft) return;` in the flights loop). Remaining flags across all 30 months are honest: month-boundary trips (complete when consecutive files are stitched — verified, incl. the Feb+Mar+Apr 2025 chain where both boundary trips resolve) and TVL positioning sectors, for which the roster prints NO flight time (deriving it from STD/STA would be wrong across time zones — LHR-SIN naive elapsed 20:12 vs real ~12:55 — so none is invented). **New suite `tests/roster-real.test.js`** (268 asserts): every `_inbox` PDF parses, month label matches filename, no phantom sectors, flagged trips match the recorded EXPECTED_FLAGS map (new uploads default to fully-clean — a failure means a new pattern to fix or record), consecutive-month stitching resolves inner boundaries, and the Feb+Mar+Apr 2025 deep case pins the SFO/LHR round trips complete. Synthetic regression for the three-row echo added to roster-pdf.test.js (132 asserts). pdfjs-dist 6.3.289 added as a tests devDependency.

**v1.23.11 (owner feedback, round 11):** timing — summary glide **40 hops × 150ms = 6.0s**, bottom pause 1000→**500** (2.18), post-archive-confirm hold 1800→**1500** (2.22; note: 2.21's pre-OK dialog hold stays 2000). **Dead-code sweep:** scanned all 1,372 declared identifiers across index.html/sw.js/tests — found and removed `caTourSaveBeat` (save-beat helper dead since the v1.23.5/6 rewrites) and `caTourKtmTrip` (superseded by `caTourParsedDemo()`), both brace-match-extracted with boundary asserts. `bindArchUi` flagged by the scanner is a **named IIFE** (`(function bindArchUi(){…})()`) that self-invokes to bind the archive UI — alive, kept. No unused files: api/sq.js backs the `/api/sq` endpoint (4 fetch sites + harness), rates.json is fetched + precached, all six icons referenced.

**v1.23.0:** the feature tour — five demonstrative steps in the owner's order: chat basics → roster showpiece (paperclip glow, a synthetic demo month through the REAL pipeline, Calculate all, the save beat, the earnings archive with tap-through) → Default calculating (chip, card, SQ 11 + a date two days out, Fetch all; offline pivots honestly — never a made-up number) → Manual calculating (Settings → the real segmented pill, the KTM layover typed by hand through the same event path hand-typing uses, $360.72 at default rates, save beat, mode restored) → menu + print, the one interactive step (the crew's typing IS the demo; a watcher glows and narrates each stage — flight field, Fetch menu, printer icon, print sheet — and pivots on the offline error). Entry points: a one-time first-run offer after the greeting (`crewAssist.tourOffered`, buttons Show me around / Not now) and the `tour` chat intent (or a bare `help`) forever; `crewAssist.tourDone` ends the offers. Skippable at every step (Next / Skip tour buttons in the bubble, Build/Discard anatomy); typing mid-tour yields the floor, the question is answered, then Resume is offered. A gold ring glow (`.ca-tour-glow`, eased 1.4s pulse) marks whatever the narration names — scroll-then-glow per the project scroll rules. Data isolation: demo saves land in `window.caTourArchiveStore` (`loadArchive`/`persistArchive` redirect to it while the tour runs), so the earnings page shows the demo month during the tour and the crew's real archive is never touched; the calculator mode is restored with persistence; every bubble the tour adds is marked `.ca-tour-bubble` and animates away at the end (the crew's own bubbles never are), while the closing line and the skip farewell outlive the cleanup. The tour is DATA, not code — a steps registry (`CA_TOUR_STEPS`) where each step is self-contained (`id`, `say`, `run`, `expectsInput`); a step whose target has vanished from the DOM skips itself (try/catch + console.warn), so future feature changes degrade instead of breaking; adding a feature to the tour = adding one step object and its test block. The demo month reuses the parser test-suite geometry (KTM layover with the split overnight + echo, HKT turnaround, PEN 4-sector shuttle — $823.23 at default rates, plus the ATDO day reported as skipped). Suite `tests/tour.test.js` (57 assertions) walks all five steps end-to-end offline, plus the offer/skip flow, interruption + resume, and cleanup isolation.
