**v1.45.46 — the visible-fit release (owner report 2026-10-29: “on my iphone 11 its still showing up to 10 entries in records page” — and the report was right, again):** the v1.45.45 answer “the ~10 you see IS your phone's fit” was TRUE of the design and FALSE of the first open — a real bug the jsdom battery was structurally blind to (its fallback masked it). Proven before editing, in a real-Chromium viewport sweep over a 14-station seed: pre-fix, EVERY viewport class — 375×667 through 430×932 — rendered exactly **7 fitted rows / 10 entries**, the jsdom-fallback value, on every first open. Root cause: `openRcPod()` called `renderRcPod(true)` BEFORE `wrap.classList.remove('hidden')` — under `display:none` the body's `clientHeight` reads 0, so `rcPodFit` returned its no-layout fallback 7 on EVERY first open in a real browser (only tab swaps, door folds and resizes — which run while the wrap is visible — ever measured truly). The fix is a two-line reorder: un-hide, then render, in the same synchronous task (no paint between, no flash). Post-fix, the sweep reads **8 entries at 375×667; 9 at 414×728 (the iPhone 11 in Safari); 13 at 414×896 (installed full-screen/PWA); 14 at 430×932** — the v1.45.39 design (fit the viewport, never a fixed number) is now actually ENFORCED on open, and the jsdom fallback 7 is untouched so every existing jsdom pin holds. APP_VERSION 1.45.46, CACHE_NAME v277, one generic fix pointer (“The records list fills the height your screen gives it.”); guard sweep **369 items, 0 violations**. Tests: roster-calendar 473→**475** (the order pin — inside `openRcPod`, the un-hide must precede the render, plus the in-source trap comment pinned so the next editor reads it); dev-ui 770→**771** (E6 ladder [0]=1.45.46 with its single pointer, 1.45.45→[1], the 1.45.23 window pins retire keeping src — floor **1.45.24**); smoke 294→**295** (the dynamic-fit pin at the run's tail: seed 14 stations, open records at 390×844 → 9 rows, close, re-open at 390×620 → 4 rows — the SHORTER window must fit FEWER, which the flat-7 regression cannot satisfy; the rig's first mid-run viewport change, waited past the 150ms resize debounce); next-flight held at 200 after a **rig fix the battery caught on the month crossing**: the split-duty fixture's date-anchor builder stripped the day's zero pad, so a run whose day-after-tomorrow lands on the 1st–9th minted an unpadded token the parser — correctly, every real roster PDF zero-pads — rejects, and the STD column lost its anchor (194/6, all six from the one token; 200/0 after padding, same total). Battery 14/14 = **3,047/0**, smoke **295/295** — green after one flake (the calendar tag-toggle gold fill, the recorded under-load class; clean on rerun). app.html rebuilt (1,284,691 bytes). Owner reviews from phone after deploy — expect ~9 entries in Safari, ~13 installed.

**v1.45.48 — the icon-arrows release (owner order 2026-10-30, from the glyph-inventory question):** the round began as an audit — *where does the app use Unicode, and can Lucide replace it?* The sweep classified every non-ASCII character in the file: the app already runs Lucide everywhere an icon belongs (185 `data-lucide` sites, ~60 distinct names), so the residue fell into five classes — (1) **typography that stays text by ruling**: the × count operator (the v1.45.40 muted-operator ruling), route arrows → inside sentences, · separators, the − money minus, prose punctuation; (2) **real content**: the menu data's accented characters (Ẃ STEAMED RICE — actual catering text); (3) **the painter**, where Lucide cannot go (the ◆ diamond is drawn as text onto the capture canvas); (4) **deliberately not Lucide**: the v1.45.47 row chevron (CSS glyph — no icon pass, renders CDN-blocked) and the zoom buttons' A−/A+ labels; (5) **the one genuine candidate**: the calendar month-nav buttons, whose ENTIRE visual was a bare ‹/› text character. Owner said PROCEED. Shipped: both buttons now carry `<i data-lucide="chevron-left/right" class="w-5 h-5">` in the family idiom (matching the 8 chevron-downs, 2 chevron-ups, 4 chevron-rights already running; chevron-left verified present in the lucide 1.64.0 package before wiring — npm pack + tar listing), the buttons' text-lg font-bold sizing classes dropped (the icon owns the box now), a v1.45.48 in-source comment records the rationale, and the conversion is HAZARD-FREE by construction: static markup, rendered once at boot, the boot-time icon pass covers them forever — no re-render path ever rebuilds them, so the v1.45.42 crown-loss class cannot occur. **v1.45.58 — the year-filter drawer fix (owner report 2026-11-01: “there seem to be a little bug in the flight stats page, when i toggle to a certain year e.g. 2024, i can still see other years record in there”):** the visit-log drawer ignored the year filter. The podium list itself filtered correctly (`rcPodStats(rcPodListYear)` in `renderRcPod`), but the FLIGHT HISTORY a station card opens re-walked the store with a BARE `rcPodStats()` — all-time — so under any year chip the drawer listed every visit the station ever had (a KUL tapped under the 2024 chip showed “3 VISITS · 5 May 2025 · 10 Mar 2024 · 2 Nov 2023”). One line: `rcPodToggle` now walks `rcPodStats(rcPodListYear)` — the drawer rides the SAME year the list is showing (both the click and the Enter/Space keyboard opens go through it; a re-render's keep-open path already read the filtered list; the all-time chip is the unfiltered walk, unchanged). The diagnosis path is the story: the list filtered fine in both rigs, so the bug was found by reading the handler call sites — `rcPodToggle`'s entry lookup was the only bare `rcPodStats()` in the file. APP_VERSION 1.45.58, CACHE_NAME v289, one generic fix pointer (“The flight stats page keeps a station’s history inside the year you chose.” — 13 words; the apostrophe rides the JS unicode escape in source, and the dev-ui pointer pin must quote that escape with its backslash doubled or JS resolves the character at parse time and the pin dies on the literal source bytes — the ladder's own micro-trap this round). Guard sweep on the diff: **0 violations** (the KUL/SIN hits are test seeds, the suite's own idiom). Tests: roster-calendar 561→**566** (the v1.45.58 block on a three-year KUL seed — all-time holds all three visits, the 2024 chip's drawer holds only the 2024 visit with no 2023/2025 leak, the keyboard open rides the same filtered walk; the src pin on the `rcPodStats(rcPodListYear)` form inside rcPodToggle); dev-ui 797→**799** (ladder [0]=1.45.58 with its single fix pointer, 1.45.57→[1], floor 1.45.41→**1.45.42**; the version-anchored shift's SECOND trap: the head insert created [1]=1.45.57 while the shift loop still had a v=57 case — the loop matched the freshly-written rung and crossed the ladder; the fix is to shift ONLY the old rungs, 56→42, so the head's own [1] is final — the version-vs-index walk passed); smoke 317→**321** (version pins; the drawer pin pair in real Chromium — a seeded 2025 KUL visit beside the 2026 one: all-time holds both years, the 2026 chip's history holds only 2026 — with a full state restore so the pins downstream still see the section's four-leg landscape). **The midnight class struck at ship time (2026-11-01 SGT): the date-anchored blocks that boot UNPINNED broke when the box crossed into November** — roster-calendar's real-October mega-block (the calendar opened November, the 2 Oct cell fell out of the grid), its Jun-2025 PDF block, its week-wrap synthetic block, and its arrow-keys block: all four now pin `now:` per the date-stability rule. The smoke rig runs the real clock with no pin to lean on, so it gained the browser equivalent: `evaluateOnNewDocument` freezes the page Date at the seeded 2026-10-26T12:00+08 — the calendar section is October-anchored with an unflown 14 Nov leg, which also defuses that leg's date bomb for good. **v1.45.57 — the whole-card roll hotfix (owner order 2026-10-31, same-day follow-up to v1.45.56): “for earnings to also have the digits on the other figures within the gold card below Earnings total plus its Gross|CPF and after-expenses line to be animated departure board roll as well.”** The roll now covers the ENTIRE earnings gold card, not just its headline: the metric quadrant row below the divider — FLIGHTS, AVG/FLIGHT and the context cell on the COP lens; BASIC, FIXED, AFTER-HOURS, COP on the Salary lens — rolls with the same `caNumRoll` departure-board treatment, on the same triggers (fresh open + every figure-changing tap). The mechanism is ONE decision at the top: `renderArchSummary` builds a **whole-card fingerprint** — `figs = [numEl]` plus the visible Gross|CPF and after-expenses lines plus every `.ca-arch-metagrid:not(.hidden) .ca-arch-metaval` — and when `renderArchSummary._open || renderArchSummary._fv !== fv`, the whole family rolls together (`figs.forEach(caNumRoll)`). The fingerprint (not the total alone) owns the decision, so a change the big number wouldn't catch — a same-total COP count change, a context-cell projection shift — still rolls the card; and the `:not(.hidden)` walk keeps the hidden-mode quadrant out (switching lens never rolls the quadrant that just left). Details that keep it honest: `caNumRoll` no-ops digit-free values (`—` stays), the labels are separate `.ca-arch-metalabel` divs so they never roll, the context cell's innerHTML (trend icons, projection strings) round-trips through the roll's current-capture restore, and the YoY badge beside the total stays excluded (it has its own reveal). APP_VERSION 1.45.57, CACHE_NAME v288 (the update-delivery mechanism — the hotfix ships as its own version, not an amended 1.45.56), one generic imp pointer (“Every figure on the gold earnings card rolls now.” — 8 words). Guard sweep on the diff: **0 violations** (airport codes + flight-number pattern). Tests: earnings-archive 545→**549** (the hotfix block — fresh-open quadrant pins: FLIGHTS and AVG/FLIGHT roll with the big number while the hidden Salary quadrant mounts zero reels; lens-change pins: BASIC and COP roll while the now-hidden COP quadrant never does; the src pin re-pointed to the fingerprint forms — the `:not(.hidden)` walk, the `fv` join, the `_fv !== fv` gate, the forEach); dev-ui 798→**797** (ladder [0]=1.45.57 with its single pointer, 1.45.56→[1] with its two, floor 1.45.40→**1.45.41** with the 1.45.40 rung's index pins retired; the shift was done VERSION-ANCHORED — each rung's `.v`+`.items` pair moved by matching its version string, so a first-occurrence sweep can't cross wires — and the version-vs-index walk passed first try, the ladder trap finally dodged); smoke 317→**319** (version pins; the quadrant mid-roll pin at the fresh open — at least two metric cells rolling AND every digit-bearing one already reading its truth, read before the settle sleep; the quadrant settle pin). **Rig-timing trap (the third payment):** five pins across two rigs read DOM SHAPE mid-roll — the ctx-cell innerHTML reads (the annual-average bare-text read, the month/year trend-icon reads, the bullseye milestone read in smoke) and my own hidden-quadrant proof, which read the OPEN's still-mounted cells ~300ms into the lens click and mistook them for a fresh roll; the fix is the v1.45.56 rule applied consistently — text pins read the truth mid-roll, shape pins wait the ~900ms settle (and the hidden-quadrant proof reads after it). sw/menu-extraction/offline-menus stamps 1.45.57/v288. Recycles #16 (both rigs wiped mid-session — reinstalled) and #17 (.git reverted to 14409f3 again; fetch + mixed reset to a9b5b54, the dirty tree verified as exactly the 8-file changeset) both struck and both recovered. **v1.45.56 — the roll + veg label release (owner order 2026-10-31, green-lit after a five-question ruling round):** two features. **(1) The departure-board roll:** the hero figures on BOTH gold cards — the passport's ten telemetry cells (values and subs alike) and the Earnings card's big `#ca-arch-total` plus its Gross|CPF and after-expenses lines — ROLL their digits: a fresh open (pod open, earnings view) and every figure-changing tap (year pill; COP/Salary lens; Month/Year/All-time scope; month navigation; a saved recalculation or archive edit landing). Digits only — letters, words, punctuation hold still as anchors (`1d 16h`, `KUL ×2`, `2 Feb 2024`, the `388` all roll their digits; `—` and pure-letter routes no-op). ~600ms, digits locking left-to-right (the per-digit stagger capped at 270ms so long money never drags), each digit a reel cell sliding a 30-row strip one full copy past its start with a mild overshoot bezier — the slot-machine landing. The Earnings numflip cross-fade RETIRES (the roll owns the figure change; the roll decision rides the displayed VALUE, not the lens key — a same-month recalc rolls too). The technique that keeps every pin honest: **the base digits stay in the DOM as the reel cells' text (transparent while rolling) and the strips draw their glyphs through ::before content** — the element's textContent NEVER changes, so mid-roll reads, live pins and screen readers always see the truth; the strips are aria-hidden decoration. `ca-reduced` stands down entirely (the text simply stays); background re-renders apply settled (the accordion lesson — a tab swap or the stats overlay mounting over the page never re-rolls); the restore returns the element's own current markup (styled sub-lines survive). **Trap (caught by the smoke rig, not jsdom):** the first draft captured the element's markup at ROLL time and restored THAT — but a second roll on the same element (open → scope → lens) would restore the FIRST capture, reverting the displayed figure to a stale value after the roll settled; real Chromium read `$3,000.00` where `$5,250.00` had landed. The fix: a roll in flight flattens to its truth first and the capture is always the CURRENT markup. **Trap 2 (rig timing, paid twice):** mid-roll reads must be SYNCHRONOUS with the triggering tap — any await can stretch past the 570ms single-digit restore under box load; and DOM-shape reads (children, innerHTML) after a figure change wait out the ~840ms restore — text reads ride the truth mid-roll, shape reads do not (both the jsdom gross-line pins and the smoke v1.45.21 pins needed the settle wait). **(2) Ethnic (Veg):** the menu viewer's culinary dropdown flattened every non-international selection to a bare `Ethnic` — two ethnic menus on one flight were indistinguishable (the owner's report; the raw header carries the distinguishing `(meatless)`). Now a selection whose raw name matches *meatless* or *vegetarian* (case-insensitive) renders as **Ethnic (Veg) — always**, never only when duplicated; a collision guard falls back to the raw headers if two selections would still render identical labels (never two identical entries in one dropdown). Scope: the dropdown only — open list and closed gate label (the print view stays as-is by owner ruling). APP_VERSION 1.45.56, CACHE_NAME v287, two generic imp pointers (“Hero figures roll in like a departure board.” / “The meatless ethnic menu is labelled (Veg).” — 8 words each); guard sweep **386 items, 0 violations** against all 139 airport codes + the flight-number pattern. Tests: roster-calendar 545→**561** (src pins — the helper contract (digits-only guard, ca-reduced stand-down, current-markup restore), the roll-flag render chain, the two roll moments, the hval/hsub walk, the ::before CSS; live pins — mid-roll truth + reel cells + aria-hidden strips + armed transition read SYNCHRONOUSLY with the open, the settle unwrap, the year-pill re-roll, the tab-swap and stats-overlay settle; the two stale literals re-pointed); earnings-archive 539→**545** (fresh-open + lens-change rolls read synchronously; the shape pins wait out the restore; the numflip-absence and value-guard src pins); offline-menus 168→**175** (the veg block live: three-selection dropdown `International|Ethnic|Ethnic (Veg)`, the gate label carrying (Veg), the course switch, the always-rule, the collision guard + the builder src pin); dev-ui 792→**798** (ladder [0]=1.45.56 with its two pointers, 1.45.55→[1], floor 1.45.39→**1.45.40**, the 1.45.39 rung retired to feed its pointers — the ladder trap struck a THIRD time despite the walk rule: the shift loop swept the freshly-inserted [1] rung and crossed 55/54; the version-vs-index walk caught it before the run); the will-change pin caught the strip's `will-change: transform` as a fifth at-rest will-change — dropped (the strips are transient); smoke 311→**317** (version pins; the passport mid-roll pin — ten values, reels mounted, strips aria-hidden, every digit-bearing value reading its truth — read before the settle sleep; the settle pin; the earnings fresh-open + lens-change + settle pins — the settle pin is what caught the stale-capture bug). sw/menu-extraction/offline-menus stamps 1.45.56/v287. the physical-card fixed-ink ruling of v1.45.52 returns for it), with ON-GOLD ESPRESSO inks: values `#1A1300`, labels and icons `rgba(26,19,0,.62)`, subs `rgba(26,19,0,.45)` — the divider is the on-gold hairline `rgba(26,19,0,.15)` (the owner rejected a literal stone-800 line: theme-correct HAIRLINE READ, on gold). **(2) The podium and stats surfaces tint by depth** — `var(--arch-surface)`: navy-night `#141823`, white by day (the earnings pair's tinted twin); the gate carries a DOUBLE static chevron-up flanking the label. **(5) The glide-up fix:** the overlay sheet was not gliding on open — `openRcStat` removed `translate-y-full` itself, racing the glide helper that owns the lift; the helper (`caSheetGlideArm`/`caSheetGlideIn`) is now the SOLE owner (openRcStat arms, never touches the translate class). **(6) The tab pills match the year pills** — padding 5px 13px, the min-height retired (a computed height-parity pin |ΔoffsetHeight| ≤ 1 keeps them level). The open-station gold ring and the fold-out behavior are untouched. **(6) The gate goes SOLID** (owner pick over quiet-outline and bare-link): filled with the theme's ink pole — the toast pair, near-black by day, cream at night, inverted text; `:active` dims to .82. **Trap (recorded in smoke/README):** theme-toggling computed pins must wait out the pills' 150ms background transition — 50ms reads read mid-fade (the visit-log ink pin also needed 650ms waits once three more theme-toggling pins preceded it). **v1.45.53 — the type + overlay release (owner orders 2026-10-30, the day-after follow-ups, two orders):** **(1) The passport's hero typography is the EARNINGS page style** — the owner asked “why is the font like that for passport, use the same style as the earnings page”: `.ca-rc-pod-hval` is now the house sans at 700/24px/-0.025em with `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` (mini cells 14px), labels are the metalabel idiom (11px/700/.03em/uppercase), `.ca-rc-pod-hsub` is 11px sans — Space Mono is retired from the hero values and sub only (the year pills and the rotated tag keep their stamp mono; the ruling scoped the hero). **(2) All Flight Stats opens ANOTHER overlay** — “instead of opening an accordion when user taps on all flight stats to open another overlay instead”: the in-page records section is gone from the pod sheet; the gate (chevron-up, static, aria-label “Open all flight stats”) raises `#ca-rc-statwrap` (fixed, z-[64] above the passport's z-[62]) with its own shade + `#ca-rc-stat-sheet` (glass, translate-y-full glide), title “All Flight Stats”, own subtitle `#ca-rc-stat-subtitle` (the list window), and the inner year pills + tab bar + body now live inside it. The two-chip model stands: the MAIN chip drives the hero and syncs; the overlay's chip drives the list alone. `closeRcStat` mirrors `closeRcPod` helper-for-helper (HideCancel/FocusIn/GlideArm out, FocusOut/GlideCancel/HideArm in — verified against the real openers), `closeRcPod` closes the overlay first, and the global Escape closers list runs the overlay BEFORE the calendar so the top sheet always closes first. The stations' Enter/Space keydown moved to the overlay's own delegated listener (`statWrapEl`). **Trap:** `openRcStat` must call `renderRcPod(true)` — the overlay's podium is a fresh mount and deserves the staggered reveal; without the `true`, the d0–d2 classes vanish and the computed-style pins read mid-transition garbage (the smoke wash/recede pins caught it: light recede read 0.306726 instead of 0.4). Tests re-pointed: roster-calendar's accordion pins became overlay pins (+ earnings-typeface src pin; 535), dev-ui's ladder takes the 1.45.53 head (floor 1.45.37; the 1.45.36 rung retired to feed pointers; glide-arm/cancel counts 11→12; 789), smoke's gateway pins became overlay-semantics pins + a computed typeface pin (no Space Mono, 700, tabular-nums) + the overlay-subtitle pin (308). sw precache v284. **v1.45.52 — the telemetry release (owner spec 2026-10-30, green-lit after three Q&A rounds; 16 rulings recorded):** the passport page gains its telemetry stack. **(1) The MAIN year pills** — a horizontally scrollable row (`flex overflow-x-auto`, the scrollbar hidden) directly under the title: “All-Time” plus one pill per travel year (unique DEPARTURE years of landed legs, newest first; a 31 Dec red-eye belongs to its departure year, the earnings rule). Tapping one filters the NAVY HERO and syncs the inner row to match — one coherent page. **(2) The navy hero card** — a FIXED dark-navy gradient in both themes (a physical passport cover; its inks are fixed literals, never theme tokens — a theme-scoped linen would fail one theme), `grid grid-cols-2 gap-4`, TEN flying metrics: the big three — **Flights** (every landed leg, positioning sectors included), **Flight Time** (roster block times, a paxing sector borrowing its SIN sibling's FT, the rcDutyLegs rule), **Airports** (distinct landed non-SIN arrivals) — then **Busiest station** (the visit walk with the shuttle fold), **Top aircraft** (leg-count tally, ties by recency), **Longest flight** (route + time), **First flight** and **Latest flight** (house date format), **Layover days** (summed real ground at layover stops — open spans never guessed), **Regions** (the salary-region map over visited stations). Every time figure follows the owner's LARGEST-UNIT PAIR rule: days+hours when a day fits, hours+minutes under a day, minutes alone under an hour (“124d 11h” / “11h 40m” / “45m”). A Lucide icon beside each label (plane, clock, map-pin, crown, plane-takeoff, route, flag, history, bed, globe — all verified in the lucide 1.65.0 registry tarball), riding the render's own icon pass. **Landed legs only** — a passport is a flown record; an imported future month never counts until its flights land. **(3) The INNER pills** — the records section carries its OWN year row above the tab bar (the owner's two-chip model): it filters the records list ALONE (podium, tabs, rows, visit logs) and never touches the hero or the main chip; the chips diverge only when the crew diverges them. **(4) The gateway** — the full-width “All Flight Stats” button (static label, rotating Lucide chevron, the v1.45.47 affordance) toggles the records section with the owner's exact utilities — `hidden` + `opacity-0` + `translate-y-2`, a 220ms settle, re-renders apply settled (no replay, the accordion lesson), standing down under ca-reduced. **Every open starts clean**: All-Time chips, gateway COLLAPSED — the hero leads the page (owner ruling); nothing persists. Mechanics: one walk — `rcPodStats(year)` accumulates the hero scalars in the SAME visit walk the list already used (rcTripsAll gained the year slice + the ac field); `rcFtToMins` parses the roster FT string; `rcPodFmtPair` is the pair rule; the second walk runs only when the two chips diverge. APP_VERSION 1.45.52, CACHE_NAME v283, two generic imp pointers (10 + 10 words); guard sweep **378 items, 0 violations**. **v1.45.51 — the passport release (owner orders 2026-10-30, two pages):** **(1) The records page is now the PASSPORT** — the owner's name for it going forward (“it will be known as passport page overlay when conversing with you”; a content change may follow in a later round). The marks' `ca-rc-popout`/`ca-rc-popbout` keyframes (`both` fill) re-render the marks at their from-state on the way out, and an off-tap landing inside the pop-in window SNAPPED mid-flight marks back to full brightness (a genuine one-frame flash, frame-sampled in Chromium: badge 0.90 → 1.00 → fade); the camera's `ca-rc-btnout` fade read as part of the same blink. **v1.45.50 — the storage-era release (owner report 2026-10-30, after the legend ship: “the whole Oct 2025 calendar shows Flying duty — the roster printed no leg times for this day” — “please go through calendar for oct 2025”):** the parser was proven healthy FIRST, on the owner's real PDF (tests/ jsdom: `rosterParse` over `docs/_reference_rosters/Oct 2025.pdf` → 13 flights with full fields, 31 days {fly 19, off 11, duty 1}, month keys 2025-10) — and a full import→store→render simulation on a fresh boot (Sep+Oct 2025 both, and Oct alone) rendered the month PERFECTLY: legs, times, prices, the cross-month MEL→SIN 1 Oct trip retold from its September report day, and the new legend labels live (PD91 → “STC-ENHANCED PAX-SEP ATT-TOKEN”). The break was the DEVICE'S STORE, reproduced exactly by seeding duty days without the flight store: every fly day in the month showed the owner's line. Root cause: `crewAssist.allFlights` (the never-pruned leg store the calendar reads via `loadAllFlights()`) was BORN in v1.39.0 hotfix 4 — a month imported before that carries duty days (the duty store predates it by a few hours) but no legs, and nothing ever back-fills it; re-imports of OTHER months never touch it (per-month replace). The old fallback then blamed the ROSTER (“the roster printed no leg times”) for the app's own storage history — actively wrong. The fix: the fly-day fallback splits on the store's own month — when the month holds NO stored flights at all, the truth is the store, and the message says so AND names the remedy (“This month's roster was saved before the calendar kept flight details — re-attach its PDF to rebuild them.”); when the month DOES hold flights but the day has none, the honest parse-gap line stays (a real roster gap). The owner's immediate remedy is one tap: re-attach Oct 2025.pdf — the import replaces the month and the legs return (proven by the simulation). Shipped: the full table embedded VERBATIM as `RO_DUTY_DESC_RAW` (a template literal, one `CODE|DESCRIPTION` line per entry, ~34KB — offline-first, the same rule as everything else this app ships), parsed at boot into `var RO_DUTY_DESC` (var, the APP_VERSION precedent — const at script scope never reaches window for the test harness's eval; discovered the hard way when the first solo run crashed ReferenceError). The calendar's duty-day label now names the code's REAL meaning (33A2 → “A330 AIRCRAFT VISIT”) instead of the retired generic course-or-ground line; a standby code with a known window keeps its formatted Window line (better than the legend's inline times, and those rows are pinned); unknown codes fall back to the short “Ground duty”. **v1.45.23 — the tabular-grid release (owner order 2026-10-26, tenth of the day — “Refactor the monthly header summary layout to use a rigid 2-column tabular grid that prevents text wrapping and clashing on small screens like the iPhone 11 (375px)”):** the month group-head money column (`.ca-arch-amt` beside the month label — the earnings list only; the card subtext/after-line, the filtered variant, COP heads, yearheads and ledger rows are untouched) is now a **rigid two-column tabular grid**: `.ca-arch-moneygrid` = `display: grid; grid-template-columns: auto auto; column-gap: 8px; row-gap: 0; align-items: baseline; align-content: center; justify-content: end; text-align: right` — **column 1 labels only, UPPERCASE, left-aligned within the block** (`.ca-arch-mglbl`, the 11px MICRO rung with metalabel `.03em` tracking), **column 2 figures only, right-aligned to one decimal edge** (tabular digits via the existing `.ca-arch-amt` rule), strict row order **NET → GROSS → AFTER EXPENSES**, `white-space: nowrap` on EVERY cell (the `> span` rule, line-height 1.25). The old 1.45.22 pair-row renderer is gone: `lbl()`/`val()` cell helpers build 6 spans (4 when no expenses — the AFTER EXPENSES row **unmounts from the DOM entirely** at month expenses ≤ 0.004, NET+GROSS compressing naturally since grid rows carry no padding). **Visual priority:** the NET row alone carries the accent — the call site keeps its `style="color: var(--sia-gold-ink, #7d650f)"` on the block, so the NET label + figure inherit the gold ink (#7d650f light / #C9A227 dark) and font-weight 700 + the 17px value rung, while GROSS/AFTER EXPENSES opt out via `.ca-arch-mgmut` (`var(--arch-muted)`) at text-sm weight 400. **The 375px no-clash contract (measured in the rig, the order's headline):** a three-row grid is ~204px wide (label rail 100 + gap 8 + figure rail 96) and a September-width month name cannot share a 375px row with it — so the head row (`flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5 py-3 px-3`) now wraps: `.ca-arch-group .ca-arch-group-head { min-width: 120px; }` floors the month block, and the money grid (`margin-left: auto`) plus the projection-tap variant (`ml-auto` added) drop to their own line, still right-aligned — month label ONE line, count line ONE line, no cell clip, no sideways scroll at 375/390/414px; ≥~430px and every no-expense month (~144px grid) keep the side-by-side form unchanged. **Cascade trap recorded:** the head button also carries Tailwind's `min-w-0`, and tw.css loads AFTER the inline `<style>` — a single-class floor rule LOSES the tie (first 375px debug still crushed at 79px); the two-class selector wins by specificity, not order. APP_VERSION 1.45.22→1.45.23, CACHE_NAME v253→v254, changelog head two imp pointers. Tests: earnings-archive 504→**512** (money pins re-pointed to the grid contract — moneygrid class + the verbatim grid rule now ending `margin-left: auto`, 4/6 children, children[0] 'NET' / [2] 'GROSS' / [4] 'AFTER EXPENSES', value hooks at [1]/[3]/[5], the block style pin, NET gold exclusivity vs the `.ca-arch-mgmut` opt-out, the wrap/floor/proj-btn source pins); dev-ui 712 (E6 ladder [0]=1.45.23 with two pointers, 1.45.22→[1], the chain shifted +1, the 1.45.6 window pins retired keeping its src pointer); smoke 227→**237** (version pins re-stamped; the 1.45.22 layer re-pointed — rows 6/4, the UPPERCASE label at `previousElementSibling`, muted ink compared on the value CELLS not the old pair rows; the money probe re-pointed to `.ca-arch-amt.ca-arch-moneygrid` — grid display, 2 tracks, 8px gap, baseline/centre/end, per-cell nowrap, label rail one vertical line, value edges straight across months; a NEW v1.45.23 375px layer — the stacked form with one-line month + count at 375, rail/edge/no-clip/12px-inset pins, NET gold + secondary muted computed in BOTH themes, the 4-cell side-by-side no-expense form, and the 430px three-row side-by-side return). Battery 14/14 = **2,785/0** (35+712+512+26+24+79+199+135+333+202+351+33+130+14), smoke **237/237** — both after the bump; one battery rerun (roster-calendar flaked “lands the selection on today” under the first run's load, 333/0 solo and in the clean rerun — no code change). **tw.css NOT recompiled — `flex-wrap` and `ml-auto` both already ship** (grep-verified, as do all other utilities the release touches). app.html rebuilt, floor 1,225,380 bytes. Rig lessons: the smoke-rig selector for `text-[17px]` needs the two-backslash CSS escape in source (one backslash survives JS string parsing as an unknown escape and arrives bracket-naked — invalid); `pkill -f` patterns that appear in the calling command's own text kill the tool harness itself; and parallel battery launches flake the timing-sensitive suites — the battery stays sequential. **v1.45.22 — the overseas-expense release (owner order 2026-10-26, ninth of the day — three orders from the parked spec: the badge icon, the money column, the layover expense):** (0) **The badge trades its text triangles for the Lucide trending pair:** `#ca-arch-netyoy` now renders `<i data-lucide="trending-up|down" class="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0"></i><span>+2.4%</span>` — the icon by the YoY sign, the percentage SIGNED (`+`/`−`, matching the COP ctx line's convention), one decimal via `archYoyPct` as before. The badge container becomes an inline-flex pair (`align-items: center; gap: 4px` added to the `.ca-arch-netyoy` rule); the 14px value rung, bold tabular nowrap, `ml-3` 12px offset, the items-baseline figure row and **deep green `#123f22` / deep brick `#6e1f1f`** tinting icon AND text are all unchanged from 1.45.21. **The recorded triangle exception is STRUCK** from the v1.45.21 entry above (owner order, no successor written — the icons-only leaning needs no exception list again). Computed-style note for future pins: the badge is a flex ITEM (its parent is the baseline row), so `display: inline-flex` blockifies to `flex` in `getComputedStyle` — pin either. (1) **The month money column becomes labelled pair rows:** each figure is its own `flex items-baseline justify-end gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap leading-tight` line — lowercase label span + value span — NET top (17px bold, hook `.ca-arch-amt-net`), GROSS under it (text-sm, muted, hook `.ca-arch-amt-gross`); the column container gains `justify-center` so the stack centres vertically against the month/count lines; tabular digits via the existing `.ca-arch-amt` rule keep the decimals tracking one vertical edge. Flight rows, COP heads, yearheads and the search-filtered variant are untouched. (2) **Overseas layover expenses (new field):** layover trips carry `detail.expense` — one SGD lump sum per trip for what the crew actually spent on the stop, deliberately post-CPF context that NEVER touches the net headline, the chart, the insights, the milestones or the calculator. **Entry:** the saved entry's summary sheet (`openArchEntry` — the chat keeps only a plain text line after save, so the re-opened summary IS the saved trip's card) grows an 'Overseas expense' row for `flightType === 'Layover'` only: receipt icon + label left, the figure (or the muted 'Add expense' affordance) right; tap opens the `archEditAmount`-pattern inline editor (`ca-arch-amt-input` gold-underline, w-24 text-right) — Enter/blur commits, Escape cancels, **0 or clearing removes the field** (absent = none), the 6s 'Expense saved' toast carries Undo, and the archive re-renders behind the sheet so the card and list pick the figure up live. **Display (salary lens + basic set, scope-gated):** the card subtext extends to 'Gross $X | CPF −$Y | Exp −$Z' (segment absent at zero) and a quiet 'After expenses $Y' line (`#ca-arch-after-line`, micro, weight 400, opacity .7) rides under it — month/year/all-time sums each from their own scope's entries; a month with expenses grows a THIRD quiet group-head row 'after exp' (`.ca-arch-amt-after`, month net minus month expenses, unfiltered month only). The COP lens and the no-basic card show nothing (the figure stays keyable behind the salary gates). **Tolerance:** `archNormalize` passes `detail` through untouched, so old backups, pre-feature entries and the earnings JSON extract read as no expense; the full backup carries the field automatically. **CSV:** the export gains a fifth `expense` column (`0.00` default, never ragged). APP_VERSION 1.45.21→1.45.22, CACHE_NAME v252→v253, changelog one new + two imp pointers. Tests: earnings-archive 475→**504** (badge pins re-pointed to the icon + signed span + the no-triangle assertion; the money-block pins re-pointed to the pair-row contract — justify-center, nowrap rows, label+value hooks, the no-expense month exactly two rows; a new expense block — the card segments, the after line via the app's own `formatMoney`, the third head row, the sheet row on layovers only, the edit/undo/escape/zero flow, the no-basic silence, the CSV fifth column through a stubbed `archShareOrDownload`); dev-ui 706→**712** (E6 ladder [0]=1.45.22 with three pointers, 1.45.21→[1], 1.45.20→[2], the window re-indexed to floor 1.45.6 at [16]); next-flight 196→**199** (**rig fix, no app change:** the split-duty blocks seeded today/tomorrow whose 0035-MEL departure lands 21:35 SGT the same evening — any battery run in that window saw the crew airborne; the seeds moved to tomorrow/+2, run-hour-immune like the fixed-walls precedent, and the label pin now discriminates 'in 2 days' (flight day) vs 'tomorrow' (report row) the same way); stamps re-pointed (menu-extraction/offline-menus/sw/smoke); smoke 211→**227** (the badge probe re-pointed to the icon name + signed span + the 14px box + the flex pair; the money probe re-pointed to the value-span hooks + justify-center/nowrap/gap-6px; a new v1.45.22 layer drives the whole expense flow in the real browser — card segments, the three-row head with all three right edges on one line, the receipt row, typed edit → commit → live card → undo → zero removal, the COP-lens silence). Battery 14/14 = **2,777/0** (35+712+504+26+24+79+199+135+333+202+351+33+130+14), smoke **227/227** on 1.45.22/v253 — both after the bump. **tw.css NOT recompiled — every utility the release touches already shipped** (`whitespace-nowrap`, `justify-center`, `gap-1.5`, `mt-0.5`, `w-3.5`/`h-3.5`, `shrink-0` all verified present with `grep -cF`); the `receipt` icon verified in the lucide 1.61.0 tarball (dist icons map carries `Receipt`; `createIcons` maps the kebab). app.html rebuilt (floor 1,223,258 bytes). Rig notes: the SIXTH recycle of the day hit mid-turn — the ancient-base drift again (HEAD rewound to 14409f3 with docs/ + tests/ untracked; the standing recipe held untouched-working-tree: `git fetch` + `git reset --mixed 0534385`); the next-flight time bomb surfaced because of it (battery at 22:2x SGT, the only window it fires); formatMoney's comma bit twice — never build expected money strings with `toFixed`, always call the app's own `formatMoney`; and a python heredoc with lone-surrogate escapes truncates the target to zero bytes before failing — write docs with plain BMP text, restore from git, redo. - **Census.** The `^\*\*` count moves 197→198 with this entry's title.

**v1.45.11 (Monthly Salary — details; the release entry above stamps them):** the salary stack computes read-only over saved entries — `archSalaryMonth`/`archSalaryAgg` never touch Calculate. Traps and decisions worth keeping: (1) the midnight count reads the stored `allFlights` legs, not archive entries — a month with saved earnings but no stored roster counts zero midnights, by design (the roster is the truth for report times). (2) The chart's partial-month geometry: in COP mode the solid bar stays **flown-only** with the hatch for the remainder (the first draft scaled the solid by the full saved total, which cancelled the hatch — the 1.29.2 projection pins caught it); in salary mode the solid is the whole current stack (basic and fixed are monthly regardless) and the hatch is the projected flying on top. (3) The average-line label reads `Avg $X` — `archPeakFmt` prefixes "Peak:" and must not be reused for it. (4) A scope-pill click clears `chartSel` (a fresh entry into Month scope falls back to the newest/current month), but a month-bar tap from Year/All-time jumps into Month scope carrying the tapped month — the pill and the bar are different doors. (5) The Month-scope group defaults OPEN but a manual tap still collapses it — the collapse map records explicit user taps, the scope only picks the default. (6) `w.eval` cannot reach `let appProfile` (eval-scoped) — the no-basic prompt is tested by booting without `basicSalary` seeded, never by mutating the profile. (7) Rig lessons: the smoke's `app.html` is generated — after touching index.html's classes, rebuild tw.css (`tests/smoke/node_modules/.bin/tailwindcss -c tw.config.cjs -o tw.css --minify` from the repo root) AND re-run `build-app.js`, or Chromium boots a blank page; and a geometry-based `page.click` on a summary pill can race `renderArch`'s node swap — drive lens flips with a DOM-level `.click()` in `page.evaluate`. (8) The old `archChartData()` died with the chart rewrite (year units replaced the twelve-month array) — removed; `renderArchChart` now builds `archChartUnits()` (months | years) with a `shapeKey` (mode|scope|yr|copScope) that adds/removes the bargrow transition classes only on real shape changes.

**v1.45.10 (the searched flight's own cycle and chips — details; the release entry above stamps them):** the searched multi-sector cycle taps wrap at the end (findIndex + 1 modulo) and keep the sector dropdown in sync (the label rebuild uses the same escCode + inline-plane markup as the dropdown builder). `sectorLegs` is cleared on every open and refilled by the render, so a failed load can never cycle stale legs. The inert single-sheet chip is intentionally NOT wired to a listener — not even a no-op refresh — per the owner's "does nothing when user taps it". The fromSearch flag's setters are unchanged from v1.45.9 (search card sets true; `openNextFlightViewer` and the printer's saved-menu chip taps clear it); the printer↔viewer swaps keep inheriting it, so a searched print's viewer swap still scopes its cycle to the sectors. Trap for future rounds: when a new viewer behavior depends on the LEG COUNT, remember the button can only be re-shown from inside `renderMenu` — `openMenuViewerForCabins` runs before any menu data exists, and the rig's cached payloads (tiny timestamps) still serve through `SQ_CACHE_TTL` (it is effectively unlimited — check its value before assuming a payload has gone stale). Also this release (the deadhead transfer — details): the walk-back half of `rosterCalDutyFlights` already glides over the positioning leg (`while (i > 0 && all[i-1].arr !== 'SIN' && all[i-1].ymd <= ymd) i--`), so only the forward chain needed the transfer tolerance; the `xfer` flag reads the two legs' own `pos` fields, not any station list, so any future city-pair transfer (or a ground deadhead the parser ever learns) needs no new code. The fix deliberately does NOT relax the same-day SIN continuation (`cont`) or the four-leg cap — those rules stay binding. One caveat for old devices: months imported before v1.45.8 may lack the paxing leg in their stored allFlights (the old import dropped positioning legs) — the orphaned day heals but the paxing day shows no leg times until that month's roster is re-uploaded once.


**v1.45.9 (the one chip line, the searched-flight hide, the summary search — details; the release entry above stamps them):** the merged chip row keeps the flight chips first because that matches the old vertical order (flights above sectors); the hairline is `w-px self-stretch my-1 bg-black/10 dark:bg-white/15` with `aria-hidden`. The fromSearch flag rides `currentMenuSearch` — the same shared state the printer/viewer swaps already pass through, so no new plumbing was needed; the flag's only setters are the search card (true), `openNextFlightViewer` (false) and the printer chip tap (false). The summary haystack builds lazily per keystroke inside `archEntryMatches` with a try/catch guard — a malformed snapshot can never break the search. **Traps recorded from this round:** (a) changelog pointer escapes — index.html's items carry LITERAL — and ’ characters; dev-ui's src-pins write them as `\u2014`/`\u2019` (single backslash) which JS interprets to the character at parse time — writing the raw `\u2019` SEQUENCE into index.html breaks the pin silently (the runtime value is identical either way, only the src-text pin sees the difference); (b) a python heredoc writing test source needs `\\` to emit one backslash — over-escaping doubled them and under-collapsing broke three neighboring pins; verify with `cat -A` on the exact line, not by reading JSON-wrapped tool output; (c) the battery must run AFTER the version bump or every release-stamp pin silently validates the pre-bump state — that is exactly how 1.45.8 shipped with four stale suites.

**v1.45.8 (the paxing leg + the legend panel — details; the release entry above stamps them):** the allStore filter change is safe against the suite's synthetic (its items carry no pos sectors — the L86-88 allCount=5 pin holds). `ftBorrow` is display-only: it never enters the persisted stores, the earnings math, or the calculator (the v1.40.1 calculator borrow `ifaPaxPartnerHours()` stays DOM-input based and independent). The dutyY/locY leftmost-x hardening matters even with `isLegendPage` fixed: a page could carry both a table and a strip-style legend heading at odd x. Trap recorded from this round: when a synthetic repro passes but the real fixture fails, dump and diff the RAW item geometry (headers at odd x/y) before suspecting parser logic — the minimal synthetic attached CHC fine while the real file failed on a legend string the synthetic never modelled; and grep for the detector's own strings ('Duty Codes', 'Duty Desc') across every fixture before trusting a page-kind flag. Paxing fixtures for future work: Feb 2026 SQ298 CHC-SIN 02-13, Apr 2025 SQ828 SIN-PVG 04-17, Jun 2025 SQ810 SIN-PKX 06-28, Oct 2024 SQ366 SIN-FCO 10-13 — all print std/sta, none in Sep–Dec 2026.

Untouched by design: the saved-menu keys `SQ<fn>:<ymd>:` (`ymd` stays the key — existing saves keep working), the calendar's report-day model (`ymd` = report day, `stdYmd` = flight day — the rptLeg "Report 2335" row on the layover day already drew this distinction), `nfArrMsZone` (its `staYmd` base was already right), and `nfAlarmDayFirst`. `layoverTzInfo()` heals transitively. The overnight echo case (STD 2355 → STA 0530, sta < std on the clock) never mends — its `ymd` is already the departure date — pinned by test.

**v1.44.10 (reduced motion implemented + the binary Motion toggle — divergence #1a of the doc-vs-app audit + the owner's Reduced · Full ruling, 2026-10-15; app change, no version bump — the release commit below stamps the round):** the doc has promised reduced-motion respect since its Motion section was written; the app now delivers it. One marker, `html.ca-reduced`, drives every reduction: entries (chat-anim-in, menu-anim-in, the ca-rc pop/btn/flash family, caClGroupIn, devResetIn) collapse to one shared 150ms opacity fade (`ca-reduce-fade`); loops stop (caClSoftPulse, caTourPulse, float, jump, animate-pulse, animate-bounce); spinners stay (ca-spin / animate-spin encode progress); position transitions snap (the chevrons, the switch knob, calendar nav, date cells, the fetch face, and every Tailwind transition-transform surface — sheets open instantly); the sky paints one still frame and repaints on resize, with `syncSkyMotion()` restarting the loop when motion returns. The guard (`caMotionPref`/`caMotionReduced`/`applyMotionClass`) reads `crewAssist.motion` = 'reduced' | 'full' | absent → follow the OS (prefers-reduced-motion, with a live change listener) — resolved at parse time and re-resolved at boot init (late storage, embeds); the old media-query-only .ca-cl-group rule retired into the class gate so a Full override can beat an OS request. The Settings sheet gains the owner-ruled binary toggle — **Motion: Reduced · Full** (circle-pause / orbit, the calcui-pill pattern) — which mirrors the resolved state before any tap and stores the override on tap; `renderMotionRow()` keeps the highlight and description honest; the motion key joins BACKUP_KEYS. REPO_RULES documents the key; UI_UX gains the Reduced-motion subsection and the icon-table row. Verified: dev-ui +10 source pins +7 functional boots (storage wins, absent follows the OS, live toggling) = 608/608; battery 14/14 (2,358); smoke 94/94 with seven motion checks via emulateMediaFeatures (normal boot full; OS reduce → fade/none/0s sheets; the toggle mirrors; Full overrides live; a cleared key follows the OS again); normal-mode computed-style snapshot pre/post (stash-cycle, both themes): ZERO value diffs across 6,256 path-stable + 522 ID-anchored elements — full-motion users see nothing change. **v1.44.9 (audit fix 7 of 7 — closed WON'T-FIX, owner ruling 2026-10-15; docs-only, no version bump):** the last audit item leaves the board. The finding wanted a trailing newline on `tw.css`; its premise misread the file as a gitignored artifact — it is **tracked**, and the sanctioned CLI rebuild (`tests/smoke/node_modules/.bin/tailwindcss -c tw.config.cjs -o tw.css --minify`) always emits it newline-less, so a hand-added byte is silently reverted by the next rebuild. The owner ruled won't-fix on the recommendation: the file is machine-stamped generated output (one minified line, never hand-edited); no browser, test or tool reads the final byte; and making the newline stick would bolt a permanent extra step onto the rebuild command — reintroducing the exact fragility the hold existed to avoid. The Known-Gaps entry is relabelled with the ruling; `tw.css` itself is untouched (38,849 bytes, ends at the final `}`). With this, all seven audit fixes are resolved: 1 d3e70b1 · 2 5cc67f1 · 3 154d301 · 4 05ce37c · 5 009e062 · 6 eabdf47→032ac13 · 7 won't-fix. No app or test-relevant file changed (HANDOVER only; nothing pins it).

- **Census.** The `^\*\*` count moves 154→155 with this entry's title.

**v1.44.6 (owner order "proceed with 2" — the colour round 2, the one-off retirement: shrink the one-off colour values, "the naked eye should not be able to tell the difference"; item 3, the chart tooltip, explicitly left as is; APP_VERSION 1.44.5→1.44.6 + CACHE_NAME v224→v225 per the standing bump-both default):**

- **The measured inventory (not the estimate).** A recursive CSS parser (scratch tooling, not shipped) split the main `<style>` block into 480 rules and classified every colour literal outside the token definitions, print and dev-panel zones: **139 raw call sites** — 7 gradient stops, 13 shadow values, 93 alpha tints, 26 solids. (This reconciles the v1.44.4 flag: its "~160 one-offs" = 47 print + 17 dev + 96 main-block sites.) Of the 139, ~44 are the calendar's duty palette and the rest are washes, hairlines, glows and stops accumulated over 44 versions.
- **The retirement.** 79 call sites now ride **59 new round-2 tokens** (+6 per-theme overrides): the neutral line-gray fabric, the gold soft/glow ladders, the save-pill and two-tap status washes, the earnings chart's gold family (bar/projection/best/low/segment/goldcard stops), the changelog chips/tags/summary, the lightbox and shadow set, and the paired surfaces (switch track, roster sticky, unified inputs, onboarding border) that now flip per theme through `html:not(.dark)` overrides instead of hardcoded twins. Raw gradient stops outside the leave-zones: **zero**.
- **The merges — 5 value snaps, every one ≤.02 alpha on the same base.** Gray .07/.09/.10 → line-gray-10, .12/.14 → -13, .15 → -18; gold .10 → soft-12, .16/.18 → soft-15, .28/.30 → soft-30 (the rendered snaps: the changelog new-tag .16→.15, the summary card .10→.12 dark / .14→.15 light, the spinner ring .28→.30, the calendar row divider .14→.13). Two exact-value merges: the tour pulse's rgba(201,162,39,1) now rides var(--ca-gold), its 0 rides var(--ca-gold-clear).
- **The leave-list (60 raw sites, each ruled).** The calendar's duty palette (~44: k-fly/sb/lo/al/crs gut tints and markers both themes, the destination tags, the arrival badge) — one coherent family, owner ruling; the chart tooltip `.ca-arch-bartip` — owner order "leave 3 as is"; every text ink (#7dd3fc/#0369a1 sky tags, #f0abfc/#a21caf pink tags, #22c55e green, #dc2626/#ff4c4c two-tap, #047857/#6b7280 pills, #4b5563 micro, #7A620F light gold text, the stone chip inks, the help tip's .95 gray) — distinct roles that cannot merge with anything; the two calendar dark-tag inset shadows; and the standing exclusions (print ~47, dev-panel ~17, shell-fail failsafes, var() fallbacks).
- **The proof (the rig, both themes, 6,224 elements each).** style-snapshot before/after: **all 246 computed-style diffs enumerate to exactly the 5 intended snaps** — 114+114 tag backgrounds, 1+1 summary cards, 12 spinner-ring borders, 4 row dividers — **zero unintended changes**. The hover/keyframe snaps are statically proven (each token definition is byte-equal to the literal it replaced, except the five documented snaps listed in the round-2 comment). tw.css byte-identical (md5 unchanged — the sweep touched only the `<style>` block).
- **Tests.** dev-ui 540→550: the E6 ladder re-points (1.44.6 head, 1.44.0 → entries[6], the 1.43.0 consolidation survives at entries[7]) and ten round-2 guard pins (the token definitions, the goldcard/chart/spinner flagships, the paired surfaces, the keyframes, the merge-map comment, and the merged one-offs gone). smoke 86→87: release stamps 1.44.6/v225, the What's New header + 1.44.6 pointer, and the 1.44.5 pointer kept in the delta. sw pins re-stamped v225; menu-extraction's wnSeen seed → 1.44.6. Battery **14/14 = 2,299 asserts, 0 failed**; app.html 1,116,517 bytes.
