**v1.44.5 hotfix (owner order "1.hotfix (do not version bump)" — the menu viewer's dropdown outlived the close: open the viewer, tap a dropdown, select nothing, close the overlay, reopen — the dropdown still hung open; APP_VERSION freezes at 1.44.5 per the owner's order and the 2026-10-03 standing ruling, CACHE_NAME v223→v224 — the cache swap is delivery plumbing, not a version change):**

- **The bug.** `toggleMenuDropdown` keeps its open state as classes on static markup (`#menu-cabin-menu` / `#menu-sector-menu` / `#menu-culinary-menu` shedding `max-h-0 opacity-0 pointer-events-none`, the chevron gaining `rotate-180`, plus `#menu-dropdown-row`'s inline `overflow: visible` so the panel can escape the row's horizontal scroll). `closeMenuViewer` closed the lightbox but never the dropdowns, so every one of those survived the close — and the v1.44.5 reopen round rebuilt the lists but not the wraps' classes.
- **The fix.** New `closeMenuDropdowns()` beside `toggleMenuDropdown`: folds all three (the same class swap, riding the same 300ms `transition-all` — the animation rule holds), stands the chevrons back up, and hands the row its `overflow-x-auto overflow-y-hidden` back. `closeMenuViewer` calls it right beside `closeMenuLightbox()` — every close path (backdrop, X, the sheet-close map, `switchViewerToPrint`'s immediate close) routes through that one chokepoint, so no dropdown can outlive the sheet again.
- **Tests.** offline-menus 82→89: the owner's exact repro pinned state-level — open with two cabins, `toggleMenuDropdown('cabin')` (the harness's jsdom never fires inline onclick attributes, so the pin drives the toggle directly — the same call a real tap makes), assert open + chevron + row-escape, `closeMenuViewer()`, assert folded + chevron + scroll returned, then reopen and assert still closed. sw/smoke/menu-extraction/offline-menus cache pins re-stamped v224 with the no-bump reason in the message; the APP_VERSION and changelog pins stay at 1.44.5 untouched (the release line "Reopening earnings, menus, and print always starts fresh" already covers this fix — it makes it true). Battery **14/14 = 2,289 asserts, 0 failed**; smoke **86/86**; app.html 1,111,441 bytes; no CSS — tw.css untouched, style rig not needed.
- **Census.** The `^\*\*` count moves 143→144 with this entry's title.

**v1.44.5 (owner order "1.hotfix" — the reopen-resets round: the earnings archive, menu viewer and print preview must always start fresh on a close+reopen, "reset all to default including dropdowns, pills, scrolls"; APP_VERSION 1.44.4→1.44.5 + CACHE_NAME v222→v223 per the standing bump-both default):**

- **Earnings archive.** The user-facing open path is `showArchiveOverlay()` (the only caller of `openArchOverlay`), which already reset scope/search/collapsed/chartSel/insights state (the v1.27.1 block) but MISSED `projOpen` — a projection left open survived the close — and never touched the scroll. The sheet slides off with `translate-y-full`, so it stays laid out and the browser hands the old `scrollTop` back on reopen; renderArch's v1.33.2 keep-scroll then preserves it. The fix: `projOpen = {}` joins the reset, and `#ca-arch-scroll` zeroes BEFORE `openArchOverlay()` → renderArch, so the keep-scroll preservation reads 0. The v1.33.2 live-sweep preservation DURING an open session is untouched — only the close/reopen boundary resets (pinned both ways: a dirty scroll survives while the sheet stays open, a reopen returns 0).
- **Menu viewer.** Investigation before change: `openMenuViewerForCabins` ALREADY resets `menuViewState`/`currentMealServices` (a whole-object reassignment just before `loadMenu`) and re-defaults the cabin dropdown to the first cabin — the state reset was never missing, only unpinned. What was genuinely missing: the pane on the error path (a failed render never runs renderSector's `scrollMenuToTop`). The open now also zeroes `#menu-content`'s scrollTop before the render, and the existing state reset is now guarded by tests.
- **Print preview.** `openMenuPrinter` reset only sectorIdx/tab/model — layout, paper, protein marking, both zooms, greyscale, descriptions and bounds all survived a close. The open now restores every default BEFORE `applyPrintChrome()` repaints the pill chrome from state (so Elegant/A4/brackets/100%/the option pills all re-light), and both panes (`#print-paper-scroll`, `#print-edit-scroll`) return to the top. Content edits reset too, since the model is rebuilt from a fresh fetch.
- **Tests.** Three behavioural pin blocks. earnings-archive 290→297 (open → search "NRT" + Month scope + scrollTop 777 → close → reopen → search empty, All-time seg on, scroll 0, the full list back). offline-menus 75→82 (a rich seeded menu carrying meals+drinks+snacks pills; the pin drives `switchMenuCategory` directly because the harness's jsdom never fires inline `onclick` attributes — measured, not assumed). menu-extraction 67→79 (every printState field dirtied + both panes scrolled → `openMenuPrinter` → all defaults back; the reset precedes the fetch, so the asserts hold on the jsdom error path too). dev-ui 536→540 (E6 ladder re-points: 1.44.5 head, 1.44.4 → entries[1] … 1.44.0 → entries[5], the 1.43.0 consolidation pin survives at entries[6]). smoke release stamps re-pointed (1.44.5 / v223 / 'fix'; the What's New delta keeps the 1.44.4–1.44.0 pointers), and menu-extraction's wnSeen seed bumps to '1.44.5' so the What's New popup stays closed during its fv-card block. Battery **14/14 = 2,282 asserts, 0 failed**; smoke **86/86**; app.html 1,110,153 bytes; no CSS this round — tw.css untouched and the style rig not needed.
- **Census.** The `^\*\*` count moves 142→143 with this entry's title.

**v1.44.4 (owner order "get my approval and flag computed-style change before committing" — the colour-token normalization sweep, ROUND 1, in zero-surprise mode: the app's repeated colour call sites now ride named semantic tokens; owner approved the zero-diff report and the commit followed the same day; APP_VERSION 1.44.3→1.44.4 + CACHE_NAME v221→v222 prepared per the standing bump-both default):**

- **The safety rig first (the round's actual deliverable).** `tests/smoke/style-snapshot.js` + `style-diff.js`: boots the real app in the rig's real Chromium, renders a deterministic journey (calculator card, next-flight nudge, the seeded roster calendar, the changelog sheet, the earnings archive, settings, the date picker), freezes animation, and snapshots EVERY element's computed colour properties — color/background/borders/shadow/outline/fill, ::before/::after included — in BOTH themes; the differ flags every computed-style change. The rig was proven deterministic on identical builds (ZERO diff run-over-run) before it was trusted. Rig lessons baked in: transient chat elements (typing indicator, the 7s confirm card) must live out their timers; animation must be frozen at base state or the pulse dot and theme fade report race-y mid-tween values; opacity is excluded (a JS tween the freeze cannot pin, and the sweep never touches the opacity property); What's New/changelog subtrees are culled from the diff because the version dance legitimately re-renders them.
- **The sweep.** A semantic token block now sits beside the existing glass/arch/surface tokens: the gold family (--ca-gold, -hi, -bright, -soft-12/15, -line, -line-strong, -clear), inks (navy, deep, faint, stone, stone-deep), the paired tokens that flip per theme (--ca-panel-bg/ink/line for the date picker, --ca-cta-bg/ink for the gold CTA, --ca-toast-bg/ink/undo, --ca-danger-ink, --ca-rc-num-ink, --ca-cl-vhead-bg), status (--ca-ok, --ca-danger-ink/-bright/-line, --ca-success-deep), and the shared grays/glows/shades. 104 call sites across 73 rules swapped their literals for tokens (every replacement count-asserted; values byte-identical), including the base+dark rule PAIRS collapsing onto theme-flipping tokens and adoption of the pre-existing vars (--glass-bg, --sia-gold-ink) where values matched exactly. The `var(--token, fallback)` pattern keeps its literal fallbacks on purpose (insurance) — a fallback inside a var() reference is not a raw call site.
- **The proof.** style-diff before/after: **6,188 elements per theme, ZERO computed-style differences, both dark and light** — pixel-identical by construction AND by measurement. 30 of the swept rule families are verified on rendered elements; 22 state-dependent selectors (menu pills mid-sweep, the toast, checked switches, archive month states, calendar tag/track states, hero edges, hover rules — a resting page cannot paint hover states even in principle) are statically proven: each token definition is byte-equal to the literal it replaced, so var() substitution cannot change the value.
- **Flagged, untouched (the honest remainder).** (a) print styles — .pe-*/.pc-*/print blocks, ~47 sites: a physical medium with its own measured palette, out of scope by the ruling's own scope note; (b) developer-panel styles — ~17 sites, internal tooling; (c) ~160 one-off literals — gradient stops (the hero/goldcard gradients), the calendar's per-duty-kind palette (the blue/amber/purple gut colours — a coherent named family of its own), one-off shadows and borders (rgba(239,68,68,.40), rgba(0,0,0,.74), #6B7280, #0B0E14…), each existing exactly once so they cannot drift by copy-paste — tokenizing them is a ROUND 2 decision (each needs its own semantic name); (d) the shell-fail screen's inline literals — deliberate failsafe hardcodes for when the app shell itself is broken; (e) one observation, no action taken: `.ca-arch-bartip` rides a near-black bg with white text in BOTH themes (a chart tooltip) — possibly intended (chart context), flagged for the owner's eye.
- **Tests.** dev-ui 536/536 (was 529): E6 re-points (1.44.4 head, the ladder shifts down, the 1.43.0 consolidation pin survives at entries[5]); new guard pins (the token block defined for both themes, the flagship call sites riding tokens, the rig files shipping). earnings-archive 290/290 (one pin re-pointed: the light-mode cream card regex now matches var(--ca-sheet-solid)). smoke 86/86 (release stamps re-pointed 1.44.4/v222 + the What's New delta keeps the 1.44.3 and 1.44.2 pointers; one intermittent: the help-row gold-flash pin failed once on a chat-busy race and passed on re-run — first-seen flake, not related to this round's CSS). menu-extraction/offline-menus/sw.test literals re-stamped. Battery **14/14 = 2,252 asserts, 0 failed**. tw.css byte-identical. app.html 1,107,916 bytes. **The commit happens only after the owner approves this report.**

**v1.44.3 (owner report on the deployed v1.44.2, 2026-10-14 — the picker's swipe glided over NOTHING: "I tap down, swipe left and hold — nothing displays, but on release it lands on the correct month." The commit logic was right; the LIVE track was invisible. Root cause + fix; APP_VERSION 1.44.2→1.44.3 + CACHE_NAME v220→v221 per the standing bump-both default):**

- **The root cause — two layers.** `#cadate-grid` is itself a 7×6 CSS grid (`grid-template-columns: repeat(7, minmax(0,1fr)); grid-template-rows: repeat(6, 2.25rem); place-items: center`). The v1.44.2 track was inserted as its child, so the 300%-wide flex row landed as ONE grid item — clipped into a single 2.25rem cell (layer one). The first fix attempt (grid `display: block` during drag) passed jsdom and looked plausible, but the REAL-browser probe showed the track still a full grid-width off: `place-items: center` kept centering the oversized child, so the middle month sat 300px left of the finger and the grid area stayed blank (layer two — caught only by real-geometry pins; jsdom has no layout, and the v1.44.2 pins only asserted the track's EXISTENCE, never its geometry).
- **The fix — span, don't stack.** While a drag owns the grid, the track now SPANS it: `.ca-date-track { grid-area: 1 / 1 / -1 / -1; justify-self: start; align-self: start; }` — the 300%-wide flex row covers all 7 columns and all 6 rows, pinned to start (deterministic in every engine; no display games, no height games). Each third carries the resting grid's own template (`.ca-date-third` — 7 columns, 6×2.25rem rows, place-items center), so the gliding months are pixel-identical to the resting one. `#cadate-grid.ca-date-drag` keeps `overflow: hidden` + `cursor: grabbing`. The engage/commit/spring/flick logic is untouched — it was never wrong.
- **The test lesson — pin GEOMETRY, not existence.** dev-ui 529/529 (was 520): the picker block grows layout pins (three `ca-date-third` children, every third carrying real month grids ≥27 days, the CSS pins for the spanning rule + the third template) and E6 re-points (1.44.3 head, 1.44.2 → entries[1], 1.44.1 → entries[2], 1.44.0 → entries[3], 1.43.0 → entries[4]). smoke 85/85 (was 78): the drag block now pins the HELD state in the real Chromium — the track's computed `grid-area: 1 / 1 / -1 / -1` + `justify-self: start`, three grid-widths wide, the middle month's left edge exactly at gridLeft−160 (under the finger), real 36px day cells, and `elementFromPoint` at the finger hit-testing INSIDE the track (a `.ca-date-third`, not the bare grid) — then the release commit and the ArrowLeft walk as before. The smoke rig's real browser is the only net that can see paint; the jsdom suites see structure. menu-extraction/offline-menus/sw.test literals re-stamped (1.44.3 / v221). Battery **14/14 = 2,245 asserts, 0 failed**. app.html new floor: 1,103,143 bytes.

**v1.44.2 (owner order, 2026-10-14 — the standing rule extended to COLOR+THEME+INTERACTION (v2 text adopted into this file's own section, replacing the v1 typography-only standing section), plus the date-picker navigation hotfix: swipe + arrow keys; APP_VERSION 1.44.1→1.44.2 + CACHE_NAME v219→v220 per the standing bump-both default; index.html + tw.css + sw.js + dev-ui + smoke + menu-extraction + offline-menus + sw.test pins + this entry):**

- **The rule swap.** The owner expanded the standing typography rule into a six-section "TYPOGRAPHY & COLOR DISCIPLINE" ruling: the type scale (unchanged from v1.44.1) now rides with (1) a semantic colour-token scale (surface tiers bg-base/raised/elevated/pressed/input; text tiers primary/secondary/tertiary/inverse; accents gold, gold-soft, success, warning, danger, info; borders subtle/default/strong — never a raw hex at a call site, never a new token, one accent per surface, gold as the leitmotif), (2) theme discipline (both themes readable — ~7:1 primary, ~4.5:1 secondary, tertiary legible; borders/icons/placeholders polarity-correct; dark-only ambient stays dark-only), (3) interaction discipline (complete state sets — desktop rest/hover/active/focus/disabled/selected, mobile rest/pressed/released/disabled/selected; press = scale-down 0.96–0.98; ≥44px touch targets; visible focus rings; no hover-only content; hover disabled on touch; scroll ≠ press; ≤250ms transitions), (4) the 22-question self-check, and (5) the consistency principle. The full text replaces the old standing section above (the v1 wording survives in the v1.44.1 entry below), and the in-source ladder comment at the head of index.html's style block now covers colour+states too. The concrete mapping is pinned in the section: the rungs table (MICRO 11 / CAPTION 12 / BODY 14 / AMOUNT 17 / COMPACT 18 / TITLE 20 / DISPLAY 24 / HERO 36) plus how this codebase already implements each token (glass-panel/glass-bubble = bg-raised; the gray-400/500 + dark:gray-300/400 ladder = text-secondary; sia-gold + --sia-gold-ink = accent-gold; ca-hairline = border-subtle; active:scale-95 = pressed; hoverOnlyWhenSupported = hover-behind-media-query). Scope note: a colour-token NORMALIZATION SWEEP of the existing CSS was NOT ordered — existing call sites are untouched; the rule governs new elements and any element that gets touched anyway. Offered as a future round.
- **The hotfix — the date picker navigates like the calendar.** Mirrors the roster calendar's hotfix-18 patterns exactly: (1) a pointer-driven three-thirds swipe — pointerdown on the day grid arms, intent locks at |dx| ≥ 12px and ≥ 1.2×|dy| (a vertical scroll never engages), the engage builds a 300%-wide track at translateX(-33.3333%) holding the current month plus real clones of the neighbouring months (`caDateMonthHtml(y, m ∓ 1)` — the hotfix-17 "never a void" lesson, and the month HTML is normalised through a Date roll so a December/January clone crosses the year honestly), the live months' day buttons stand down (`pointer-events: none` via the `ca-date-drag` class, `user-select: none` on the track); release past the threshold (max(48px, 22% of width) — the picker's months are unbounded so there is no rubber band, unlike the calendar) commits the month change with a 240ms snap to the resting third and re-renders WITHOUT a second slide (the track WAS the glide); a short drag springs back to the resting month, restoring the base grid so no synthetic day-click leaks; a fast flick with no move events (≥48px) falls back to the chevron's canned slide. (2) The arrow keys — while the picker backdrop is open the arrows drive `cadate-prev`/`cadate-next`, with the calendar's own guards: the caret guard (INPUT/TEXTAREA/contentEditable keeps its arrows), the closing-window guard (the backdrop's 200ms fade-out — `opacity-0` — stands down; note the open's own 10ms fade-in start shares the class, so the keys arm ~10ms after open), a live-track guard (a mid-snap drag owns the grid), and a modal-ownership check BEFORE the calendar's topSheet branch — the picker sits at z-[92] above every sheet, so it takes the key outright. renderCaDateGrid also now flushes any live drag at the top (a re-render during a snap cancels cleanly). Touch and mouse both ride the same pointer events — no separate mouse path, matching the calendar.
- **tw.css:** rebuilt with the sanctioned command — byte-identical (the new styles are custom CSS classes, not utilities; one self-catch: a comment phrase "a clone's m-1 / m+1" made the scanner mint a stray `.m-1` — reworded to "minus-one / plus-one" and the build came back identical).
- **Tests.** dev-ui 520/520 (was 500): E6 re-points (1.44.2 head with its single pointer, 1.44.1 → entries[1], 1.44.0 → entries[2], 1.43.0 → entries[3]); a new picker-navigation block pins the whole contract jsdom-side — open on a fixed date, arrows walk months and preventDefault, the caret guard, the engage (track + drag class), the commit (no second slide, track gone, month +1), the spring-back (month untouched, day buttons restored), the no-move flick, the vertical-dominant guard, the closed-picker stand-down, and source pins (real neighbouring clones, the chevron wiring + live-track guard, the no-select/stand-down CSS); the will-change census grows 2→3 (the calendar's drag + glide tracks plus the picker's swipe track — all transient, none at rest); the documented-scale pin follows the v2 title. smoke 78/78 (was 75): release stamps re-pointed (APP_VERSION 1.44.2, changelog entry, What's New v1.44.2 header + the picker pointer + the 1.44.1 type-scale pointer in the delta, version-once, sw v220) plus a real-gesture block — a real card's date button opens the picker, a REAL mouse drag (-160px, steps 8) changes the month, ArrowLeft walks back, cancel closes (two rig lessons: `:last-of-type` lies once non-card divs follow the card in the chat, and the card's sector fields mount a tick after the card, so the wait is on the date button itself). menu-extraction/offline-menus/sw.test literals re-stamped (1.44.2 / v220). Battery **14/14 = 2,236 asserts, 0 failed**. app.html new floor: 1,102,020 bytes.

**v1.44.1 (owner ruling adopted + applied project-wide, 2026-10-14 — TYPOGRAPHY DISCIPLINE, a standing rule: one fixed type scale chosen by role, never tuned by eye; APP_VERSION 1.44.0→1.44.1 + CACHE_NAME v218→v219 per the standing bump-both default; index.html + tw.css + sw.js + dev-ui + smoke + menu-extraction + offline-menus + sw.test pins + this entry):**

- **The rule.** The owner's nine-point typography ruling is now a standing rule, added verbatim as its own HANDOVER section ("Typography discipline — standing owner rule") with this app's concrete rungs mapped beside it: MICRO 11 (`ca-micro`) · CAPTION 12 (`text-xs`) · BODY 14 (`text-sm`) · AMOUNT 17 (`text-[17px]` + `ca-amt`, tabular — the v1.32.0 E5 money ladder, kept) · COMPACT 18 (`text-lg`) · TITLE 20 (`text-xl`) · DISPLAY 24 (`text-2xl`) · HERO 36 (`text-4xl`). The same ladder is documented in-source at the head of index.html's `<style>` block, where the old money-only ladder comment lived. Print sheets (`.pe-*`, `--print-type`) keep their own physically measured scale; icon glyph sizes are not text.
- **The sweep — every off-scale size in the app UI moved onto a rung.** Sub-MICRO text is gone (nothing readable below 11px): the calendar's tags 10→11 and arrival badge 10.5→11; the earnings archive's meta labels, bar labels, bar tooltips, badges and insight labels 10→11; the changelog sheet's NEW/CURRENT badges 9→11 and new/fix/imp/fun tags 9.5→11; the dev rates table headers 9→11 and accordion titles 10→11; the print editor's eye buttons 10→11, badges 9→11, field labels 9→11. In-between sizes are gone: the calendar's two day-number treatments now MATCH end to end (pill-grid `.ca-rc-num` 13→14, timeline gutter `.n` 15→14 with weight 800→700 — same job, same treatment); the custom date picker's day cells 12.8→14; the chat-card date inputs dropped their 13px one-off and inherit BODY like every sibling input; the archive meta values 13→14 and toast 13→14; the help-card keyword and the print-editor title input 13→14; the route hero's station codes stepped down one rung 30→24 (nothing sits between DISPLAY and HERO — the codes now match their times); the shell-guard fallback screen's body/secondary/CTA 15.2/13.6/12.8→14/12/12. Rule 8 (inputs = BODY): the dev rates inputs and print-editor fields 12→14.
- **Nothing else moved.** Weights, colours, letter-spacing and line-heights are untouched except the one gutter-weight unification; the AMOUNT role keeps its documented 17px; print CSS untouched; icon sizes untouched.
- **tw.css rebuilt:** net −3 selectors (`.text-3xl`, `.text-[13px]` — both dropped from source — and `.tabular-nums`, which turned out to be comment-induced dead CSS: no element carries the class, the token only appeared in the old ladder COMMENT, and the Tailwind scanner extracts tokens from comments — the same gotcha that once re-emitted a dead navy utility, running in reverse: a comment keeping a dead utility alive).
- **Tests.** dev-ui 500/500 (was 494): the E6 block re-points (1.44.1 head with its one pointer, 1.44.0 → entries[1], the 1.43.0 consolidation → entries[2]; word-cap loops re-size with the data), the type-floor pin extends to all arbitrary utilities, and a new typography-discipline pin set holds the rungs (no sub-MICRO CSS, no 12.8/13/15 CSS, no `text-3xl`, the two day-number rules matching, inputs at BODY, the ladder documented in-source); the cadate merged-rule pin re-points to 0.875rem; the help-keyword pin now asserts its BODY rung. smoke 75/75 first run (was 73): release stamps re-pointed (APP_VERSION 1.44.1, the changelog entry, What's New v1.44.1 header + the type-scale pointer + the 1.44.0 help pointer still in the delta, version-once, sw v219), the marks pin tightened to the 11px MICRO floor, and a new computed pin reads the calendar's rungs in the real browser (day numbers 14, marks 11). menu-extraction/offline-menus/sw.test literals re-stamped (one self-catch: the first menu-extraction re-stamp changed the pin's label but not its `crewassist-v218` literal — the battery caught it, both now v219). Battery **14/14 = 2,216 asserts, 0 failed**. app.html new floor: 1,091,597 bytes.
- **Census.** The `^\*\*` count moves 136→139: this entry's title, plus the new standing-rule section's two bold paragraph leads (`**This app's concrete rungs**`, `**Scope notes:**`) — the census counts bold-led lines, not just round titles.

**v1.43.0 round 31 (owner-approved rework, 2026-10-11 — the proactive-trio visual + behaviour rework, rulings A–L all ruled in; APP_VERSION stays 1.43.0, CACHE_NAME v212→v213; index.html + sw.js + next-flight + smoke pins + this entry):**

- **The change (A–L, as ruled).** The proactive trio is recast as a two-tier feed. **A** — row pills with real states: Ready (grey) / Saving (gold + spinner) / Saved (emerald + check) / Partial "n/N" (gold, keeps the count) / "Not published" (grey-INFO — a getcabin statusCode 101 is not a red failure) / "Couldn't save" (red); an unfinished sweep flips the CTA to "Retry failed", which re-runs ONLY rows whose pill ≠ ok. **B** — single-flight CTA stays "Open menus" (save-then-open). **C** — rows are ALWAYS tappable (→ openNextFlightMenus, which saves too); the single-flight nudge is headline + CTA only, no row (rows are the multi-flight pattern). **D** — X dismissal unchanged (permanent per flight + date). **E** — a slim confirm card after a full save: one line, emerald check, "Menus saved. Available offline on your flights.", X + 7s auto-dismiss, slides in as the newest feed item. **F** — the layover TZ card rides hero-lite (non-dismissible, no CTA). **G** — hero gradient: dark navy→deep, light warm ivory, gold top edge both. **H/I/K** — nudge pruning (menusSavedFor), the online-only gate, and all anchor/duty/alarm logic untouched. **J** — order-preserving render + scroll discipline: the feed order TZ → flight card → nudge → confirm is CANONICAL (`NF_FEED_ORDER` + `nfInsertAtSlot`) — this is the fix for the owner's "it goes up" report (a badge refresh used to re-append the card BELOW a completed nudge). **L** — APP_VERSION frozen 1.43.0, CACHE_NAME v213.
- **The CSS (search "round 31 (owner spec)").** Tokens `--ca-hero-bg/-border/-shadow` with `html.dark` pairs; `.ca-hero-card` (radius 22 tl-0, gradient via background-image, gold border, real shadow in light / border luminance in dark), `.ca-hero-edge` (absolute 2px gold gradient line, inset 14px, pointer-events:none), `.ca-hero-lite` (surface-1 + hairline, radius 22), `.ca-nudge-card` (surface-1 + hairline, radius 16, lighter padding), `.ca-menunudge-row + .ca-menunudge-row` hairline dividers, and `.ca-pill` — six data-states × light+dark = 12 declarations (6 dark twins), greys from gray, gold from sia, green/red from the existing save/failure families. NO backdrop-filter on either tier (hotfix 16/18 doctrine: only the login hero keeps blur).
- **The JS.** `nfInsertAtSlot(el, slot)` + `NF_FEED_ORDER` sit before renderLayoverTzCard; the TZ card swaps to hero-lite and takes slot 0; the flight card captures `hadOld` BEFORE the remove (chat-anim-in and scrollToBottom fire only when genuinely new), swaps to the hero surface + edge span, and takes slot 1. renderMenuNudge rewritten: a live nudge (`data-nfBusy`/`data-nfDone`) is never reaped by a refresh; rows are wired from the start; `runSweep` walks saveMenusForFlightOffline sequentially and maps ok / partial saved+"/"+cabins / reason==='unpublished' / fail exactly per ruling A; a full save completes the nudge IN PLACE (the v1.28.2 `ca-menulaunch-card` rename is retired) — emerald "Saved" CTA with pointerEvents none, headline swap, `renderNextFlightCard()` refresh (which now keeps its slot above), and `showNfConfirm()` (slot 3, max-height/opacity collapse, 7s auto-kill). Amber is retired from the nudge entirely.
- **The incident (a new standing lesson).** Mid-round, six parallel edit_file calls against index.html RACED — each applied to a stale read, last-writer-wins: six intended edits silently vanished (the helper, hadOld, the hero innerHTML, both slot conversions) while four UNRELATED regions got spliced/duplicated (a mashed appConfirm line ~:12888, a mangled applyRecentFlight ~:13136, a duplicated fetchMenuIfNeeded ~:13421, garbage after `</html>`). Caught by the node --check-after-every-write rule; diagnosed via git-diff hunk arithmetic (the missing edits' line-offset shifts were absent); recovered by rebuilding from HEAD with a throwaway exact-match script (unique-occurrence assert on every anchor + final invariants: 4 slot call sites, CSS pins 2/12/6, launcher rename gone, `restoreFullBackup` unique, EOF intact) — the verified diff then contained ONLY the intended hunks. **NEVER issue parallel edit_file calls against the same file — sequential only; and read the full hunk map after any multi-edit round.**
- **Pins.** next-flight 143→173 (+30): header de-ambered; single-flight pins → utensils icon, w-full CTA, no-row; the launcher block → done-state pins (nfDone flag, 3× ok pills, confirm present, inert CTA, card-above-nudge via compareDocumentPosition); NEW anatomy+order block (hero/edge/nudge surfaces, ready pills, no amber, order survives `w.eval('renderNextFlightCard()')`, nudge node never reaped, full sweep → ok pills + confirm + X dismiss); NEW pill-semantics block (getcabin 101 → "Not published", one-of-two menus 101 → "1/2", "Retry failed", fixed-stub retry → done + confirm line + 7s auto-dismiss); NEW source-pair block (2/12/6 + NF_FEED_ORDER + 4 slot sites); TZ block gains the hero-lite pin. smoke 62→65 (+3, before the health block): hero gradient vs flat nudge + differing radii; the BOTH-THEME computed pair (light vs dark fills shift — real var() resolution in Chromium; jsdom cannot resolve var(), so the jsdom side pins source pairs); the gold edge computed at 2px. sw literal ×6 sites v212→v213 (sw.js:1, smoke.js:87, menu-extraction:185, offline-menus:201, sw.test.js:16+96).
- **Measurements.** index.html 17,650 → 17,758 lines (+108), 1,061,820 → 1,069,919 bytes. tw.css + app.html rebuilt — new byte floors: tw.css md5 b8674cc969bde1fcebd8745166a73ef3 (root + rig copies identical), tests/smoke/app.html md5 620e1951dc69b3ef47267bd4e06ea712 (1,068,768 bytes, gitignored).
- **Tests.** Battery 14/14 = **2,155 asserts**, 0 failed (next-flight 173; every other suite at its round-30 total — dev-rates 25, dev-ui 471, earnings-archive 290, exports 26, logic-cases 24, menu-extraction 67, offline-menus 75, roster-calendar 299, roster-pdf 191, roster-real 338, sw 32, tour 130, trip-bar 14). Smoke 65/65 FIRST RUN, zero page errors, zero console errors. (The first battery attempt ran 13/14 — next-flight crashed on `Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING`: Node's CommonJS main thread exposes no global `Node`; fixed via `d.defaultView.Node` and the battery re-run clean. The suite's jsdom "Not implemented: navigation" stderr line is the pre-existing reload noise, exit 0.)
- **Environment.** Drift #17 at the turn boundary (branch pointer reset + both node_modules, app.html and /tmp/chr-libs wiped — fetch+reset recipe, npm installs, app.html rebuilt md5-IDENTICAL to the round-30 floor before any edit, proving the sources intact). The same-file parallel-edit corruption and its HEAD-rebuild recovery are described above.

**v1.43.0 round 27 (owner-approved test-side fix, 2026-10-11 — the task-2 toggle flake, hardening option "class + bounded colour poll"; tests/smoke/smoke.js ONLY; no app code, no version bump, no sw bump, no changelog pointer):**

- **The change.** The toggle-is-exempt pin (the last live TEST-MAINT task) no longer races a colour fade. The old form tapped `#ca-rc-tag`, slept a fixed 300ms, and string-compared the computed backgroundColor against the gold `rgb(201, 162, 39)`.
  The new form asserts the logical state first — the `ca-rc-tagon` class the tap flips instantly (index.html:4803) — then polls the computed gold to settled inside a bounded 2s `waitForFunction`, caught so a timeout reports as a FAILED CHECK, never a rig crash (an uncaught rejection would exit 2 and take the whole run with it).
  The fixed `sleep(300)` is gone and nothing was blindly lengthened. The check count goes 61→62 (one pin became two); both labels keep the "hotfix: the toggle is exempt" prefix.
- **Why (the diagnosed race).** The tap flips the class synchronously, but the button carries Tailwind's `transition-colors` (150ms, index.html:1703), so the computed colour FADES to gold; under main-thread jank the fade starts late and 300ms was not always enough — a false FAIL with the class correctly present.
  Same morning, same bytes: the pre-fix pin fired 3/5 runs (twice consecutively) on the md5-verified round-26 app.html with only a docs diff in flight — pure environmental timing, which is why the fix asserts the class and bounds the colour wait instead of sleeping longer.
- **Proof.** Smoke ×5 on the hardened pin: 62/62, 62/62, 62/62, 62/62, 62/62 — zero failures, zero page/console errors, on the same artifact (app.html untouched, still the round-26 bytes, md5 7bd72974…).
  Battery 13/13 = 2,137 asserts, 0 failed, run for the record — smoke.js is not part of the jsdom battery (run-all globs `tests/*.test.js`), so it is unaffected by construction.
- **TEST-MAINT bookkeeping.** Item 2 relabelled [CLOSED — round 27, class + bounded poll] with the fix record appended; the block header now reads ALL CLOSED (1, 2, 3) — no live tasks. The `^\*\*` census moves 123→124 with this entry's title.

**v1.43.0 round 24 (owner-authored diff, approved as-is after agent review; 2026-10-11 — four dedupe/extract refactors in index.html; +62/−79 applied VERBATIM; no version bump, no sw bump, no changelog pointer):** like round 19, the owner wrote the diff and the agent reviewed it before implementation — all four changes approved, including one known behavioural delta the review surfaced (below). **(1) Telegram link hardening:** the Settings Telegram anchor is the file's ONLY `target="_blank"`; it now carries `rel="noopener noreferrer"` (noopener is implied by modern browsers on _blank, but old webviews in crew-hands don't all imply it, and noreferrer additionally suppresses the Referer header — belt-and-braces, zero visible change). **(2) `caPaintTripRoute(route, nodes, fetched, prefix)`:** the LMA and IFA trip-bar painters were proven BYTE-IDENTICAL modulo the literal `lma`/`ifa` prefix (both blocks extracted, prefix-normalised, diff clean) before extraction; one shared painter now serves both flows, the prefix making the id/class strings per-flow as before. **(3) `menuCabinNames()`:** the fv card and the menu viewer each carried an identical inline `cabinNames` map (FCL/JCL/SCL/YCL → labels, each with its own 'Premium Economy' copy); the skeleton is extracted once and the three are now one-line delegates — each callback preserves its builder's exact field logic (jsdom: JCL/SCL/YCL render Business/Premium Economy/Economy; FCL A380 → 'Suites', 777 → 'First'; WCL unchanged). **Guard pins, 6 asserts across 3 suites (per house convention; trivially removable):** trip-bar — `caPaintTripRoute(` ×3 in source + the trip-node class list exactly once; menu-extraction — `menuCabinNames()` ×3, 'Premium Economy' ×1 in the whole file (the F-02 dedup discipline: a third copy cannot silently return), the bare-string row pattern `menuItemRow(item, '', '', '')` ×1; stash popped clean. **Tests:** battery 13/13 = 2,137 asserts, 0 failed (dev-ui 507, menu-extraction 67, trip-bar 14; all others unchanged). **Smoke ×3 (app.html rebuilt, 1,061,320 bytes): 61/61, 61/61, 61/61 — the recorded task-2 toggle flake did not fire this session.** **Environment:** git drift #14 wiped node_modules in BOTH rigs again (reinstalled; **v1.43.0 round 23 (owner-approved, 2026-10-11 — F-07 executed per the rain-check: a standalone, explicitly-labelled formatting-only round; index.html + ONE forced test-regex touch + HANDOVER; no version bump, no sw bump, no changelog pointer):** the ~830-line airport/timezone anomaly region (old lines 7692–8530: the airports table, AIRPORT_TZ, the TZ helpers, countryToRegion, getRegionForAirport — the file's single off-ladder zone, 26% on the 8-space ladder vs 100% everywhere else) is normalised onto the file's 8/12/16/20 ladder, and the whole file swept for remaining inconsistencies. **What changed:** 81 line-joins (the region's split assignments, split `if (` conditions, `Date.parse(`/`new Set(` arg openers and method chains collapsed to the file's dense style), 57 blank-line removals (blank-after-`{`, doubles, blank-before-closers), depth-driven reindent with a closer-run rule (so `});` aligns with its statement), a file-wide trailing-whitespace strip (161 lines; 576 template-interior lines detected and PROTECTED by the scanner — the file's multi-line template literals embed HTML whose indentation is string content), and one out-of-region stray: the block-2 section comment `// Inflight Menu Viewer Logic (Step 5)` that sat at column 0 behind a four-blank run, moved onto the ladder. **v1.43.0 round 22 (owner-approved batch, 2026-10-11 — the owner's index.html review F-01..F-08 shipped, minus F-07 which the owner rain-checked for a standalone formatting-only commit in a quiet round; index.html + offline-menus/dev-ui/earnings-archive + smoke; no version bump, no sw bump, no changelog pointer):** every finding was independently verified before implementation, with two new facts beyond the review: the v1.37.0 "3.9:1 on cream" contrast claim does not reproduce (measured: #7d650f = 4.99:1 and #7A620F = 5.21:1 on the real cream #F6F1E8 — BOTH pass WCAG AA, so the dead override was imperceptible, not an accessibility emergency), and the escape table existed THREE times, not two. **F-01:** the stray post-`</html>` fragment (three lines: `script>` / `</body>` / `</html>`; shipped since v1.36.1 commit aab2bfe — a paste artifact; the spec re-parents the text node into `<body>`, invisible below the fold behind `body{overflow:hidden}` but live in the a11y tree; proven in jsdom AND real Chromium) — removed. Behavioral pins (offline-menus, reusing its setOnline idiom): offline + styles missing → the repair screen shows at once; styles present → the guard stays silent. **jsdom note:** the harness loads no tw.css, so `caTwLoaded()` is genuinely false there — the guard's 6s online timer flips #ca-shell-fail to flex mid-suite in long jsdom boots; harmless (no jsdom pin reads that element; structural pins don't see display) — the 13/13 battery proves it. The smoke's boot pin ("shell guard passes, tw.css loaded") is now meaningful rather than trivial. **F-04:** three escapers — `rosterEsc` (4079), `ratesEsc` (8575) and `escapePrintHtml` (15157, block 2 — a THIRD copy the review missed, caught by the new one-table count pin during implementation). One implementation `caEsc`; all three legacy names are one-line delegates; every call site reads unchanged. Pin: exactly one `/[&<>"']/g` table in the file. **F-05:** eight icon-only buttons had no accessible name (btn-theme, btn-settings, btn-reset, btn-send + the four sheet closers) — aria-labels added per the sibling convention (closers = "Close", matching ca-changelog-close; btn-reset = "Reset chat", accurate for the v1.29.0 two-tap whose armed state swaps innerHTML). Pins: each class list exists exactly twice in source (On-add + Off-remove; inside caPillPaint). **F-08:** dead CSS removed (`.ca-rc-row.is-ctx` ×2, `.ca-arch-dot-hollow`) plus the VACUOUS pin at earnings-archive:915 (it queried a class no emitter ever produces — could never fail; the "projected row" concept has zero references left in the app). Pin: `is-ctx|dot-hollow` gone from source. **F-07 (rain-check, recorded):** the ~830-line airport/timezone region is the file's one formatting anomaly (26% on the 8-space ladder vs 100% everywhere else) — deliberately NOT normalised here; the owner wants a standalone, explicitly-labelled, tests-untouched formatting commit in a quiet round. **Tests:** offline-menus 68→75 (+5 biting source pins, +2 guard-behavior pins), dev-ui 502→506 (+4), earnings-archive 291→290 (vacuous pin removed), smoke 60→61 (computed-color pin). **Bite proof:** `git stash push -- index.html` → offline-menus 70 passed/5 failed, dev-ui 502/4 (every new pin failed pre-fix), pre-fix smoke 59/2 (the color pin FAILED + the recorded toggle flake). **Battery 13/13 = 2,131 asserts, 0 failed.** **Smoke ×3 (app.html rebuilt, 1,061,122 bytes): 60 passed/1 failed each run — the only failure is the RECORDED task-2 toggle flake** (failed 3/3 this session AND in the pre-fix bite run; pre-existing test-side, not fixed without owner approval). **Environment:** git drift #12 between rounds (branch pointer reset to 14409f3 again; fetch+reset recipe) and the usual restore casualties (tests/node_modules, tests/smoke/node_modules, tests/smoke/app.html — reinstalled and rebuilt before use).

**v1.43.0 round 21 (owner-approved, 2026-10-11 — README-only de-drift: the README stops carrying volatile literals; docs/README.md + tests/offline-menus.test.js; no app code, no version bump, no sw bump, no changelog pointer):** the old README had drifted in three independent places at once (it claimed "currently 1.40.0" in two spots while APP_VERSION was 1.43.0, named the `crewassist-v211` cache, and stated suite counts + a "~25 s" jsdom runtime that is actually ~10 min) — fixing the numbers would just re-drift on the next bump, so the owner approved REMOVAL plus a drift-guard instead. **v1.43.0 round 19 (owner-authored sw.js review, approved after agent validation; 2026-10-10 — six zero-behaviour-change cleanups + the file's first behavioural test coverage; no version bump, no sw bump, no changelog pointer):** sw.js is 119 lines with zero behavioural coverage (only three cache-name string pins: menu-extraction:185, offline-menus:~203, smoke:86) — so every change below is pinned by the new suite, not just by reading. **(1)** the two byte-identical "clone, open, put" blocks (network-first branch + runtime backfill) became one `cachePut(req, res)` helper — pure clone+open+put, deliberately FIRE-AND-FORGET (the put promise is never returned, so respondWith never waits on the cache and a failed put can never break a page load — do not "fix" this) — with the differing guards (`res.ok` vs `res.ok || res.type === 'opaque' && runtimeCacheable`) left at the call sites; `caches.open(CACHE_NAME)` now appears exactly twice (install + the helper). **(2)** activate's `keys.map` with implicit-undefined returns rewritten as `keys.filter((k) => k !== CACHE_NAME).map((k) => caches.delete(k))` — identical semantics (Promise.all tolerates undefined; filter states intent); the `clients.claim()` tail unchanged. **(3)** `const isNetworkFirst = isAppShell || isRates` names the strategy once — the two flags survive because they select different offline fallbacks (rates → synthetic 503 '{}'; shell → cached ./index.html). **(4)** `RUNTIME_CACHE_HOSTS` moved from between activate and fetch into the constants block; `CDN_RETRIES` stays colocated with its only consumer `addWithRetry` (owner's call, agent recommendation). **(5)** the three version-stamped comments (v1.39.0 hotfix 18, two v1.36.0) rewritten as present-tense rationale — the WHY is preserved (why Tailwind is absent from CDN_ASSETS, why retries exist, what backfill protects), the history stays in git/HANDOVER; no comment count change. **(6)** the 204-char Google Fonts URL split by family into a joined `FONTS_CSS_URL` const — verified byte-identical to the old string AND to index.html's stylesheet href. **Tests: new `tests/sw.test.js` (32 pins) — a vm harness that runs the REAL sw.js with stubbed `caches`/`fetch`/`Response`/`self` (fake CacheStorage normalises keys to absolute URLs like the real one; URL must be passed in — vm contexts get ECMAScript intrinsics only, host APIs like URL/setTimeout are NOT provided) and drives the captured install/activate/fetch handlers: source shape (cache name v211, stamps gone, one cachePut, filter+map, the alias), install precache + 4 individual CDN fetches + skipWaiting, CDN retry (2 fails → 3rd lands, cached), retry exhaustion swallowed (install resolves, 3 attempts, not cached), activate deletes stale caches and claims, network-first navigation answered + cached, offline navigation → cached copy, uncached offline path → ./index.html fallback, offline rates → 503 '{}' json, cache-first hit without network, same-origin backfill, opaque backfill, failing-basic not cached, off-list origin not cached, non-GET not intercepted — PLUS the cross-file identity pins: the URLs the SW tries to cache must equal index.html's stylesheet href, unpkg script src and both ROSTER_PDFJS_* constants (the deliberate sw.js↔index.html duplication is now a guarded invariant).** Suite battery now 13 (sw joins run-all): **13/13 = 2,110 asserts, 0 failed.** **Smoke: 58/60 then 59/60** — the two failures are BOTH test-side and neither is sw.js-related: the recorded toggle-pin flake, and (new, diagnosed this round) the round-18 fv tap pin races the typing queue — see TEST-MAINTENANCE TASKS #3. **Flagged, NOT changed (owner ruling: behaviour change):** `unpkg.com/lucide@latest` is unpinned while pdf.js is pinned 3.11.174 — a precached @latest can drift from a fresh install and a breaking lucide major would kill every icon call (the app has seven unguarded bare createIcons plus refreshIcons); pinning later means coordinated sw.js CDN_ASSETS + index.html script tag + CACHE_NAME bump. index.html also carries two cdnjs URLs NOT precached (pdf.js standard_fonts/, html2canvas 1.4.1) — they ride the runtime backfill by design. **Environment notes:** the workspace restore wiped tests/node_modules and tests/smoke/node_modules (npm install in both, drift-#8 recipe); **git drift #10** mid-round (branch pointer reset to ancient 14409f3 'v1.5.9'; working tree untouched; recovered with fetch + reset to origin tip, the standing recipe).

**v1.43.0 round 18, improvement #5 (owner-approved, 2026-10-10 — the five flight-verification inline onclicks become data-attributes served by ONE delegated document listener; zero behaviour change; no version bump, no sw bump, no changelog pointer):** the fv card's Today / Tomorrow / Pick-a-date pills and Print / Full-menu submits carried inline `onclick` attributes; they now carry `data-fv-act` (today|tomorrow|pick|print|menu) + `data-fv-id` (the card uid) + `data-fv-date` (the literal date string, today/tomorrow pills only), handled by a single document-level click listener declared between `renderFlightVerificationCard` and `showDatePickerUI` in block 3: `e.target.closest('[data-fv-act]')` (nested icons safe), return when no match or no `data-fv-id` (foreign elements skipped), a whitelist switch — today/tomorrow → `setDateAndFetch(id, date, act)`, pick → `showDatePickerUI(id)`, print/menu → `submitFlightVerification(id, act)` — one action per click, everything else ignored. All five functions' names/signatures/bodies, IDs, classes, keys, date math and UI flow are untouched; the date input's `onchange` and all other out-of-scope inline handlers are unchanged. **Harness lesson:** the jsdom rig boots `runScripts:'outside-only'`, which never compiles ATTRIBUTE handlers — real `.click()` on an inline-onclick element is a no-op in tests; only true listeners (like the new delegate, or the harness's own `w.eval`) run. The new menu-extraction block therefore simulates the out-of-scope cabin toggle's effect directly (`classList.add('selected-cabin', …)` + `checkCabinsSelected(uid)`) before testing the delegated submit path, and the smoke's real-Chromium tap covers genuine end-to-end attribute → delegation (browsers DO compile inline handlers; there they'd also have worked, this change is about one binding paradigm for the card). **Tests:** menu-extraction grows a 16-pin fv block (source shape — 5 `data-fv-act` sites, 0 leftover inline fv onclicks, exactly 1 delegated registration; card render + uid; tomorrow/today real clicks stamp the hidden date input and swap the selected pill; pick resets the pill label and opens `#cadate-backdrop`; whitelist guards — a synthetic no-id button and a bogus-act button are both ignored, date unchanged; offline saved-schedule render with 2 cabin buttons; cabin selection arms the actions section; nested-icon submit click through delegation → the queued 'Please select at least one cabin.' bot message; the armed Menu submit dispatches the fetch and the count increments exactly once) — 64 total; smoke grows 1 pin (real finger tap on the Today pill in Chromium → hidden input = todayLocalYMD(), pill selected; the card render rides a try/catch because its tail carries one of the seven pre-existing BARE `lucide.createIcons()` calls, which throws in the CDN-blocked browser AFTER the card mounts — known, out of scope, still on the audit list) — 60/60 first try; full battery 12/12 = 2,078 asserts, 0 failed (dev-ui 494, next-flight 143, roster-calendar 299, earnings-archive 291, roster-real 338, roster-pdf 191, tour 130, menu-extraction 64, offline-menus 65, exports 26, dev-rates 25, trip-bar 12); zero page errors, zero console errors. **jsdom queue note (test-side):** `addBotMessage` rides the typing queue — the fv pins poll up to 6s for bot text instead of fixed waits (two pins raced and failed at fixed 200–1200ms before the polling fix).

**v1.43.0 round 18, improvement #4 (owner-approved, 2026-10-10 — merge the two .cadate-day declarations; zero computed-style change; no version bump, no sw bump, no changelog pointer):** the date-picker day button had TWO same-specificity (0,1,0) standalone rules — the transition-only rule and the layout/typography rule — with DISJOINT property sets and nothing .cadate-day-matching between them (only #cadate-hairline*, the legend-swatch, @keyframes ca-spin and #cadate-panel rules; a brace-depth scan confirmed every .cadate-day rule sits at depth 0, no @media anywhere). The full selector map: earlier is-out rules (higher specificity, other properties), later html.dark / .is-sel / .is-today state rules (higher specificity), the legend swatches' ID-qualified overrides (order-independent), and the JS builds day buttons with pure cadate-day + state classes, no inline styles. The transition line folded into the base rule as its first property; the standalone transition rule deleted (−3/+1). **Tests:** dev-ui grows an 8-pin block (six jsdom computed pins on a mounted .cadate-day — width/height/radius/font-size/font-weight/display, since jsdom cannot expand the transition shorthand — plus two source pins: exactly one line-anchored standalone rule remains, and the merged rule carries transition + layout together) — 494 total; full battery 12/12 = 2,062 asserts, 0 failed (next-flight 143, roster-calendar 299, earnings-archive 291, roster-real 338, roster-pdf 191, tour 130, menu-extraction 48, offline-menus 65, exports 26, dev-rates 25, trip-bar 12); smoke 59/59 first try, zero page errors, zero console errors (app.html rebuilt per the artifact workflow before the run — it is gitignored, regenerated by tests/smoke/build-app.js). **Drift note:** the sandbox reset the local branch pointer to the ancient baseline mid-round AGAIN (third occurrence) — working tree unaffected (all suites ran against the correct files); recovered by fetch + reset to the remote tip before the diff review, exactly the round-17 recipe. Improvement #5 (flight-verification inline onclick → data-attribute delegation) awaits the owner's go.
**v1.42.0 (owner orders 2026-10-08 round 6 — the welcome-screen restore + Settings alignment; sw v200):** **(1) The feature — restore straight from onboarding** (owner idea; agent refined): the welcome screen's CTA row is now `Let's Go` (flex-1) + a square gold-outline icon button `#ob-restore` (lucide `log-in`, `aria-label` "Restore from a backup", ~56px, gold border/icon on the light cream form and the dark navy one alike — same classes as the Data Management gold pills, theme-safe by construction). The hidden file input `#ob-restore-file` overlays the button (the Settings-door pattern; accept `.json,.txt`). Rationale: onboarding is gated by the profile itself (`initUI`: name+gender+rank → `showMain`), so a full backup restores the profile and the post-apply `location.reload()` boots STRAIGHT PAST onboarding — zero special cases, the new-phone journey is install → tap the door → pick file → confirm. `backupRestoreFile(file, opts)`/`backupRestoreRead(name, text, opts)` grew an opts param: `{fromOnboarding:true}` (only the welcome door passes it) makes legacy earnings/rates exports REDIRECT with an honest alert ("carries pay data, not your profile — finish setting up, then Settings → Data Management") instead of importing — a partial file cannot complete onboarding. Graceful degradation: a backup whose profile is incomplete leaves the gate showing onboarding with all other data restored (free behavior, no code). The Settings door is untouched. **(2) The hotfix batch — Settings alignment** (owner orders, labeled fix per instruction): profile **Edit**, **Replay**, **View** and the **Telegram** anchor all run `w-32` (one standard width, the widest label "Telegram" sets it); Data Management's buttons moved inside the house box (`glass-panel rounded-2xl p-4`) so they run the same width as Storage's Clear saved menus; **Reset rates to defaults** (`#btn-dev-reset`) moved from Data Management into the Developer card directly below **Save rates** (still `hidden` off-devMode; the 10-tap toggle still works); the dev buttons wear the Data Management uniform — Save rates lost its h-12 gold-gradient hero for the h-[42px] gold-outline pill (`.dev-save` class kept: the `is-saved` green state and `[disabled]` CSS still bind); What's New row subtitle "New features, fixes, and improvements" → "Features, fixes and improvements"; the feature-tour row subtitle "See everything the app can do in 90 seconds" → **"Placeholder"** (owner: feature-tour mini-overhaul coming later — per the owner's labeling order this swap rides UNLABELED in the changelog; the overhaul gets its pointer when it ships). **Changelog labeling decision (recorded):** the owner said "label all above as hotfixes except for the feature tour portion" — read as: the Settings edits are the hotfixes; the restore button stays the round's one `new` (it was greenlit as the 1.42.0 feature minutes earlier); v1.42.0 = minor bump (new feature), cache v200. Entry (2026-10-08): new "New phone? Restore your backup straight from the welcome screen."; fix "Settings buttons align to one width per section."; fix "Reset rates moves below Save rates in Developer." **Tests:** dev-ui 427 (welcome-door anatomy pins: aria-label, log-in icon, overlaying input, accept list, flex-1 CTA, door tappable while the form is empty; dev uniform + Save/Reset/Export order; Data Mgmt house box + new order pin; w-32 pins ×4; Placeholder + new WN subtitle), earnings-archive 289 (welcome-door block: both redirect alerts + no-op asserts, full backup through the door applies wholesale, profile comes home), smoke 31 (the journey: fresh-boot flag `__smokeFreshBoot` skips the evaluateOnNewDocument seed on reloads → profile-less boot shows the welcome screen → door preview → OK → reload lands in main view with archive+profile+dutyDays intact → partial-export refusal). Suite 12/12. New-phone checklist, final form: **old phone** Settings → Data Management → Export backup (private — pay data inside); **new phone** install PWA → tap the log-in door on the welcome screen → pick the file → confirm → the app reloads past onboarding with everything; check calendar months, earnings totals, rates, upcoming.

**v1.41.0 (owner orders 2026-10-08 round 5 — the one-shot backup; sw v199):** **the problem:** every export was piecemeal (earnings JSON/CSV, dev rates JSON) while the roster calendar's duty data, upcoming flights, profile and preferences had NO off-device path at all — a device reset meant re-uploading every roster PDF, and a new phone meant rebuilding by hand. **(1) Export backup** (Settings → Data Management, gold pill, `#btn-backup-export`): one file `crewassist-backup-YYYYMMDD.json` — manifest `{v:1, app:'CrewAssist', exportedAt, counts:{earnings,dutyMonths}}` + `data` holding the twelve crew keys (`profile, prefs, theme, calcUi, rates, ratesUpdatedAt, archive, dutyDays, allFlights, upcoming, recentFlights, manualCards` — via `BACKUP_KEYS`). Menu caches and one-time flags are deliberately excluded (caches re-fetch; flags are per-device). It is the only thing that stamps `crewAssist.lastBackupAt` / silences the v1.27.0 backup nudge — the nudge copy now says “Your data lives only on this phone” and its Export button runs the FULL backup. **(2) Restore backup** (`#btn-backup-restore`, the ONE import door, file input inside the button, accepts .json/.txt): auto-detects the format — a v1.41.0 full backup (confirm shows date + earnings count + duty months + profile name, then `applyFullBackupData` REPLACES the twelve keys wholesale and reloads: a key absent from the backup is cleared, device flags untouched), a legacy earnings export (array or `{entries:[…]}` — the old validate-preview-merge machinery relocated into `restoreEarningsJson`, dup skip + 6s undo toast, honest nothing-new alert when fully duplicate), or a legacy rates export (`restoreRatesJson` — confirm, then applies to `crewAssist.rates` + engine + editor at once; note this is a RESTORE action, it applies directly, unlike the old dev import's fill-then-review). **(3) Removals:** the earnings header's import arrow (`#ca-arch-import`), the dev footer's Import button (`#btn-dev-import`/`dev-file-import` — Export is now the single full-width secondary), `archImport`/`archImportRead`/`importDevRatesFile` (folded into the door). The earnings export stays as a scoped EXTRACT: subtitle now points at Settings for the device backup, JSON button reads “JSON - pay data”, and it no longer stamps the backup clock. The dev rates Export stays untouched (it is the rates.json publishing path). Tests: dev-ui block 1 rewritten (backup door anatomy + order), dev-rates import block repurposed to the door (cancel-safe + apply), exports' .txt-accept pin repointed, earnings-archive gained the full round-trip block (manifest counts, key wipe + restore incl. dutyDays — “the calendar survives a reset”, replace semantics, all three file formats through the door, dup alert, cancel safety, header anatomy) and its nudge block re-pinned (extract doesn't stamp; full backup does) — 282 asserts; smoke pins the stamps + a real-browser build/wipe/restore round-trip. Changelog (2026-10-08): new “One backup file now carries everything — Settings → Data Management.”; imp “Restore reads old earnings and rates files too.”; imp “The backup reminder asks for the full backup.” **Also:** the sandbox drifted a third time mid-round (same signature; both node_modules wiped) — fetch-and-reset recovery, no loss. Harness lesson recorded: top-level `const`/`let` in eval'd scripts are per-eval closures — pass OBJECTS to eval'd functions with a single `JSON.stringify`, never `JSON.stringify(JSON.stringify(x))` (that passes the JSON *text* and `Object.keys` walks characters).

**v1.40.1 polish round (owner orders 2026-10-07 round 4; version bump 1.40.0 → 1.40.1 as an owner-ordered patch, sw v198):** **(1) Paxing flights borrow the Singapore leg’s flight time.** `ifaPaxPartnerHours(id, i)` (top-level, beside `ifaRankKeyForDate`): a paxing sector with no flight time of its own — the roster never prints one for PU/TVL positioning sectors — uses the same time as its paired Singapore leg, the flight from Singapore or the flight back to Singapore, whichever carries a time; an own typed/fetched time always wins. Wired at the two choke points: the `computeCardResults` sector loop (an empty paxing sector borrows instead of being skipped at $0) and the Calculate arm gate (the borrow arms the card — no more Fetch-first wait; the v1.32.1 “card honestly waits” behavior is superseded). The Paxing toggle only exists on two-sector cards, so the partner is the other leg; if that is empty too the honest missing-time note still fires. The roster confirm note now reads “…the Singapore leg’s time is used, or Fetch fills the exact flight.” LOGIC.md §1.4 documents the rule. **(2) Onboarding polish:** the first-solo label reads **Date of First Solo** (title case) and the field rhythm is tighter (`space-y-6` → `space-y-4` on the onboarding form). Changelog (2026-10-07): fix “Paxing flights borrow their Singapore leg’s flight time.”; imp “Date of First Solo reads in title case.”; imp “Onboarding fields sit closer together.” Tests: roster-pdf’s positioning block rewritten for the borrow (card arms without Fetch, borrowed 11h35m prices at 0.75×, a typed own time outranks the borrow, the confirm note pinned); dev-ui pins the title-case label and the tightened field rhythm; the version pins re-stamped 1.40.1/v198 (smoke changelog pin now reads the 1.40.1 entry, fix+imp+imp). **Infrastructure note:** the platform’s workspace connection went down for over an hour mid-round (503 on every tool call — nothing could be read or written; the owner nudged twice before it recovered), and the sandbox had drifted again on recovery (re-clone onto the stale base, both `node_modules` wiped) — recovered by the documented fetch-and-reset protocol, both rigs reinstalled. **Two test-rig faults surfaced and were fixed mid-round:** (1) `_harness.js` stubbed `navigator.serviceWorker` as a value-undefined property — that made `'serviceWorker' in navigator` TRUE while the read gave undefined, so the app's guarded SW registration threw a TypeError inside DOMContentLoaded; jsdom rethrew it flakily and killed whole suites (dev-ui crashed in one run-all, passed standalone — the long-standing “register” stack noise finally explained). The stub is now simply ABSENT (what jsdom 24 really provides) — zero noise since. (2) next-flight’s v1.38.0 in-air block seeded times relative to the REAL clock; at 00:34 SGT the “-60 min” seed wrapped past midnight and read as a future departure (2 fails). The block now pins the harness clock (`now: '2026-10-07T14:32:00+08:00'`, seeds from the same base) — the date-stability rule extended to clock-RELATIVE seeds, documented in tests/README.

**Onboarding overhaul + copy clean (owner orders 2026-10-07 round 3; APP_VERSION frozen at 1.40.0 per the no-bump ruling, sw v197; three pointers appended to the frozen 1.40.0 changelog entry):** **(1) The LMA turnaround note is now honest and clause-free.** The combined-summary banner, the single-card overlay banner and the flight-overview narrative tail all read exactly **"LMA is not eligible for Turnaround flights."** — the "unless an overseas transit spans 3 hours or more (Clause 36(4))" tail is gone from display copy (the three-hours-or-more rule itself still applies and still explains itself in the overview text; roster-pdf:358 and the smoke's "three hours or more" pin still hold). A one-time sweep confirmed **zero Clause references in any user-visible app string** — the ten remaining hits are code comments, which stay (owner: current-state clean, not a standing rule). **(2) The onboarding mini-overhaul (owner spec, shipped inside the 1.40.0 hotfix):** a **date-of-first-solo field** sits between Name and Gender — masked DD/MM/YYYY (typing `11022024` renders `11/02/2024`), `inputmode="numeric"` for the phone keypad, calendar icon, and only a REAL calendar date validates (`31/02/2024` refuses). **Rank reveals only after name + valid date + gender** (`checkObReveal()` — editing any of the three away folds it back and clears the pick); **rank pills are gender-filtered with no junior pills** (M: FS/LS/CS/IFM, F: FSS/LSS/CSS/IFM) — the junior tier is never a picked rank anymore. `rates.json` and the dev editor still carry all tiers, unchanged. **(3) The junior tier is derived, not picked (FS/FSS only):** `ifaRankKeyForDate(ymd)` (top-level, next to `computeCardResults`) returns `'Jr. FS'`/`'Jr. FSS'` while the trip's flight date sits **within 24 months of `appProfile.firstSoloYMD`** — decided per card from sector 1's date, so every flight-date source (manual, fetched, roster upload) gets it for free at the single rate lookup in `computeCardResults`. Boundary: strictly-before-cutoff = junior; the anniversary day itself prices full. LS/LSS/CS/CSS/IFM never tier. **Legacy safety:** a profile without a solo date (pre-hotfix install) keeps its stored rank — full rate, zero behavior change — and a legacy picked `'Jr. FS'`/`'Jr. FSS'` rank still resolves through `baseRates` (and re-onboarding normalises the pill to the base rank). Profile gains `firstSoloYMD` (stored 'YYYY-MM-DD'; onboarding converts to/from the masked display). Tests: tour's two onboarding completions type a 2020 solo date (full rate — the demo's $360.72 pins survive); dev-ui gained a full behavioral block (mask, reveal ladder, impossible-date refusal, gender-filtered pill lists, submit gating, stored YMD, `ifaRankKeyForDate` boundary + legacy cases, an end-to-end $10/$13.50 card pair); smoke gained the four unit pins plus a real-browser junior card (`× $10 ×`) with its changelog pin re-counted to six pointers. LOGIC.md §3.1 now documents the date-driven tier. Changelog pointers: new "Onboarding asks your date of first solo, masked DD/MM/YYYY." / imp "FS/FSS pay the junior tier for two years from first solo." / imp "Ranks appear only after name, date and gender are set."

**Backend hygiene round (owner orders 2026-10-07; no version bump — still 1.40.0, sw v196; nothing in the changelog/What's New):** **(1) The Agreement is in the repo.** The owner uploaded `docs/CA_058_of_2025_Singapore_Airlines_Staff_Members_Agreement.pdf` to main (the full 2025–2027 Staff Collective Agreement, 456 KB; **only Clauses 34–6 concern this app**). `docs/LOGIC.md` §0 and the file map now point at it. **(2) Markdown cleanup:** 41 literal `\uXXXX` escape sequences (’ “ ” → – · ≤) decoded to real characters in this file and `tests/README.md` — they had rendered as garbage on GitHub; this file's header stamp was two bumps behind (v194 → v196); README's two stamps v195 → v196; the localStorage-keys table gained its nine missing rows (`archive`, `dutyDays`, `allFlights`, `manualEdits`/`manualCards`/`nudgedCalcUi`/`nudgedRoster`, `installHintDone`, `ratesUpdatedAt`) — and zero stale rows existed: every documented key was verified in use in `index.html`. **(3) index.html dead-code sweep:** a whole-file reference scan of all 3,202 top-level declarations (functions + const/let/var, cross-checked against `index.html` + `sw.js` + every test + the smoke rig; dynamic-call hazards ruled out — no `window[…]` indirection, no eval) found exactly **two dead functions**: `rcKindLookup` (superseded by `rcDayLookup`) and `nfInAir` (v1.33.1's in-air leg state, superseded by the v1.38.0 duty logic). Both removed; the re-scan reached fixpoint at zero — the codebase is tight, nothing else was stale. `sw.js` CACHE_NAME → v196 per the standing no-bump ruling (APP_VERSION frozen at 1.40.0; the pins in menu-extraction/offline-menus/smoke updated). **Also this round:** the sandbox drifted AGAIN mid-round (silent re-clone onto the stale base; `tests/node_modules`, the smoke rig's `node_modules` and `/tmp` wiped) — recovered by the documented protocol (fetch the branch ref → `git reset --mixed`); two `npm install`s were the whole cost, which is exactly what the permanent rig home was for. Verification: suite 12/12; browser smoke 19/19.

**Repo reorganization (owner orders 2026-10-07; no version bump — still 1.40.0, sw v195; backend only, nothing in the changelog/What's New):** the repo root is the PWA's web root, so the app spine stays put (`index.html`, `sw.js`, `manifest.json`, `rates.json`, `tw.css` + `tw.config.cjs`, `icons/`, `api/`) — everything written moved into **`docs/`**: `LOGIC.md`, `HANDOVER.md`, `API_REFERENCE.md`, and `TOUR-BREAKDOWN.md` (committed for the first time — it was referenced by README + this file but never tracked). **`_screenshots/` is deleted with all content** (the four bug-report jpgs and the Agreement PDF; the pending workspace-vs-committed `document.pdf` diff is moot) — the owner will upload the full collective agreement separately as `CA_058_of_2025_Singapore_Airlines_Staff_Members_Agreement.pdf`. **`_inbox/` became `docs/_reference_rosters/` and is now committed** (35 owner PDFs + `menu/sq_main_courses.json`): an explicit owner order that overrides the old purge-after-sign-off privacy protocol — a fresh clone now runs the full suite, because `roster-real`/`roster-calendar` read those PDFs directly (test paths updated, as was the parser comment in `index.html` — comment-only, hence no cache bump). README refreshed for accuracy ahead of the owner's planned overhaul: the v1.24.0/v131-era stamps → 1.40.0/v195, Tailwind described as the compiled stylesheet (not CDN), offline menus marked built, the What's New mechanism corrected to `APP_CHANGELOG` + `crewAssist.wnSeen`, the file tree and every doc link repointed at `docs/`, and the mega-bullets tightened. `.gitignore` lost the `_screenshots/` rule. **Flagged to the owner:** the screenshot drop zone's removal takes the old screenshot-reporting channel with it (owner uploads via GitHub web UI → OCR) — bug reports now arrive as chat text until a new channel is agreed. Verification: suite 12/12 against the new fixture path; browser smoke 19/19.

**v1.40.0 hotfix (owner orders, no version bump — still 1.40.0, sw v195; nothing added to the changelog/What's New per the owner's explicit order, which overrides the hotfixes-get-pointers ruling for this round only):** **(1) #3 ruled:** paxing stays the app's flat 0.75× — the owner reviewed the Clause 34(5) difference (official = 0.75-credit then the ladder; ours = flat 75%) and ruled no change. LOGIC.md §1.4 now records the ruling where the pending-decision note stood. **(2) LOGIC.md §5 (CALCULATION PIPELINE / SEQUENCE) removed** on the owner's order; §6 Edge Cases and §7 Verification renumbered to §5/§6. **(3) The What's New duplicate version removed:** the sheet showed "v1.40.0" twice — the white `text-sm` title line (`#whatsnew-title`, which also carried the vX → vY range on multi-version jumps) above the gold list header that `renderChangelogInto` renders per entry. The white line is gone (element removed, `aria-labelledby` dropped, the dead title code in `maybeWhatsNewOverlay` deleted); the gold per-version headers keep multi-jump sheets grouped. **(4) The smoke rig now lives inside the repo at `tests/smoke/`** (owner orders 6+7: everything build-critical that a workspace restore keeps wiping goes into the test folder): `build-app.js` / `server.js` / `smoke.js` / `run.js` + `package.json` (puppeteer-core, @sparticuz/chromium, and the `tailwindcss` CLI the tw.css rebuild recipe needs — previously the CLI rode the rig's wipeable node_modules). `run.js` self-heals `/tmp/chr-libs` (brotli-extract of the packed al2023 libs) so a restore costs one `npm install`, not a rebuild. The old `/home/user/smoke` location is deleted. What still gets wiped and cannot be vendored: `node_modules` anywhere (snapshot rule) — `tests/` and `tests/smoke/` both recover with `npm install` (~10s + ~1min). Verification: suite 12/12; browser smoke 19/19 from the new home (new check: the version appears exactly once in the What's New sheet).

**v1.40.0 (owner greenlight: the transit-meals release — the rules verified against the Staff Members' Agreement; sw v194):** the round the calculation rules were checked line-by-line against the owner's Agreement upload (Clauses 34–36, ASCA 2025, CA No.058/2025) and the app changed to match. **(1) Transits of three hours or more earn location meals (Clause 36(4)).** The LMA block no longer refuses Turnarounds: a same-day slip station whose span from scheduled arrival to REPORT time (departure − 60 min, Clause 36(3)) is ≥180 minutes earns the meal windows it covers — inclusive at both ends. `suggestShuttle` pre-ticks only under-3h transits; the roster prefill fills a turnaround's transit stations (filtered to ≥3h ground); the LMA section shows on turnarounds that carry station data; the combined summary, the single-card overlay and the archive reopen all render the breakdown; the archive amount is always the full COP (IFA + LMA). **(2) Report time closes the day (Clause 36(3)).** Every departure-side window comparison now uses `reportMins = depMins − 60` — same-day and multi-day departure days alike; arrival-day logic unchanged; displays keep actual departure times. **(3) The phantom-station fix (caught by the tour suite mid-round):** the roster import's station builder merged a double shuttle's two stays into one first-in-last-out record (PEN 1102→1809) — a 7-hour "presence" counting the SIN out-and-back in the middle which, under the new gate, bought a lunch the crew never sat through (and could leak one into layover-typed double shuttles under the old code too). Stations are now per-VISIT. Related: an untouched LMA station row (default dates, no code, no times) is silently skipped — only a PARTIALLY filled row raises the red flag — and the plain-turnaround note reads "…unless an overseas transit spans 3 hours or more (Clause 36(4))." **(4) LOGIC.md rewritten** around the Agreement: §0 the official basis (34(7) definitions; the exclusions table — 34(2)(c) delay, 34(3) 85h, 34(4) deadheading, 34(5) compassionate, 34(6) diversions; Jr tier ≤24 months since first solo; buffers ≈ 34(7)(c); rates renegotiated every January), §1.4 the paxing deviation (the app's 0.75 flat multiplier vs the Agreement's 0.75-credit + ladder — the open #3 question, awaiting the owner), §1.8–1.10 the report-time/3h-gate/multi-day rules, §3.6 rates wef 01 Jan 2026, §4.6 the always-full-COP archive; UI-only sections removed per the owner's order. **Rig loss + rebuild (disclosed):** the 2026-10-07 workspace restore wiped `/home/user/smoke` (the 89-check rig), `tests/node_modules` and all `/tmp` tooling. The jsdom suite was restored with `npm install` (12/12, 1,787 asserts; the version pins in menu-extraction/offline-menus stamped 1.40.0/v194) and the browser rig was REBUILT compact at `/home/user/smoke` (build-app.js / server.js / run.js / smoke.js; 18 checks: boot health, shell guard, stamps, the What's New sheet, the inclusive 180-minute KUL boundary earning the $55.00 Southeast Asia lunch, the 179-minute refusal, the 19:31/19:29 report-time dinner boundary, zero page/console errors) — the old 89 checks were not recreated one-for-one; the motion/foldable coverage (hotfixes 15–19) survives only in the jsdom pins. **Open:** #3 (paxing 0.75× vs the Agreement's credit+ladder) awaits the owner's decision, and `_screenshots/document.pdf` differs from the committed copy after the restore (300,883 vs 313,812 B) — left uncommitted for the owner to rule on. Changelog (2026-10-07): new "Transits of three hours or more now earn location meals."; imp "Meal windows now close at report time, an hour before departure."; imp "The rules are verified line-by-line against the Staff Members' Agreement."

**v1.39.0 hotfix 19 (two owner reports; sw v193):** **(1) "Why is there a weird white pill with gold border at the bottom of the screen?" — a hotfix-18 regression, found and fixed in one hop.** The archive toast (`.ca-arch-toast`: a fixed bottom pill, cream in dark mode, always gold-bordered) toggles visibility with the Tailwind `hidden` class while its own CSS declares `display:flex` — a specificity TIE that the Play CDN always broke in `hidden`'s favor, because the runtime compiler injected its styles at the END of `<head>`, after the app's inline block. Hotfix 18 replaced the CDN with the compiled `tw.css` via a `<link>` placed BEFORE the inline styles — flipping every such tie. The toast (and `ca-roster-sticky`, hidden-toggled the same way) rendered permanently: an empty cream pill with a gold border at the bottom. **Fix: the `<link>` now sits AFTER the inline `</style>` — exact CDN-era cascade parity (utilities win ties, always).** Pinned three ways: a dev-ui source-order pin (`link` index > `</style>` index), a smoke check (`ca-arch-toast` computed `display: none` at rest), and a comment block at the link itself. **The general lesson is recorded in the link's comment: any future stylesheet must load AFTER the inline block, or every hidden-toggled element with an app `display` class sticks on screen.** **(2) "When the user taps a flight — in the calendar or the earnings page — make the summary open below the chat header, similar to all overlays."** Both taps already routed to the right content (`rosterCalOpenEarnings` files lookups across every date a duty touches, with live-pricing fallback; `openArchEntry` renders the saved summary) — the ask was presentation: the sub-sheet was a bottom-anchored partial sheet (`max-h-[calc(100vh-120px)]`, no handle, no close button, shade at /50). It now wears the full overlay chrome, identical to Settings / Earnings / Changelog: starts below the chat header (`top-[calc(60px+env(safe-area-inset-top,20px))]`, rounded-t-3xl, glass-sheet), grab handle, a title row (route + date · type subtitle) with its own close button, and a scrolling body (`ca-arch-sub-body`) — same shared glide, focus trap, shade-tap close and Escape handling. `openArchSub(html, title, subtitle)` carries the title line; the entry summary, the live-priced duty, and the import/export dialogs (which moved their `<h4>`s into the title row) all pass theirs. **Verification:** suite 12/12 (1,807 asserts; dev-ui 399 — new order pin + overlay-chrome pins; earnings-archive 256 — geometry pins re-pinned to the body div), browser smoke 89/89 — new "the summary overlay (hotfix 19)" section: toast hidden at rest (the cascade regression), tapping a calendar flight card opens the summary, its resting top sits within 2px of the header's bottom edge, chrome + close button present, plus pointer 5 and the v193 stamp. **Two rig lessons this round:** (a) jsdom tests that speak month-day words ("sq802 6 oct") MUST pin the harness clock before their seeded dates — dev-ui's natural-command block crossed midnight (6 Oct 2026 passed) and the year-rollover mapped the phrase to 2027, missing the roster seed and crashing the suite; the block now boots with `now: '2026-10-05T12:00:00+08:00'`. (b) The smoke's flight-card tap picks the fly day CLOSEST to today (the timeline virtualizes — a first-in-seed-order day three weeks out is unmounted and untappable), seeds a saved archive entry for it (the instant path — the live calculator's timing belongs to the slots section), and polls the sheet's resting top (the 300ms glide must finish before geometry is judged). Changelog pointer 5: "Hotfix: the stuck gold-bordered pill at the bottom is gone, and flight summaries open below the chat header wearing every other overlay’s look."

**v1.39.0 hotfix 18 (owner follow-up after 17: "same issue persisting… holding down and moving my finger left and right in the calendar grid or up and down in the date entries, the content flickers as if there is some black box — only on the unfolded inner screen"; sw v192):** 17's four fixes were real but the symptom survived them, which meant the remaining causes were things the headless rig fundamentally could not see — and the biggest one was hiding IN the rig itself. **(1) THE APP RAN THE TAILWIND PLAY CDN IN PRODUCTION (the root-weight):** `cdn.tailwindcss.com` is a ~300KB runtime JIT compiler that keeps a MutationObserver over the whole document and recompiles + INJECTS stylesheet changes on every DOM mutation — every timeline row mounted during a finger scroll, every month clone built at drag-engage, every amount span filled mid-motion.
