Each injection invalidates matching styles across the document; on a 5-megapixel 120Hz inner screen those mid-gesture invalidations overrun frames and the compositor drops tiles — the black boxes. **The smoke rig never saw this because its offliner had been silently swapping the CDN for a compiled `/tw.css` since it was built — the rig was exempt from the app's single heaviest runtime cost.** The app now ships the compiled stylesheet (`tw.css`, 38KB, committed at repo root, precached in the SW) and the CDN script + inline config are gone. **Rebuild recipe after editing class names in index.html: `/home/user/smoke/node_modules/.bin/tailwindcss -c tw.config.cjs -o tw.css --minify && cp tw.css /home/user/smoke/tw.css` (config mirrors the old inline one; content-scans index.html, which contains every class literal the app can generate — verified by a new smoke gate that asserts every class the running app wears exists in the compiled sheet; 811 tokens, zero gaps).** The v1.36 shell guard now probes the LIVE stylesheet (a `hidden`-classed sentinel span stays invisible exactly when the sheet loaded) instead of `window.tailwind`. **(2) The always-visible chrome still carried live backdrop-filter:** hotfix 16 de-blurred the sheets but `.glass-panel` (the sticky header, the action chips, the dashboard cards) still ran a 16px blur every frame — the same compositor hazard, permanently on screen around every gesture. They now use `--glass-solid-bg` (same hue, 0.92/0.94 alpha, borders + shadow untouched). **(3) The glide track was unpromoted:** a CSS transition self-promotes only once it STARTS, and that promotion frame — layer allocation + raster mid-animation — is exactly where the big screen flashed. The glide track now carries `will-change: transform` from birth (destroyed on land, like the drag track). **(4) The drag track had zero raster head-start:** it was built AT the first move past slop. A finger that RESTS first (the owner's exact gesture) now parks the track after 140ms — the layer rasterizes while the finger is still — and the first real move only flips it live (`rcDragEngage(pre)`; a parked-never-moved hold restores instantly so plain day taps still land — no `ca-rc-drag` class, no pointer-events lockdown while parked). **(5) Per-event main-thread work:** `lucide.createIcons()` is a whole-document query and ran on EVERY window pass (now only when a pass actually mounted rows); pricing a duty builds an entire hidden calculator card and parses the archive store — that ran DURING finger scrolls (now deferred: `rosterCalScheduleEarns` prices immediately only when no scroll happened in the last 400ms — `rcTLMotion`, reset by `scrollend` — otherwise it waits for ~350ms of quiet; the churn probe shows zero calculator cards across 50 mid-scroll steps). **(6) Found via the rig while hardening: scroll events ride the display's frames — under load the LAST event can land frames behind a fast scroller, so the window can stop short of the resting position (the "bottom details not rendering" state can recur through this door — 3 TLWindow calls across a 70-step scroll, measured). The 140ms scroll-quiet debounce now runs an UNCONDITIONAL settle pass before its month-flip guards (it only reads geometry and mounts rows, so nothing may skip it), and a `scrollend` listener (where the engine provides it) is the exact rest signal.** **(7) Long-press guards:** the gesture surfaces are not text — `#ca-rc-grid`/`#ca-rc-detail` carry `user-select: none` + `-webkit-touch-callout: none` and the calendar suppresses `contextmenu`, so a hold-that-moves can never raise the selection machinery on top of the motion. **Verification:** suite 12/12 (1,821 asserts; dev-ui 395, roster-calendar 221 — new pins: exactly two transient will-changes, the compiled-stylesheet link with no CDN, the sentinel guard, the glass-panel de-blur, the hold-parks track, the settle pass, scrollend, motion-aware pricing, the selection guards). Browser smoke 83/83 across repeated runs — new "motion-frame diet" section: the header computes `backdropFilter: none` with a near-opaque surface, no `window.tailwind` exists, the stylesheet coverage gate, a 230ms hold parks the track (and the tap after it still selects), long-press-and-move raises no selection on either surface, the glide track is promoted at birth, and no calculator cards build mid-scroll. anchordebug 12/12 stops with vis=5 each (windows marching 0-19 → 59-79). **Two rig lessons recorded: programmatic-scroll checks must dispatch the scroll event at the resting position before measuring (browsers coalesce/delay scroll events for programmatic jumps under load — a real finger always fires them; the smoke's phone/foldable checks and anchordebug now do this), and `rcLegCache` (const, dkey→legs) must be key-evicted when seeding legs mid-session. The settings-shade glide check (hotfix 14 era, untouched this round) can flake under back-to-back smoke load — it passes in every spaced run.** Changelog pointer 4 reworded again (34 words): "…finishes its slide, answers quick taps and swipes, keeps its rows and text at scale, and holds steady on the foldable's inner screen as the finger moves."

**v1.39.0 hotfix 17 (owner follow-up: "same issue persisting… holding down and moving my finger left and right in the calendar grid or up and down in the date entries, the content flickers as if there is some black box — only on the unfolded inner screen"; sw v191):** the de-blur (hotfix 16) was necessary but not sufficient — this round found and fixed FOUR concrete, headless-reproducible defects the finger-gesture report pointed straight at. **(1) The stranded slide (the big one):** tapping the month arrows faster than the 380ms glide bumped the slide's generation counter, and the running glide's cleanup then aborted on the stale-generation guard WITHOUT removing `data-rc-slide` or the `overflow:hidden` clamp — **the swipe and the timeline scroll-flip stayed dead for the rest of the session** (reproduced: 5 rapid prev-taps → attr stuck, drag dead; the owner's "it changes depending where I tap"). Cleanup is now unconditional; only the commit is generation-owned. **(2) The black box in the drag:** at the roster's first/last month the drag track's void-side third was an EMPTY div, and the rubber band dragged that black slab into view while the finger swung (reproduced at 898×901: engage leftward at month 1, swing back right → empty third exposed). Both void edges now carry a clone of the current month — the resistance slides over real content. **(3) The live drag track had no compositor layer:** it transforms on EVERY pointer event; unpromoted, each move forced a full-grid repaint (the flash on the big screen — invisible on the phone's smaller canvas). It now carries `will-change: transform` and is destroyed when the drag lands (the one-shot slide tracks stay unpromoted — hotfix 15's pin now allows exactly one will-change, the drag track). **(4) The timeline window wiped itself on every crossing AND could rest uncovered:** `el.innerHTML = html` destroyed and rebuilt every row each time the ±6-row overscan was crossed (the whole visible window re-rasterized at once — black tiles on the inner screen), and because heights were corrected only AFTER the window was chosen, a resting viewport could sit outside the freshly corrected window with no further scroll event coming — **the persistent "bottom details not rendering" screenshot state** (reproduced with real-shaped legs: window [4,21] covering a viewport at 2480 → 0 visible rows at rest). The window is now DIFFED (rows mount/unmount one at a time between two stable spacers — `rcTL.diff`), derives from REAL geometry (`rosterCalTLCover` — the mounted rows' rects, extrapolating when the viewport sits outside them; jsdom's zero rects fall back to the offsets), is applied a SECOND time on corrected offsets so the viewport is never left uncovered, and height corrections ANCHOR the scroller (`scrollTop += Δoff[a]`) so corrected spacers never slide the visible rows under the eye. **Verification (all at the real inner-screen shape 898×901 @ dpr 2.4, with real-shaped multi-leg duties so card heights vary 41–267px):** rapid prev-taps → clean; the swing gesture → 31/31/30 numbers in the track's thirds, no empty third; 12 resting stops after 620px jumps → visible rows at EVERY stop (was 0 at five of twelve); gradual 30px scrolls rest covered; render times 1–3ms. **The repro-seed lesson (why four rounds of testing missed all this): synthetic duty rows are uniform, so the height estimates never drifted and the window always covered — the owner's real legs (2-leg turnarounds, 3-day patterns, standbys) make the estimates wrong by 15–45%, which is what bends the offsets and exposes every one of these defects. Real-shaped legs (`crewAssist.allFlights` month→[{fn,dep,arr,ymd,std,sta,stdYmd,staYmd,ft,ac,rpt,pos}]) are now part of the smoke's hotfix-17 section.** Suite 12/12, 1784+ asserts (dev-ui 392: the window is diffed never wiped; roster-calendar 212: the slide always cleans up, the void edges clone the month, the cover-deriver + jsdom guard, the anchor). Browser smoke 69/69, zero page errors — new section: rapid arrow taps leave no stranded slide, the drag engages with no empty third, gradual scrolls rest covered with at most rare mass-swaps (the old code wiped every crossing). Changelog pointer 4 reworded (35 words): "…finishes its slide open, answers quick taps and swipes, keeps its lines to one row and its text at scale, and paints fully on the foldable's inner screen."

**v1.39.0 hotfix 16 (owner pushed four named screenshots to the branch — `_screenshots/`: "calender_grid_not_rendering", "bottom_details_not_rendering", "calender_grid_and_bottom_details_not_rendering", "when_tapping_on_date_entry_earnings_overlay_has_nothing_rendered"; sw v190):** hotfix 15 did not cure it, and the screenshots ended the guessing. Forensic decoding (ImageMagick + a pure-python PPM analyzer — band-level edge density and mean color, column bounds): the captures are full native-resolution, 2156×~2170 device px, and the painted content column measures 1514 device px = the 632-CSS content width at **dpr 2.4 → the real inner-screen viewport is ~898×901** (a size the sweeps never hit, but geometry there is provably fine — every painted region sits exactly where the layout puts it). What varies per shot is WHICH regions paint: the grid band, the timeline's rows (in alternating ~tile-sized bands — some rows painted, their neighbours blank at a regular pitch), the entire upper sheet, or the earnings sheets' content. Sheet chrome (glass, scrim) paints; content regions drop. That is real-device COMPOSITOR failure under load, and the app was feeding it **three nested live-blur surfaces**: the full-screen `backdrop-blur-sm` shade, the `glass-sheet`'s `backdrop-filter: blur(20px)` (over a background that is **95% opaque** — the blur was invisible but still forced a full-sheet blur pass every frame), and the earnings sheets (also `glass-sheet`). **Fix:** the blur is gone from every big surface — `.glass-sheet` (all sheets: calendar, settings, changelog, menu viewer, print, results, earnings, earnings-sub), all ten full-screen shades/backdrops (now plain `bg-black/60` scrims — the smear was already invisible under 60% black), and the changelog's sticky header (0.92-opaque + sticky + blur inside a scroller is a classic Android paint-killer). The login hero keeps its blur (a different scene, no complaints); the small identity surfaces keep theirs (the app header strip, glass bubbles, the photo lightbox). Visual delta: none to the eye — the dropped blurs were all under near-opaque backgrounds. **Changelog:** folded per the standing order — pointer 4 reworded (34 words): "…keeps its lines to one row and its text at scale, and paints fully on the foldable's inner screen." **Suite:** 12/12, 1781 asserts (dev-ui 391 +3: no backdrop-filter on .glass-sheet, exactly one backdrop-blur-sm survives (the login hero), no blur on the sticky header; roster-calendar 207 +2 net: the shade is the plain scrim, no full-screen blur under the sheet — and the obsolete "same blur as Settings" pin reworded to "the same scrim"). **Browser smoke 64/64, zero page errors** — new: computed-style checks that the calendar/settings/earnings/earnings-sub sheets and the shade are blur-free in the live app; the foldable stress now includes the REAL inner-screen shape 898×901 at dpr 2.4 alongside 781×719/793×777/1066×1096; the fold+scroll window check got a settle retry (a 10-iteration fold probe runs clean — the single-shot check was a headless timing flake). **Technique bank:** owner screenshots decode with `identify` + `convert …ppm` + pure-python band/column analysis — mean color identifies WHICH surface painted (sheet 0.95-alpha navy vs app bg), edge density identifies content, and known CSS widths (the 672 column) recover the true dpr. The screenshots live in `_screenshots/` (owner's commit 6f9a2e8, on the branch — keep them).

**v1.39.0 hotfix 15 (owner report — the calendar renders improperly on the foldable's INNER screen only; fine on PC, iPhone and the cover screen; every time, and reopening doesn't clear it; "sometimes the month grid, sometimes the list below, it changes depending where I tap or if I scroll"; no version bump — still 1.39.0, sw v189):** the owner's answers ruled out every reproducible class: the same real-assets rig (real lucide + real fonts + dpr 3) was hammered at 781×719/793×777/1066×1096 with tap batteries across every grid column, month swipes, hard timeline scrolls, fold/unfold, jump, interleavings — the panes stay consistent and every pixel-edge-density check is healthy; a simulated Android font boost (uniform 1.15×/1.3×/1.45× inside the calendar) holds geometry at every factor. What the device pattern DOES uniquely match is **Chrome on Android's text autosizer**: it inflates text on wide mobile viewports, per-block (non-uniformly, by block width and text length) — the cover screen is too narrow to trigger it, desktop Chrome never applies it, iOS Safari never applies it. The wide inner screen was the one place the app rendered at a different text scale, and per-block inflation is exactly the kind of thing that misaligns pixel-tuned cards differently depending which day is tapped. **Fix (two parts): (1) `html { -webkit-text-size-adjust: 100%; text-size-adjust: 100%; }`** — the standard lock; every screen now draws the designed text scale (consistent with the app's existing no-zoom viewport). **(2) dropped the standing `will-change: transform`** from both month-slide tracks (200% and 300% wide) — Chrome promotes one-shot transform transitions on its own, and the flag was a free 2×-sheet-width compositor layer at dpr 3 on a ~5-megapixel screen. **Changelog:** folded per the standing order — pointer 4 reworded (35 words): "…finishes its slide open, survives a quick close-and-reopen, keeps its text size steady and each card line to one row…". **Suite:** 12/12, 1778 asserts (dev-ui 388 +2: the lock rule is present, no will-change survives anywhere; roster-calendar 205 +1: the slide track carries no standing layer). **Browser smoke rebuilt from scratch at 55/55, zero page errors** (the turn-boundary wipe took smoke.js; it was rewritten comprehensive): phone calendar anatomy (open, today's month, full grid + timeline, arrows away-and-back, tap-selection syncing both panes, scroll window clean, fold collapse/grow/expand, press-and-hold today-jump), the hotfix-14 quick close-and-reopen battery (same-tick + 120ms × 2 each: sheet stays open, full render, today's month — date-proofed; settings shade), the foldable stress trio (open/next/fold/scroll/calm-reopen + the max-w-2xl cap centered at all three sizes), the hotfix-15 checks (computed text-size-adjust = 100% live at 781×719, full calendar render there), the themed changelog pins (1.39.0 present, folded pointer 4, old wording gone), the What's-New replay (seen stays quiet; 1.38.0 replays through `maybeWhatsNewOverlay`; **merely closing never stamps seen — only the checkbox**, honoring the owner ruling), the v189 stamp, and a final phone open. NOTE for the owner report: if the inner screen still misrenders after v189, ask for a PHOTO — compositing-level corruption (backdrop-filter/tile budget) is the remaining untestable class and a screenshot identifies it instantly.

**v1.39.0 hotfix 14 (owner report — "some of the calendar contents are not rendering/displaying properly" on the foldable's inner screen; no version bump — still 1.39.0, sw v188):** the previous round's fixes were verified clean at every foldable size through every static path — so this round rebuilt the smoke app with **the REAL lucide UMD + the REAL Plus Jakarta Sans / Space Mono / Cormorant Garamond woff2s (via @fontsource) + deviceScaleFactor 3**, and the bug fell out immediately: **a quick close-and-reopen (<300ms) collapsed the calendar entirely** — grid 0px, detail 0px, and the month label reading the roster's FIRST month (Sep). Root cause: every close arms a 300ms `display:none` on its wrap/backdrop; a reopen inside that window un-hides the element, but **the stale timer fires anyway — display:none lands mid-open**. On the calendar that hid the whole wrap; under `display:none` the timeline's `scrollTop` zeroes, the browser fires the scroll listener, and the scroll-flip then "corrects" the month to the timeline's first date — so even a later reopen started on the wrong month. On the other sheets the same race only killed the shade (the sheet floated on, shadeless). Quick taps on a fast foldable sit squarely inside the 300ms window; the smoke's stepwise open/close loops always had a 600-700ms gap and never saw it. **Fix: one shared `caSheetHideArm(el, ms)` / `caSheetHideCancel(el)` pair** (tracked per-element as `el.__caHideT`): every close ARMS through the pair, every open CANCELS before un-hiding — 13 arms + 15 cancels across calendar, settings, changelog, What's New, both results sheets, archive + sub-sheet, menu viewer, print sheet, the app dialog and the date picker (the arch sub-sheet's bespoke `archSubTimer` was folded into the pair). Double-closing can't double-arm (the arm clears its own prior timer); the immediate-close variants (`closeMenuViewer(true)`) cancel-then-hide so a later open never fights a pending timer. **Verified in the real-assets browser**: same-tick `close(); open();` ×6, 120ms-gap reopens ×4, and settings same-tick ×3 — all render fully (grid 311px, detail healthy, today's month) with the shade intact; the full open/next/scroll/reopen matrix at 781×719, 793×777 and 1066×1096 @3x is clean. **Changelog:** folded per the standing order — pointer 4 now reads "…always finishes its slide open, survives a quick close-and-reopen…" (31 words, still one sentence). **Suite:** 12/12, 1775 asserts (dev-ui 386 +5: the pair exists, ≥12 cancels, ≥10 arms, no bare 300ms hide timers survive, the arm clears its prior timer; roster-calendar 204 +2: the open cancels before un-hiding, the close arms through the pair). **Browser smoke 82/82, zero page errors** — new: the quick close-and-reopen regression (same-tick and 120ms gaps at foldable size, twice each: the sheet stays open, grid + detail + all numbers render, the month is today's — date-proofed), the settings shade probe, the folded pointer-4 pin, and the v188 stamp. The real-assets app (`app-real.html` + `server-real.js` in the smoke workspace — real lucide + real fonts + dpr 3) stays the recon rig for foldable reports: stubs hide timing bugs. sw v188; the 1.39.0 pointers stay folded at 3 new + 6 fix.

**v1.39.0 hotfix 13 (owner bug report — the foldable's calendar still broken + a gold line on first launch; no version bump — still 1.39.0, sw v187):** **(1) "top calendar number sometimes not showing, bottom details sometimes not showing" on the Magic V3 inner screen.** REPRODUCED in headless Chromium at a foldable viewport (1066×1096): after a close, the next open left the sheet parked at the closed pose — `translate-y-full` removed, computed transform stuck at `translateY(1036px)`, no transition running. Root cause: every sheet hides its wrap with `display:none` 300ms after closing; when that lands before the closing glide's last composited frame the transition is CANCELED, and the next open's glide can refuse to start (the engine's transition bookkeeping for that element is wedged). A big sheet makes the race easy to hit — phones almost never do, which is why this surfaced only on the foldable; a stuck-at-any-pose sheet cuts whatever sits below the fold (sometimes the top rows, sometimes the detail). **Fix: one shared `caSheetGlideIn(sheet, shade)` now opens EVERY sheet** (calendar, settings, changelog, menu viewer, both results sheets, archive + sub-sheet, print — 9 call sites, no bare `translate-y-full` toggles left): (a) a forced reflow between un-hide and the class change pins the start pose — the same medicine hotfix 8 gave the fold and month slide; (b) heal passes at **+400ms** (restart a dead glide cleanly — transition:none, pin the closed pose, reflow, clear) and **+750ms** (snap open: a wedged engine refuses even the restart's fresh transition, so the snap forces the pose — a sheet below the fold is worse than one instant landing; the shade gets the same mercy). Heals are per-sheet (`sheet.__caHealT`) so stacked sheets never cross-cancel, and each pass stands down the moment its sheet closes. Verified twice over: the plain open/close loop at 1066×1096 lands top:60 every time (10/10), and a FORCED wedge (inline-pinned closed pose) heals to open through the snap pass. **(2) "on first launch, sometimes a colored gold line across the top of settings/calendar overlays."** CONFIRMED as the focus trap: `caSheetFocusIn` focuses the sheet itself (`tabindex=-1`), and on a first launch the onboarding's keyboard typing leaves the browser's `:focus-visible` heuristic in keyboard modality — the programmatic focus INHERITS it (spec: focus-visible carries from the previously focused element) and the full-bleed sheet drew its 2px gold outline; the sheet spans the screen, so only the outline's TOP edge was on-screen — literally a gold line across the overlay's top. Probe: fresh profile → type in the name field → `openSettings()` → sheet `matches(':focus-visible') === true`, computed outline `solid 2px rgb(201,162,39)`. **Fix:** the focus catch is now invisible — `caSheetFocusIn` tags the sheet `ca-focuscatch` and one CSS rule (`.ca-focuscatch:focus, :focus-visible { outline: none !important }`) kills the container's outline; real controls keep their outlines (the sheet is a container, not a control). **(3) Changelog: the round folds into the themed pointers per the standing order** — the slide fix joins pointer 4 ("…centers on wide screens and always finishes its slide open…"), the gold line merges the dev-mode-silence and layover one-liner pointers into one polish line ("overlays open clean on a fresh install with no stray gold line…"); 1.39.0 keeps 3 new + 6 fix pointers, nothing added. **Suite:** 12/12, 1768 asserts (dev-ui 381 +3: the glide exists, 10 call sites, the reflow-pin order, the heal timings, the invisible catch; roster-calendar 202 +1: the calendar's open routes through the shared glide). **Browser smoke 69/69, zero page errors** (~30s, twice) — new checks: a 4-cycle open/close stress on the 1066×1096 foldable viewport (sheet lands open every time, month header + every grid number on-screen, detail rows rendered with the selected row visible), a forced-keyboard-modality gold-line probe (type in chat → open settings → the focused sheet computes NO outline even while `:focus-visible` matches), the folded changelog pointers, and the v187 stamp. Lesson recorded: a smoke check must wait past the LAST heal pass (+750ms) before judging a glide — the first draft checked at +850ms against a +900ms snap and "failed" a working fix. sw v187; the 1.39.0 changelog pointers stay folded per the owner's order.

**v1.39.0 hotfix 12 (owner's five orders — the foldable's stretched calendar, one-row flight lines, resting header buttons, and the changelog's hotfix pile; no version bump — still 1.39.0, sw v186):** **(1+2) The calendar stretched edge-to-edge on the Magic V3 foldable's inner screen** (fine on the owner's PC): the sheet's data tier ran full-bleed. The content column now centers at `max-w-2xl mx-auto w-full` — the menu viewer's exact pattern. The sheet keeps its full-bleed glass, shade and drag handle; a phone never reaches the 672px cap, and a wide screen gets the calm centered column. **(3) The next-flight card's lines flowed onto two rows on narrow screens:** every text line — the date, in-air/departs, both lands lines, and the alarm's two lines — carries `whitespace-nowrap` (the fns line already truncated; the WRAPPER span stays wrappable, so the lines never fuse into one — nowrap belongs to the leaves only). **(4) While the chat types, the calendar, settings and refresh buttons stand down** with the existing busy controls: `html.chat-busy` dims them to 0.45 and cuts pointer-events. **The theme button stays live at all times (owner order)** — it appears in no chat-busy rule. CSS-only, no JS guards, exactly like the input/send/roster set above it: the tour drives these buttons programmatically and `.click()` sails past pointer-events, so the tour keeps working mid-busy; `btn-roster-cal` keeps its pre-existing JS guard. **(5) The 1.39.0 changelog entry had 19 "Hotfix:" pointers — the owner called the pile too many and authorized rewriting THIS one entry** (append-only stands for every other shipped version): now 3 new (untouched) + 7 themed one-sentence fix pointers — the calendar's becoming, its gestures, its tints, this round's trio, the filing-span fix (kept verbatim), the dev-mode silence, the layover one-liner. The per-pointer word cap widens for consolidated fix pointers only (12 → 35; feature pointers keep 12). **Suite:** 12/12, 1764 asserts (roster-calendar 201 +1: the `max-w-2xl mx-auto` literal; next-flight 109 +1: every LEAF text line carries nowrap — the first draft matched the wrapper span and failed, the lesson being textContent matches wrappers too, filter by `!sp.querySelector('span')`; dev-ui 378: +2 busy pins — the three buttons in a chat-busy rule, `#btn-theme` in none — and −36 from the consolidation itself: 12 fewer pointers × 3 per-pointer pins; the twelve-word pin is now a two-tier cap). **Browser smoke 53/53, zero page errors** (~18s, twice for stability). The smoke workspace was rebuilt from scratch again after the turn-boundary wipe, and the rebuild taught three things worth keeping: (a) `@sparticuz/chromium` must be pinned to **123.0.1** — the newer major ships a different API (`inflate`/`setupLambdaEnvironment`, no `executablePath`); (b) the app **reloads on SW `controllerchange`** — a first-visit SW install claims the page mid-run and tears down the smoke's execution context, so the smoke server now serves an **inert stub SW** (registers cleanly, never activates, never claims) and reads the REAL `sw.js` v186 stamp from disk; (c) the settings backdrop is `fixed inset-0 z-40` over the `z-30` header BY DESIGN — theme taps are probed with settings closed, never under its modal. New checks this round: the wide-viewport foldable probe (900×844 → grid ≤ 672px, centered, the glass still full-bleed, then back to 390×844 full width), computed `white-space: nowrap` on the card's leaf lines, the busy probe caught mid-greeting (cal/settings/reset at 0.45 + pointer-dead, theme at 1 + tappable, input 0.45), the consolidated changelog pointers present with three old one-liners gone, and the v186 stamp. sw v186; changelog pointers consolidated per the owner's order.

**v1.39.0 hotfix 11 (owner question — "why can some flights not be tapped open, but I can open them from the earnings page?"; no version bump — still 1.39.0, sw v185):** the diagnosis ran the whole live-pricing pipeline over every fly duty in the real Sep+Oct+Nov rosters — **21/21 chain and price clean**, so the toast was never the calculator refusing a priceable duty. The real bug: **the save flow files an entry under the FIRST LEG's date, not the report day.** Four of the 21 duties diverge: the JNB trip reports 16 Sep but departs 0118 on the 17th (files as the 17th), FCO reports the 24th and departs 0130 on the 25th, London reports 8 Nov and departs 0110 on the 9th, and the 1 Nov duty's roster row sits on 1 Nov while SQ 504 actually departs 1840 on 31 Oct (files as 31 Oct). `rosterCalOpenEarnings` matched `sectorDate === dkey` (the report day) only — it missed the filing, fell through to live pricing, and showed the honest "can't be priced yet" card whenever that duty's pairing didn't fit the hidden one-shot calculator (the chat's Build flow lets the owner fix a prefill by hand; the calendar's hidden card cannot — anything the owner had hand-fixed and saved lives only in the filing). **Fix:** the lookup now spans every date the duty touches (report day plus each leg's `ymd`/`stdYmd`/`staYmd`), exact-key first; a date-only fallback opens only ROUTE-LESS entries (`—`), and a routed entry on a shared date must name one of the duty's own stations — a neighbour duty's same-date filing (a PEN turnaround filed on the 17th, say) never opens from the JNB tap. **Suite:** roster-calendar 200 (+5: the chevron pins, the London duty filed under the 9th opens its FILED summary with no live re-price, the route guard, and the 1-Nov-departs-31-Oct back-reach) — pinned on a synthetic archive shaped exactly as the save flow files (persistArchive + decoy). **Browser smoke 56/56, zero page errors** (+3: the JNB duty filed on the 17th opens the filed summary from the calendar, not a live re-price, not the neighbour's filing; the newest What's New pointer). Also this round: the smoke workspace was rebuilt from scratch after a turn-boundary wipe (tailwind rebuilt WITH preflight to match the real CDN). sw v185; changelog pointer "Hotfix: duties saved under their first leg's date open from the calendar."

**v1.39.0 hotfix 10 (owner report + owner order — the first open and the duty tints; no version bump — still 1.39.0, sw v184):** two orders. **(1) The first open STILL showed the roster's earliest dates on the owner's phone.** Root cause: the reveal ran exactly ONCE, inside `openRosterCalendar` — while the sheet still sat fully off-screen at `translateY(100%)` (the slide only starts +10ms later). Chromium honors a `scrollTop` write in that state; the owner's WebKit evidently drops it, and nothing after the slide ever corrected the anchor. Fix: the measured-delta loop is now a standalone idempotent `rcRevealSelected()`, and the open ARMS three settle passes — **+90ms** (the slide has started), **+360ms** (it has landed), **+760ms** (late layout, fonts, icons). Each pass is a no-op unless the selected row is NOT the first line (unrendered, or measurably below the detail's top) — so passes never fight a reveal that landed, on any engine. A `pointerdown` anywhere on the sheet CANCELS the pending passes (a real touch owns the timeline), and `closeRosterCalendar` cancels them too; a healing pass re-runs the full measured-delta loop and refreshes `rcTlTopKnown` + `rcTlProgUntil` so the scroll-flip can never eat it. **(2) The duty tints (owner order): flight BLUE, standby GREEN, course BROWNISH GOLD, each tuned for both themes.** `rcTintKind(kind, code)` splits the old shared `duty` bucket by the owner's own standby table (`SSS*`/`SS*`/`SN*` → `sb`; SDLC and every other code → `crs`) — pills and timeline gutters (with their `is-on` edges) follow the same key; run-merging is untouched (duty runs still join only while the SAME code continues). Palette: fly `rgba(37,99,235,.30)` light / `rgba(96,165,250,.36)` dark; sb `rgba(21,128,61,.24)` / `rgba(74,222,128,.30)`; crs `rgba(146,94,14,.26)` / `rgba(212,167,76,.34)`. Layover keeps its teal, leave its purple, and SELECTION stays the app's gold everywhere (the selected-cell ring, today bar, row gradient) — the duty-type colors and the app's accent are separate languages. Real-roster fact learned: SDLC only lands as a duty day under stitched multi-month parses — an October-only parse yields standby days alone — so the course-day pin seeds a synthetic SDLC day rather than leaning on parse quirks. **Suite:** roster-calendar 195 (+20: the six tint literals, `rcTintKind` unit pins, the synthetic course pill + gutter, the standby gutter, the sabotage-and-heal behavioral pin — fling to the far bottom, re-arm, today leads again — and the arm/cancel src pins; the standby pins relabel `ca-rc-k-duty` → `ca-rc-k-sb`). 12/12, 1788 asserts. **Browser smoke 52/52, zero page errors** (+10: the heal verified in a real engine; six computed-style probes — the app boots DARK in headless Chromium, so each probe sets the theme explicitly and restores it; the real October grid shows green standby and blue fly circles with no shared duty tint left). sw v184; changelog pointer "Hotfix: flights blue, standby green, courses bronze on the calendar."

**v1.39.0 hotfix 9 (owner bug report — where the timeline detail opens and lands; no version bump — still 1.39.0, sw v183):** three orders. **(1) Bug 1 — open on today:** on a fresh open the detail showed the roster's EARLIEST dates while the grid sat on today's month — today's entry must be the first line below the calendar. **(2) Bug 2 — the blank detail after a chat refresh:** tap a date, close, `initChat()`, reopen and the detail was empty — the sheet kept its old `scrollTop` while the rebuilt virtualized window was shorter, so the scroller floated past every row. `rosterCalTimelineInit` now clamps a stale `scrollTop` to the new maxTop before the reveal. **(3) Order 3 — the tap flush:** tapping a date scrolls its entry to just below the calendar, always. The reveal is a **measured-delta loop** (≤4 passes): park `scrollTop` at the row's stored offset, render, then correct by `row.getBoundingClientRect().top − detail.getBoundingClientRect().top` until |delta| ≤ 1px — geometry only, never computed padding (this Chromium anchors content BELOW the scroller's padding, so a padding-derived anchor inverted the sign and doubled the error, 13px → 26px), and `lucide.createIcons()` runs BEFORE `rosterCalTLWindow`'s height measurement (a placeholder `<i>` and its svg differ in size; offsets measured before the swap go stale — and TLWindow's dedup early-return skips the re-measure on unchanged windows, which the loop tolerates because the stored offsets are already corrected). A fresh open whose window hasn't rendered the selected row yet `continue`s instead of breaking — the row arrives with the scroll. In jsdom `getBoundingClientRect` returns zeros, so delta is 0 and the loop lands on pass one at the stored offset — suite-compatible, no clamp. `openRosterCalendar` no longer hides the sheet first (the hide-first raced the reveal against the sheet's own layout), and the reveal always runs (the old `rcTlProgUntil` guard skipped it when a scroll was already pending). Same-version hotfixes never re-show What's New (the gate is `semverCmp(APP_VERSION, wnSeen) > 0`) — the changelog's 1.39.0 entry carries the pointer "Hotfix: the timeline opens on today and follows every tap." **Suite:** roster-calendar +4 pins (175) — src pins (reveal before first paint, the wrap visible, the stale-scroll clamp) plus the behavioral rendered-window row-index pin. **Two wall-clock reseed fixes (convention #5 — app behaviour untouched):** earnings-archive's backup-nudge block seeded fixed dates whose newest entry crossed the rolling 30-day backup window overnight — the seeds now ride the run date (40d + 3d back: always two distinct months, always "new" against a 30-day-old backup); next-flight's sweep block seeded bare `hh:mm` strings that straddled midnight in the 00:00–03:00 window and tripped `nfArrivalMs`'s overnight rule (arrival pushed to TOMORROW → never landed → never pruned — a 02:17 SGT suite run caught it) — the leg now carries true `ymd`/`staYmd` from real past moments, and the boot leg's lookback clamps at midnight (`nfFlightEnded` retires any flight whose DEPARTURE date has passed). **Browser smoke 42/42, zero page errors** (39 + three new: today as the first line on open, the tap flush on 2026-10-17, today again after close → `initChat()` → reopen), sw v183 read from disk, What's New seeded `wnSeen=1.38.0` so the 1.39.0 delta with its hotfix pointers shows. Tests: 12/12.

**v1.39.0 hotfix 8 (owner orders — gliding months, the PEK one-liner, secrecy; no version bump — still 1.39.0, sw v182):** seven orders (order 7's wording: finger-tracked drag, transient track carousel), all suite-pinned, plus a real-Chromium browser smoke (39/39, zero page errors) that caught four latent bugs. **(1) Month swipes GLIDE:** the swipe is finger-tracked — past 12px horizontal the grid builds a transient 3-pane track (`[prev][current][next]` at 300% width) whose `transform` follows the pointer (`translateX(calc(-33.3333% ± Npx))`, rubber-banded ×0.35 at the data edges), the release glides to the nearer side (.24s cubic-bezier) and commits, a vertical-dominant gesture never starts a drag, an untracked flick keeps the classic 48px threshold, and no track is ever left behind. **(2) The gesture rides window listeners** — `pointermove/up/cancel` attach to the window for the gesture's life and detach at its end, so a finger or mouse that wanders off the grid mid-swipe can no longer strand the drag (touch pointers capture implicitly; mouse and pen do not — the smoke's swipe ended 5px off the grid's left edge and hung the track). Deliberately NO `setPointerCapture`: capture retargets the tap's own click to the grid and silently breaks every day tap (the smoke caught this too — the click target becomes the grid and `closest('.ca-rc-day')` misses). **(3) The arrow slide pins its start pose with a forced reflow** (`void track.offsetWidth`) rather than a rAF chain — a busy compositor can starve rAFs past the 380ms commit timer and the glide then never starts (the smoke browser did exactly that); the fold has always used the same reflow pattern. **(4) The scroll-flip never fights the animation:** the debounced timeline flip ignores scroll events while a slide or drag owns the grid (its own reflow clamps the scroller and fires synthetic scrolls — a smoke run watched the flip revert an arrow slide mid-glide), and never fires when the scroller still sits exactly where the app parked it (`rcTlTopKnown`, read back after every reveal/re-render — a reflow-clamp scroll is the app's own, whenever it arrives). **(5) The fold GLIDES via the Web Animations API** (`g.animate`, 260ms height+opacity, finish-then-land + a 500ms fallback): a CSS transition off `display:none` never starts in Chromium, so **the expand had never animated on any real device** — it measured `hasLayout` while still hidden (always false) and took the instant path; the expand now un-hides the grid BEFORE the layout check, a fold generation counter plus explicit animation cancel keep a late-settling `finished` promise from re-landing over a newer fold, and jsdom (no `.animate`) keeps the inline-style glide the suite pins as the fallback. **(6) The PEK one-liner:** at a station whose clock matches SGT (|offset| ≤ 1min — PEK, KUL, HKG) the layover card folds to ONE line — "PEK — 1526H same as SGT" — no second SG line, no offset phrase. **(7) Secrecy:** the What's New sheet and the changelog never tell how Developer Mode unlocks (the ten-taps wording was stripped from the changelog entry; it keeps "Hidden Developer Mode, edits calculation modifiers via JSON."), and What's New carries the pointer "Hotfix: month swipes glide sideways like the timeline scrolls." Also: the drag transform formatting writes `+ N`/`- N` in its own branch, and the 4-sector turnaround (3 Sep SQ 134/133/138/137, SIN→PEN→SIN→PEN, priced through the live calculator) and RQ99's "Enjoy your birthday off" are pinned from the real September 2026 PDF. **The browser smoke** (puppeteer-core + @sparticuz/chromium on a 390×844 phone viewport, the real Sep+Oct+Nov 2026 PDFs through the real parser, the What's New sheet closed the way a user would, the chat waited to idle around the one-time tour offer): 39 checks — the three-month parse, the sheet opening from the header icon onto today's month, the swipe into November, hold-for-today, the BUG-3 regression (the swipe after hold STAYS November, the selection resets to the 1st), mid-gesture finger tracking, the tracked drag back to October, the arrow slide's transform, the 4-sector duty and its live pricing, RQ99's birthday line, both fold glides, the timeline scroll flipping the month both ways, the sheet never scrolling, sw v182 from disk, the PEK one-liner, both secrecy checks, and zero uncaught page errors. Tests: 12/12, 1758 asserts — roster-calendar 171 (drag glide + fold pins + the 4-sector/RQ99 September block), next-flight 108 (the PEK one-liner), dev-ui 403 (secrecy), roster-real 332 (September 2026), menu-extraction 48 + offline-menus 65 (v182 stamps). Stamps 1.39.0/v182; fix pointers on the 1.39.0 changelog entry.

**v1.39.0 hotfix 7 (owner orders — scroll architecture, virtualized timeline, live earnings; no version bump — still 1.39.0, sw v181):** smoke-verified on the owner's REAL October + November PDFs (33/33) and pinned in roster-calendar (154). **(1) Scroll architecture:** the sheet itself never scrolls (`overflow-hidden`); the month grid is fixed at the top and `#ca-rc-detail` is `flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto` — the timeline owns the vertical scroll, its scrollbar hidden (`scrollbar-width: none` + `::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}`); the grid keeps horizontal swipe navigation. **(2) Header:** the fold chevron moved to sit JUST AFTER the month header (arrows stay beside the close icon); today is now a PRESS-AND-HOLD (550ms pointer timer with move-slop and release cancel) instead of a double tap — a quick tap does nothing. **(3) Timeline:** `rosterCalTimelineInit` spans EVERY date of the attached roster (first stored day → last, honest rows for unprinted dates, a clean device still gets today's honest row); `rosterCalTLWindow` renders it VIRTUALIZED (estimated heights per row, a ±6-row overscan window, measured heights correct the estimates in browsers, top/bottom spacers carry the rest — thousands of dates scroll in constant DOM size); scrolling it flips the calendar to the month in view (throttled 140ms, `renderRosterCalendar(0, true)` keeps the selection), arrows/swipes scroll the timeline to their month, tapping a timeline row selects that date in the grid (and vice versa: grid taps reveal the row). Rows are full opacity now — only the selected row carries the highlighted band (`is-ctx` is gone). **(4) Flight card rework (owner orders):** flight number + aircraft sit CENTERED above the plane (aircraft in the flight number's type size, its own muted colour); the earnings amount shares the flight-number row at the card's right edge so the arrival code/time own their corner; the chevron stays at the far right; the plane keeps its rotation (owner: fine as-is). **(5) The London fix — every duty is tappable into earnings, saved or not:** previously only SAVED duties had a chevron (the owner's 8–12 Nov trip was never calculated+saved, so nothing opened). Now `rosterCalLiveEarnings` prices any duty through the SAME engine the Build path uses — `rosterChainTrips` (extracted from rosterParse, the days pass now iterates `trips`) rebuilds the trip from the stored legs (the allFlights store now carries `pos` for paxing), `renderCalculatorCard` grew an optional hidden `target` (chat side effects and scroll skipped), the hidden card is prefilled via `rosterPrefillCard` and computed via `computeCardResults`, then removed — nothing saved. Card amounts fill async from a one-at-a-time queue (`rcEarnCache` per session); a SAVED entry always outranks the live figure (re-checked every pass). Tapping a card opens the archive sub-sheet (saved view via `openArchEntry`, or a live overlay with Grand Total + Flight Overview + "Figured from your roster — not saved yet"); unpriceable duties say so via toast. London 306/305 prices at **$1511.94** (FS + repo rates — pinned). Also fixed: a latent crash where `validateInputs` ran on a card that had left the DOM. Tests: 12/12, 1708 asserts. **Re-attach roster PDFs after v181** (the store's `pos` field and paxing-correct earnings need a fresh parse). Stamps 1.39.0/v181; 9 fix pointers on the 1.39.0 changelog entry.

**v1.39.0 hotfix 6 (owner orders — header + timeline detail; no version bump — still 1.39.0, sw v180):** two overhauls, both smoke-verified on the owner's REAL October + November PDFs (39/39) and pinned in roster-calendar (136). **Header:** the month spells three letters ("Oct 2026"); the Today pill is GONE — a double tap on the month header (420ms window) jumps to today's month and selects today; the month label is now a button (`#ca-rc-jump` wrapping `h3#ca-rc-month`); the prev/next arrows ALWAYS render and move to the left of the close icon, graying out (`ca-rc-off`, opacity .3 + pointer-events none, `aria-disabled`) at the data edges instead of hiding; a fold chevron (`#ca-rc-fold`, chevron-up/down spans toggled) collapses `#ca-rc-grid` so the timeline fills the sheet and expands it back — every open resets to expanded. **Bottom detail = a day-row timeline** (`rosterCalFlowDays`/`rosterCalDayRow`/`rosterCalFlightCard`/`rosterCalLabelRow`/`rosterCalBlockHm`, replacing the old single-day retell): the selected day is the highlighted band (`.is-on`, gold gradient + tinted gutter + full-height accent bar on the gutter's right edge); the rest of its duty (by dkey, across month edges) — or, for a non-fly day, its run of consecutive non-duty days — flows around it as muted `.is-ctx` rows (opacity .58, faint grey gutter edge). Every row = a 46px date gutter (day number over a three-letter month) tinted by state (gold fly / blue duty / grey off-lo-leave) + a content column. Flying days render hero cards: flight number in gold + aircraft in mono (roster's own **A/C row**, newly parsed: anchor `'A/C'`, regex `/^[0-9][A-Z0-9]{2}$/` — 359/7M8/35M/7WR), a straight dashed track with filled endpoint dots and the Lucide plane rotated 45° nose-right, port-local times under each IATA code (arrivals past midnight keep "(+1)"), and the block time centered below — taken from the roster's FT row (14H 30M SIN-LHR), falling back to local-time subtraction only when FT is absent; the drill-in chevron (with the saved amount) opens the earnings overlay when the duty has an archive entry. A day that departs from a layover station stacks its "LO · LHR" label above the card; a report-day row shows "Report 2310H" (**Rpt values now pool** — the deliberate `best !== 'rpt'` exclusion is gone, they join their row cluster as `rpt`); a landing-day row reads "Lands SIN · 0615H"; interior layover days read "LO · LHR". Standby rows show "Standby / SN60" + "Window 0600H – 2159H"; off rows show their code + "Enjoy your day off" (coffee; OFFD = circle-slash; AALV = tree-palm). Icons are Lucide throughout; dynamic rows call `lucide.createIcons()`; selection changes animate a 180ms fade/slide-up (`ca-rc-rise`, disabled under prefers-reduced-motion). The never-pruned `crewAssist.allFlights` store now carries `ft`/`ac`/`rpt` per leg. **Owners must re-attach roster PDFs after v180** for aircraft/block/report on the cards (old stores fall back to honest/blank). Caught by the design-system pins: aircraft text uses `ca-micro` (11px floor) and label chips use `ca-inset` (no hand-mixed bg pairs). Suite 12/12, 1690 asserts. Stamps 1.39.0/v180; 8 fix pointers on the 1.39.0 changelog entry.

**v1.39.0 hotfix 5 (owner orders, no version bump — still 1.39.0, sw v179):** six uniform rules for the pill calendar, all smoke-verified on the owner's REAL October + November PDFs. **(1) Layover days never draw green pills** — a lo outside every duty keeps its plain day cell only; on the grid lo days render nothing. **(2) A whole duty is ONE gold capsule, report day → final landing day** — the parser now collects `dutySpans` per duty chain and a post-pass fills blank days inside a span as fly+dkey, converts interior lo days to fly+dkey (keeping the duty's flights), and absorbs a lo run that PRECEDES a span (a roster that begins mid-trip) into it; 8–12 Nov SIN-LHR-SIN is one 5-day capsule including the 10th, whose detail retells the whole duty (SQ 306 · 305, "SIN → LHR → SIN · 9 Nov – 12 Nov") via a new dkey interior-day walk in `rosterCalDutyFlights`. The renderer ALSO absorbs at render time (`effDay`: a lo strictly inside a fly span becomes a synthetic fly with that dkey), so pre-v179 stored months still render correctly without re-import. **(3) Standby runs merge only when the SAME code continues** — 6–7 Oct SSS1/SS80 and 20–21 Oct SN60/SN80 are two separate circles each; duty (fly) runs still merge by dkey. **(4) Standby windows in the detail** from the owner's 19-code table (`RC_STANDBY_TIMES`): "SN60 · 0600H – 2159H" on 20 Oct, "SSS3 · 1200H – 2359H" on 4 Oct, etc. **(5) Swipe left/right on the grid changes months** — pointer events on `#ca-rc-grid` (|dx| ≥ 48px, |dx| ≥ 1.5·|dy|, horizontal-dominant only; vertical stays scroll; `touch-action: pan-y`), bounded by the data months so a swipe past the edge is a no-op. **(6) The sheet matches Settings exactly** — same shade (`bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm`), same top offset (`top-[calc(60px+env(safe-area-inset-top,20px))]`), full width (`max-w-xl` dropped), Settings-style drag handle, content wrapper `px-5 pb-8`. **Owners must re-attach roster PDFs after v179** for detail/fill correctness on months stored before this hotfix (the grid is covered by the render-time fallback). Tests: roster-calendar 115 — real pins now include the 8–12 Nov single capsule (left `calc(0*100%/7 + 3px)`, width `calc(5*100%/7 - 3px - 3px)`), 20/21 Oct separate circles, zero `ca-rc-k-lo` pills, standby windows, duck-typed swipe events (jsdom has no PointerEvent constructor — plain `Event` + `clientX/clientY` expandos on the real handler), and Settings-pattern sheet asserts; the synthetic NRT wrap/flush block was re-seeded as a fly+dkey duty (26 Oct–2 Nov). Suite 12/12, 1663 asserts. Stamps 1.39.0/v179; 7 fix pointers on the 1.39.0 changelog entry.

**v1.39.0 hotfix 4 (owner report on the REAL roster, no version bump — still 1.39.0, sw v178):** four orders, all traced to how the generator actually prints duties. **(1) Duties never bleed together** — 1 Oct SIN-KUL-SIN and 2 Oct SIN-HKT-SIN were one capsule because runs merged by KIND; now every fly day carries its duty’s key (`dkey` = the duty chain’s span start, from the same `merged` trip chains the calculator uses) and fly days join into one capsule only within ONE duty — back-to-back turnarounds render as two circles. **(2) Duties run to their LANDING day** — the generator splits a duty across rows (report row dated D, STD row dated D+1, STA row dated D+2; e.g. SQ 242 “13 Oct 1900H → 0015H” with the STA row dated 14 Oct). The flights pass already echo-merged those rows but kept only the first row’s date; the day pass now marks each leg’s `ymd` (report evening) + `depDate` + `arrDate`, so 12–14 Oct SIN-SYD-SIN is ONE capsule through the 0015H landing, and the detail shows the owner’s exact format **“13 Oct · 1900H → (+1) 0015H”** (legs also date from `depDate`, and Turnaround/Layover uses the calculator’s ≤6h-ground rule — a same-midnight DPS out-and-back reads Turnaround again). **(3) Dateless LO rows are the LANDING day** of the flight above them (the generator prints layovers from touchdown — x423 after SQ 306’s 09Nov row), not +1; that + the fly-beats-LO precedence fixed 8–12 Nov SIN-LHR-SIN: gold capsule 8–9 (report 2310 + 0110 dep), green LO 10th, gold capsule 11–12 (0910 dep, (+1) 0615 landing). **(4) Past flights** — the next-flight card prunes landed legs (`upcomingPrune` persists), so flown days said “no leg times”; new never-pruned twin store `crewAssist.allFlights` (written in `upcomingRemember` beside `dutyRemember`, month-replaced, carries `stdYmd`/`staYmd` true dates) feeds `rosterCalDutyFlights`, which now matches a day by report/departure/landing date and walks the FULL store — 2 Oct retells SQ 736/735 with times. ** owners must re-attach roster PDFs after this hotfix** — stored duty days are pre-hotfix shape (no dkey, no landing days); each re-import refreshes that month. Tests: roster-calendar 105 — synthetic pins the report-row/echo split + overnight landing + duty keys; real pins both Oct+Nov PDFs (12–14 capsule, 23–25 week-wrap with flush edges, 8–12 Nov continuous, (+1) formats, flown-duty legs, allFlights store); today-pins are now DYNAMIC via `todayLocalYMD()` (the date rolled to 5 Oct mid-session and caught them). Stamps 1.39.0/v178; 6 fix pointers on the 1.39.0 changelog entry.

**v1.39.0 hotfix 3 (owner spec, no version bump — still 1.39.0, sw v177):** the calendar redesigned to the owner's pill-grid spec. **Grid**: no cell borders/backgrounds/chips — aligned columns carry the structure; every day of the month is now tappable (31 buttons in October). **Pills** (behind the numbers, `pointer-events:none` layer per week row): single-day duties = 38px circles centered on the column; multi-day runs = ONE capsule per week segment — `left/width` in `calc(col * 100% / 7)` so joins are seamless, rounded (`999px`) only at the run's true ends, `0`-radius flush edges where the run continues past a week or month boundary (`contPrev`/`contNext` look at neighbour months via `rcKindLookup`); runs = maximal consecutive same-kind days (OFF/empty break); four tints: gold fly / blue standby-course / teal layover / purple leave (`ca-rc-k-*`, light+dark). **Today**: the gold ring is gone; a short accent bar sits directly beneath the number — every cell reserves the bar slot so numbers align and the indicator shows on duty and off days alike. **Selection**: tap selects (`is-selected` ring via `::after`), and `#ca-rc-detail` below a `ca-hairline` divider retells the day (aria-live): fly days list the duty legs (route, Turnaround/Layover, HHMMH) + a "Saved · $X — open" button when an earnings entry exists (`openArchEntry`); lo = "You're in AMD"; off/leave/standby say so; empty = honest line; fly-without-leg-times = honest line. The hotfix-2 popup-on-tap is superseded by this section. **Nav**: chevrons step the data-month list; at the ends the arrow is `invisible` (not `hidden` — the label doesn't jump); label updates; grid rebuilds; selection resets to today-if-in-month else the 1st (also on open). **Legend removed.** **Interaction**: `@media (hover:hover)` gates every calendar hover (incl. new `ca-rc-nav` chevron class, replacing their Tailwind `hover:` classes); press feedback = `:active` scale on days, nav, Today; chevrons stay Lucide. Pills use %-geometry + fixed 56px rows → orientation/width reflows can't misalign. Tests: roster-calendar 76 (synthetic NRT layover 26 Oct–2 Nov proves week-wrap flush joins + month continuation; real Oct pins circle col2/capsule cols5–6/standby capsule/OFF-no-pill/nav bounds/selection resets); stamps 1.39.0/v177; 5 fix pointers on the 1.39.0 changelog entry.

**v1.39.0 hotfix 2 (owner orders, no version bump — still 1.39.0, sw v176):** three calendar/header rulings. **(1) Full-height calendar** — `#ca-rc-sheet` now wears the overlay-page pattern (`top-[calc(60px+env(safe-area-inset-top,20px))]`, `flex flex-col`, scroll inside) exactly like Settings/Earnings instead of the short bottom-sheet `max-h`. **(2) Day taps pop up the flight summary, never a calculator card** — saved duties still reopen their full earnings entry (`openArchEntry`); unsaved ones get `rosterCalFlightSummaryHtml` in the same `ca-arch-sub` sheet (aria-label "Flight details" — a perfect fit): SQ numbers + Turnaround/Layover/Duty label, stops joined `→` with the date span, one hairline row per leg with `HHMMH` times, all strictly what the roster printed. The calendar stays open beneath (z-62 over z-60); the `renderCalculatorCard`/`rosterPrefillCard` path and its bot line are GONE from day taps. Note: the real Oct 2026 duty is **SQ 164/163 SIN–SAI** (0840H→0955H / 1045H→1415H) — the first test pin wrongly borrowed the synthetic KUL route. **(3) Header icons pack tighter** — the four header buttons step down to `p-3` (44px targets, still standard-compliant) and the group `gap-2 → gap-1`, reclaiming ~28px so the row stops eating the CrewAssist title and the online dot; sheet close buttons elsewhere keep the roomier 48px (`p-3.5`), and dev-ui pins both standards explicitly. Tests: roster-calendar 42 (real-button open + ancestor visibility from hotfix 1 retained; popup asserts: no `[data-calc-card]` created, legs + type + route + HHMMH pinned), dev-ui 356; stamps 1.39.0/v176; 4 fix pointers on the 1.39.0 changelog entry.

**v1.39.0 hotfix (owner report, no version bump — still 1.39.0, sw v175):** the roster calendar did not open on its header icon — but DID appear after tapping a saved flight in Earnings. Cause: the `#ca-roster-cal` markup was inserted INSIDE `#ca-arch-sub` (the archive sub-sheet wrapper, `hidden` until a flight row is tapped) — the icon tap removed the calendar's own `hidden` while the wrapper's kept it blind; opening the archive sub-sheet later un-hid the parent and the waiting calendar popped out. Fix: the sheet is now a sibling after `#ca-arch-sub` closes. Standing rule written into the markup comment: **a sheet is never nested inside another sheet's wrapper.** Tests learned the lesson: roster-calendar now opens via the REAL button click (not eval) and asserts visibility through the whole ancestor chain (`wrap.closest('.hidden') === null`) plus no sheet-in-sheet nesting — the old asserts checked only the element's own class and passed against the bug (39 asserts now). Changelog: 1 fix pointer on 1.39.0 (per the hotfixes-in-changelog ruling). No version bump; stamps 1.39.0/v175.

**v1.39.0 (owner greenlight: round two — A5 menu diff, B7 natural commands, B6 roster calendar; sw v174):** **A5, the menu route-diff.** Owner rulings: comparison = **same flight number across dates**, shown **in the menu viewer**. `renderMenuDiffRow` rides every `renderMenu` (cached, fresh, stale — the comparison is about content, not age): `menuPreviousLookup` scans the cache for the newest OTHER-date `SQ<fn>:<ymd>:<cabin>` entry, `menuDiffSignature` builds name sets per class (`menuDiffClass`: /main|entrée/ and /dessert|sweet|pastry|cake/ — everything between is not compared, honestly), and the `#menu-diff-row` (max-h/opacity animated, `git-compare-arrows`/`check` Lucide) reads exactly the owner's copy: "vs your 3 Oct lookup: 2 mains changed, same desserts." / "Same menu as your 3 Oct lookup." No second date cached → no row. **B7, natural calculator commands.** `parseNaturalCommand` (grammar: optional mode word menu|ifa|lma|cop, then 1–4 (flight, date) pairs; `sq802`/`sq 802`/`802`, `4 oct`/`oct 4`/`4oct`, full month names, nearest-upcoming year; anything not parsing CLEANLY falls through to the old keyword engine) runs ahead of the keyword branches in `analyzeIntent`. `runNaturalCommand`: LMA guards first (owner rulings: single sector → "LMA needs a layover…"; all pairs same date → same-day turnaround refusal; route-known Turnaround via `natBuildTrip` also refuses), then per-pair resolution — `natRosterInfo` (raw `loadUpcoming`, so past duties resolve) first, online `natResolveOnline` (getcabin → cached CABINS → `fetchMenuForPrinter` first cabin → route + printed local times, both landing in the cache) second, offline misses stay blank with the honest "Fetch all when you're connected" line. `natBuildTrip` builds the `rosterPrefillCard` trip (type Turnaround iff first dep SIN + last arr SIN + same-day; `natFtHm` derives FT from STD/STA rolling past midnight); `natChooserBubble` is the retire-on-press button pair (bare command → Menus / COP allowance; multi-leg menu → one button per leg). **B6, the roster calendar.** The parser now reads the Duty column (lowest-y 'Duty' header; Duty-Time pair sits further along) and bare Sector-column station codes (SIN on OFF rows, MEL on LO rows): `parsed.days` = fly days (from `flights`, fn list on the chip), `lo@STN`, the OFF family (ATDO/OFFD/EXDO/RQ99/RX99 — owner's list), AALV → AL, any other code → standby/course keeping its code as the label. **The Duty Codes legend page (every report ends with one) is detected by its own headers and never becomes days** — the first smoke run invented a phantom November from it. Dateless layover-continuation rows (real quirk: the generator skips the date) carry forward one day from the last seen date. `dutyRemember` persists `crewAssist.dutyDays` (month-replaced like `upcoming`, never touched by the menu clear). UI: `#btn-roster-cal` in the header (Lucide calendar-days, p-3.5, tour-locked with the other chrome) opens `#ca-roster-cal` (bottom sheet, shade + slide, Escape in the stack): Sunday-first grid like the date picker, `.ca-rc-cell` chips (gold fly with `SQ 106·105`, outlined lo station, muted OFF/AL, amber standby code), today ring, legend, animated month nav, honest empty state ("No roster for this month…"). Tap a flying day: saved earnings entry reopens (`sectorDate` match, `openArchEntry`); else the duty's flights chain (`rosterCalDutyFlights`: walk back to the duty start, forward to arr SIN) into a prefilled COP card and the calendar steps aside; flown-and-unsaved says so; OFF/AL/LO/standby days answer in chat. **Ruling change (owner, this round): hotfixes join the changelog even without a version bump** — the v173 hotfix pointers are retroactively appended to the 1.38.0 entry (What's New still shows only the current release, so no replay). Also this round: the suite's "landed 6.5h ago" seeds were a 27%-of-the-day flake (they wrap past midnight and turn future) — both landed-leg seeds now read yesterday with fixed walls. Tests: 12 suites / 1564 asserts / 0 fail (new roster-calendar suite 35; next-flight 104; dev-ui 352; offline-menus 65); stamps 1.39.0/v174.

**v1.38.0 hotfix (owner orders, no version bump — still 1.38.0, sw v173):** five rulings, all owner-verbatim. **(1) A1 chevron removed** — it was "shifting some of the text"; the month's $ total stays a tappable toggle (`.ca-arch-proj-btn` button, arm-away) with no chevron glyph; `.ca-arch-proj-chev` CSS deleted. **(2) A2 itinerary rows WITHDRAWN** ("remove previous a2 feature") — `overviewBlock` is back to prose-only (`overviewItinHtml` and the `.ca-ov-itin` CSS deleted; the three call sites revert), BUT the whole zone machinery stays and is repurposed: `AIRPORT_TZ` + `zonedWallToUtcMs` now power the next-flight card and the layover clock. `landingSgtPhrase` also learned to return '' for same-offset-as-SG stations (KUL: no "(1432H SGT)" noise). **(3) B9 finished** — the storage rows moved inside one house-style glass-panel box with hairline-separated rows, and the two "Learn the app" rows (tour + changelog) merged into their own single box to match. **(4) A3 approved with changes** — no transport time, 24-hour `HHMMH` format, folded into the next-flight card (see (5)). **(5) the next-flight card REDESIGNED as a two-part duty card.** `nfDutyOf(all)` chains legs into duties (a leg joins the previous one unless the previous arrived SIN or departs from a different station); the card then shows one PART at a time: **part 1** (crew in SG, duty departs SIN — the `out` legs) = suggested alarm `STD−4h` + `Reporting STD−2h` + `STD` (alarm section only inside a 12h-before-alarm window), the part's flight numbers joined ` · ` + station IATA + a Save-menu button (`#ca-nf-save`, plural "menus" for multisector), then real-time details: date + relative day, `Departs HHMMH · in Xh Ym`, `Lands STATION HHMMH local · HHMMH SGT`. **Part 2** (crew at the station — the `ret` legs): no alarm, `Departs HHMMH local`, `Lands SIN HHMMH SGT`; a leg currently airborne shows `In the air · lands in Xh Ym` instead and suppresses the layover card. A duty with a leg in the air is found zone-aware via `nfDepMs`/`nfArrMsZone` (process-TZ-naive math is NOT mixed in — that was smoke bug #1). Multisector duties: part 1 = every leg until the first SIN arrival, so `SQ 123 · SQ 124 · NRT` with the FIRST sector's departure and the FINAL station's landing. The old `#ca-nextflight-tap` id is preserved on the presented part (tap opens its menus). **New: the layover time-zone card** (`renderLayoverTzCard`, owner's exact format) — while in part 2, not airborne, station zone known: `NRT — 1532H · 1 hour ahead of SG` over `SG — 1432H`, enqueued in chat after the greetings and BEFORE the flight card; it refreshes in place every minute via `caRealtimeSweep` keyed on a `data-sig` (station|stH|sgH) so the 60s sweep never re-appends. Same-offset stations read "same as SG". **Bug the rewrite exposed:** `rosterYmAdd` parsed local midnight but formatted via `toISOString` (UTC) — on the owner's real SGT phone every same-row overnight STA<STD arrival stitched one day early; fixed to pure-UTC math (`'T00:00:00Z'` + `setUTCDate`, identical output on a UTC box). Related standing trap the fix rode in on: **the test box must be pinned to SGT** — `tests/_harness.js` now sets `process.env.TZ='Asia/Singapore'` at require time, because the app's naive wall math (expiry sweeps, day labels) and its zone-aware station math only agree on an SGT device; the 2026-10-03 sandbox came up UTC and split them. Tests rewritten to the new card: next-flight 104 (was 74 — the v1.31.1 two-row block and v1.33.1 in-air block re-specced, plus six new blocks: alarm window in/out, part 2 + tz card, multisector, save-button flow, airborne suppression), dev-ui 330, roster-pdf 187 (A2 pins removed), offline-menus/menu-extraction v173 stamps; full suite 11/11, 1499 asserts, 0 fail. No changelog entry (no-bump ruling).

**v1.38.0 (owner greenlight: feature round one — A1 forecast, A2 time-zone conversions, B9 storage; sw v172):** **A1, the still-to-fly projection.** The owner's rulings: calculated-but-unflown only (nothing auto-priced), LMA counts like IFA, and the projection **appears/disappears on tapping the month's $ total**, with an up/down chevron signalling tappability. Implementation: a month header with unflown duties (from `archMonthState`) renders its amount as a `.ca-arch-proj-btn` beside the collapse button (completed months keep a plain span — the old always-on "Projected · N flights" hollow-dot row is REPLACED by this panel; the three old pins were rewritten). The panel (`.ca-arch-proj-wrap`, max-height/opacity animation like every reveal) shows "Still to fly · N flights / $X" + "Landed $Y · on track for $Z"; opening it also expands a collapsed group so the reveal is never hidden. State is `archState.projOpen` (in-memory, like `collapsed`). **A2, SGT landing conversions.** The owner chose the duty card / Flight Overview with "lands 06:35 local (05:35 SGT)" on every sector row. New `AIRPORT_TZ` — a curated SQ-network code→IANA-zone table (~95 stations; the 574-entry IATA DB stays city data) — plus `zonedWallToUtcMs` (two-pass offset solve, so DST lands right: LHR Jul 01:35 vs Dec 02:35; Kathmandu's :45 offset crosses SGT midnight) and `landingSgtPhrase` (empty for SIN — local IS SGT — and for unknown stations: **never a guessed offset**). Data path: `rosterPrefillCard` now stamps `data-dep-ymd/-dep-hm/-arr-ymd/-arr-hm` on sector blocks (Fetch already set the arr pair); the results snapshot's sectors carry `std/sta/arrYmd`; `overviewBlock(text, snap)` renders one itinerary row per sector with times above the prose — on the duty card (both mode), the results sheet, and the earnings sub-sheet. Pre-1.38.0 saved snapshots lack sta → prose only (honest). Pinned end-to-end via the KTM demo roster (lands 21:55 local (00:10 SGT)). **B9, the storage panel.** Settings gains a Storage section (rendered fresh by `renderStoragePanel` on every open): exact counts for earnings entries and saved menus (flights), the browser's usage figure labelled approximate, persisted-storage status (all guarded — jsdom shows "—"), and **Clear saved menus** as an amber two-tap pill (armTwoTap is the click handler, not a binder — bind with addEventListener). The clear keeps every upcoming duty's menus (the v1.34.1 lesson: the next-flight card's offline promise must never break) and says so. **A3 was specced but NOT built (owner order)**: suggested alarm for SIN departures = STD − 4h (2h report + 1h prep + 1h transport) — where/when proposed to the owner in chat. Changelog: 3 new pointers. Tests: 11 suites / 1472 asserts / 0 fail (earnings-archive 254, dev-ui 331, roster-pdf 189, offline-menus 57); stamps 1.38.0/v172.

**v1.37.0 hotfix (owner order: NO version bump — still 1.37.0, sw v171):** the onboarding name field (`#ob-name` — the same input the Settings → Edit reflow prefills) now carries `maxlength="23"`, capping the profile name at 23 characters including spaces. Attribute-only by design: the cap governs INPUT (typing and paste both), while longer names that arrive through the developer JSON import keep their stored text — the settings row already truncates visually (v1.37.0's 4.4), and a programmatic prefill of an over-long imported name still displays (maxlength constrains user input, not script sets; noted so nobody "fixes" it into silent data rewriting). No changelog entry (no new version ⇒ no What's-New delta — the v1.35.0 discipline). Tests: offline-menus +2 (cap attribute, sw v171), menu-extraction stamp; 11 suites / 1449 asserts / 0 fail; sw v171.

**v1.37.0 (owner greenlight: the 13-item polish review — 1.1 value-prop line skipped by owner; sw v170):** the in-app nudge trio: **1.2** the second manually-created card (counter `crewAssist.manualCards`; roster bulk-builds don't count) earns a one-time "just attach your roster PDF" chat hint (`crewAssist.nudgedRoster`); **1.4** after the third hand edit to a Fetch-owned field (`-ifa-t*` / `-lma-iata*`) while `calcUiMode === 'default'`, a one-time "Settings → Allowance calculator interface → Manual" hint (`crewAssist.manualEdits` / `crewAssist.nudgedCalcUi`); **1.3** the earnings empty state now reads mental-model-first: "Entries appear when you tap Save on a calculation — or import a JSON backup with the arrow above." Contrast: **2.1** `--arch-muted` 0.45→0.60 (dark) / 0.62 (light) and every earnings micro-label steps 9→10px, the metalabel swapping `opacity:.55` for the token color; **2.2** light-mode gold TEXT only gets `#7A620F` via `html:not(.dark) .text-sia-gold` (icons/borders keep their gold — gold-on-gold combos are provably absent); **2.3** dark onboarding pills brighten to white/75 (CSS-only, so the selected state — which drops `ui-input` — and the v1.33.1 pin stay intact); **2.4** both semver stamps white/15→/25. Tap targets: **3.1** the connection dot button grows to 44×44 (`w-11 h-11`, visual 8px dot unchanged) and `.print-edit-eye` to `min-height:44px`; **3.3** offline renders a hollow amber ring (`bg-transparent border-2 border-amber-500`) against online's solid pulsing green. **3.2 Escape closes the topmost open sheet** (whats-new → changelog → earnings → settings → menu viewer → print → results; results closes inline — it never had a named close fn). Ownership guards: the app dialog, the archive's inline figure edit, and the menu lightbox each keep their own Escape; if the earnings sub-sheet is up, Escape closes just the sub-sheet (`closeArchSub`). AUDIT CORRECTION: the gold focus ring was already global since v1.32.0 (E3) — nothing to redo there. Data safety: **4.1** `maybeInstallHint()` (2.5s post-boot, once, `crewAssist.installHintDone`) tells browser-TAB users with ≥1 saved earnings that Safari's ITP can clear tab storage after 7 days and installing (Share → Add to Home Screen / ⋮ → Install app) is exempt — skipped in standalone (`matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)')` / `navigator.standalone`) and deferred (no flag) while what's-new is on screen; **4.2** the backup nudge gains a count arm — ≥10 entries newer than `lastBackupAt` re-arms it even inside the 21-day quiet window, copy "10 new entries since your last backup…", 14-day snooze unchanged. **RULING (p), month attribution (2026-10-03):** a duty's earnings count in the month its FIRST SECTOR DEPARTS (SIN→XXX out Nov 30, back Dec 2 = November). Recon proved the code already does this (`buildCopArchivePayload` takes monthKey from `${id}-ifa-d1`), so the change is zero-behavior — pinned by a Nov-30 test in earnings-archive and recorded here. **4.4** the settings profile name truncates and the changelog + earnings gutters unify at px-6 (6 edits). Harness: one-time nudge flags are pre-suppressed in `_harness` boot (deterministic suites) with per-test opt-out — dev-ui verifies each nudge behaviorally by clearing its flag. Changelog: 7 pointers (4 imp, 1 fix, 2 new). Tests: 11 suites / 1447 asserts / 0 fail (offline-menus 52, dev-ui 323 — changelog loops derive their counts from source, earnings-archive 244); stamps 1.37.0/v170.

**v1.36.1 (owner tier ruling — the wide-screen column system; sw v169):** from the foldable-inner-screen discussion (Earnings rendered edge-to-edge while every other sheet buffers inwards), the owner approved the **tiered container system**: **sm (384px) = modal cards** (what's-new, date picker, confirms), **xl (576px) = reading surfaces** (chat + chips, settings, changelog, results, print, flight-details sub-sheet) — and **Earnings joins the READING tier** (owner preference: tapping a flight in Earnings opens the summary sub-sheet, already xl — the column must not jump width mid-flow), **2xl (672px) = the menu viewer alone** (the chart-and-cards data page). Every full-screen sheet keeps identical chrome (full-bleed glass, rounded top, handle); only the inner content column centers + caps — that column, not one width, is the coherence. It is also the PC story: nothing exceeds 672px, so desktop self-buffers with the starfield framing, no media queries; on phones no cap ever binds (nothing changes). Implementation: the Earnings header row + `#ca-arch-scroll` gained `max-w-xl mx-auto w-full` (the changelog header pattern); the toast was already centre-anchored; the sub-sheet already carried the column. Tests: earnings-archive +2 (header + scroll carry the reading column); dev-ui's Fixes-filtered sort assert now derives the newest fix-carrying release from the source (the current release may be imp-only, as 1.36.1 is); stamps 1.36.1/v169. 11 suites / 1408 asserts / 0 fail; sw v169.

**v1.34.3 (owner rulings, what's-new discipline + extraction nitpicks):** (1) *What's New showed past features.* The v1.29.1 no-accumulate rule regressed — v1.34.1/v1.34.2 carried earlier bullets forward, so updating re-showed features the crew had already seen. Fixed and made mechanical: `APP_WHAT_NEW` holds ONLY the current release's bullets, each tagged `// vX.Y.Z`, and dev-ui E6 rejects any bullet not tagged with the current `APP_VERSION` (rule restated in the ship checklist above at owner request). (2) *Extraction nitpicks on the compact sheet:* "fish cake" in a hidden description yielded a FISH protein — fish cake is made with fish but is not a whole seafood protein; it joins the sauce/broth mask list (`EX_SAUCEY` + fish cake/cakes/ball/balls). And "rice noodles" surfaced as bare RICE (or NOODLES) — rice and rice noodles are totally different; `rice noodles`/`rice noodle` are now starch compounds that win over their parts, so Beef Hor Fun reads `Ẃ RICE NOODLES` (and `wok-fried rice noodles`, `wide rice noodles` keep their modifiers). Corpus consequence: the 1,139-pair fixture regenerated **deliberately** — 22 pairs changed, every one in exactly the two ruled classes (15 rice-noodle starch upgrades; 7 fish-cake masks: Khao Thom, 4x Nasi Lemak, 2x Laksa/Mee Siam); Fishball Kway Teow Soup keeps its FISH (from "fish dumplings", a different call — say the word if that class should mask too). New pinned asserts: Beef Hor Fun, Laksa, Khao Thom + two engine-level checks. Earnings "erased" report closed: owner confirmed no recurrence (the v1.34.2 readable-confirm + 10s Undo hardening stands). Tests: menu-extraction 48, dev-ui 113; 11 suites / 1218 asserts / 0 fail; sw v160.

**v1.34.2 (owner reports x3):** (1) *Earnings "erased" after import + pill toggles.* Investigated to the bone: the Month/Year/All-time pills on the gold summary card are provably read-only (imported through the real `archImportRead` path in the harness and toggled every scope — storage and rows intact; the pills only set `archState.scope` and re-render). The archive has exactly three write paths (trash, import, save) and only ONE one-tap "erase everything": the trash icon 48px above the pills, guarded by a confirm dialog. That dialog was the actual bug — see (2). Hardening shipped anyway: a confirmed delete now holds its Undo for the full **ten seconds** (the E1 whole-roster window — deleting the entire archive is the biggest action there is). (2) *Light-mode pop-ups unreadable.* Every alert/confirm rides `.glass-bubble` over the black/60 scrim; in light mode the gold tint composites to dark olive under near-black text (~3.4:1) — the v1.31 fix covered only the What's-New bubble. The app-dialog now gets the same cream card (`rgba(246,241,232,.96)`), so "Delete all N entries?" is finally READABLE before anyone taps the gold OK. (3) *Duty menu "overridden" after checking other flights (SQ736/SQ735).* Root cause: `submitFlightVerification` parks the searched flight's sector picks on `currentMenuSearch.selectedSectors`; `openNextFlightViewer` never cleared them, so the duty's legs filtered to nothing and the sheet kept the LAST-RENDERED flight's menu until restart. Fix: the upcoming card resets the sector filter (it opens the whole duty), plus a defense-in-depth fallback in both `renderMenu` and `filterPrintLegs` — a sector filter that matches nothing falls back to every leg instead of blanking/staling the sheet. `currentMenuSearch` and `printState` became `var` (was `let`) so the harness can reach them — browser-identical. Tests: offline-menus 39 (+2: stale sector filter cleared, duty's own menu renders), earnings-archive 238 (+2: cream-card rule, 10s delete Undo); 11 suites / 1218 asserts / 0 fail; sw v159.

**v1.34.1 (owner report, next-flight menus wiped by searches):** the duty's saved menus were being deleted by unrelated menu searches. Chain: rendering a searched flight's schedule calls `rememberRecentFlight` → pushes {flight, date} onto `crewAssist.recentFlights` (capped at 5) → `pruneSqMenuCache()` deletes every `SQ{fn}:{ymd}:*` cache key not in those 5. The owner saved SQ736 (SIN-HKT) + SQ735 (HKT-SIN) from the next-flight card — those menus were protected only by their position in the recents list, so once other lookups filled it, the next search evicted a duty flight and wiped its menus (badge gone, offline open broken). Fix: `pruneSqMenuCache`'s keep-set now also includes every upcoming roster flight (`upcomingList()` — already prunes landed legs, so it is exactly "duties still to fly"). Storage stays bounded: after a duty lands it falls out of upcoming and the 5-recents cap tidies its menus exactly as before. The v1.26.0 "5 most recent flights" hygiene is otherwise unchanged (a flight neither recent nor upcoming still prunes). Tests: offline-menus 37 (+4: SQ736/SQ735 survive a full recents list, badge persists via `menusSavedFor`, flown duty still tidies away); 11 suites / 1208 asserts / 0 fail; sw v158.

**v1.34.0 (owner request, protein marking mode + SQ106 hidden protein):** two changes to the compact print sheet.
