(1) New toolbar toggle between the text-size control and the Bounds pill — lucide `brackets` / `square` icons, compact layout only (hidden otherwise, like the elegant-only options hide). **Brackets is the default**: protein renders as plain black text wrapped in `[ ]` — no background fill at all, so it cannot fall foul of any print engine's background-graphics setting (belt-and-braces after v1.33.3's print-color-adjust). The square restores today's white-on-black invert. The mode flows everywhere automatically: the DOM sheet, the 300-dpi PNG/JPEG export (canvas tokens draw `[WORD]` instead of the black rect — the token `invert` flags are unchanged, only the renderer branches) and Export→Word (it clones the rendered DOM). In-memory only, like every other print option. (2) SQ106 owner report: the Cold Refreshment "open-faced sandwich" hid its protein in the description ("sliced smoked chicken") and the sheet showed nothing. Root cause: additions were mains-only (`if (isMain)` in both the DOM renderer and the canvas tokeniser). Fix: new `compactNonMainProtein(name, desc)` — non-main courses surface **proteins only** (never starch — the dessert ruling stands: "Cake with Cream" stays clean), only when the name itself shows no protein, reusing the frozen extraction vocabulary verbatim with the same dash (—) / flow (Ẃ) connectors and dedupe as mains. Light bites (the comma-joined single line, `asBites`) are deliberately left without additions — say the word if they should carry proteins too. Tests: 11 suites / 1200 asserts / 0 fail (menu-extraction 43: brackets default, square restore, SQ106 hidden + primary phrasings, canvas token carry-through, name-shows-protein no-op, dessert starch no-op); sw v157.

**v1.33.3 (owner report, print fix — jumps the queue ahead of the parked review items):** printed compact sheets lost the protein words — `.pc-hl` (the white-on-black invert) relies on its background, and browsers print with background graphics OFF by default: the black fill vanished, leaving white text on white paper. Fix: `-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; print-color-adjust: exact;` on `.pc-hl` forces the fill through every print engine (WebKit included — the owner's iOS print path); screen rendering, the 300-dpi canvas export (`exportCompactImage` bakes its own fill) and the elegant sheet (dark-on-white only) are untouched. The menu-extraction suite pins the rule. NOTE: if any specific printer flow still drops the fill, the next escalation is a `beforeprint` swap to a border-boxed dark-text highlight (borders always print) — say the word. The v1.33.2-review items (#18–#29 in the second PM/UX review) are PARKED by owner ruling until this print fix is confirmed on device. Tests: 11 suites / 1189 asserts / 0 fail; sw v156.

**v1.33.2 (owner report):** (earnings tracks landings, same rule as the next-flight card) a duty counts as FLOWN once it has landed, not at midnight: `rosterPrefillCard` stashes the roster's last-sector arrival on the card (`data-duty-end`, from arrDate+staHm; hand-built cards carry nothing), `buildCopArchivePayload` records it as `endAt`, and `archEntryLanded` (`endAt` → nowMs ≥ endAt; no arrival on record → the old `sectorDate < today` date rule, never a guessed duration) feeds `archMonthState` — so the In progress / Completed / Upcoming badges, the Projected rows, the On-track-for-X-·Y-left context, and the chart's solid/stripe split all follow the duty's actual landing; a same-day duty that landed reads Completed on the day itself. The earnings sheet also follows real time while open: `archLandedSig` (a landed-fingerprint) is stamped by every `renderArch`, and `archLandedSweep` — riding the SAME triggers as the next-flight sweep (`caRealtimeSweep`: 60s interval + the instant the app returns to the screen) — re-renders only when a tracked duty actually crosses its landing, so an open list never scrolls under a thumb. Entries saved before v1.33.2 have no `endAt` and keep the date rule (re-attaching + re-saving refreshes them). TEST FIXTURE REPAIRS: (a) earnings-archive's v1.33.0 MoM block had been inserted AFTER `process.exit` — dead code since that release; moved to life, corrected to month-scope (the MoM line lives in the month-scope summary), now genuinely enforced. (b) roster-pdf's Flight Overview block raced `chat-container.lastElementChild` against the typing greeting and used a fixed 1000ms wait — now polls the calc-all button and the overlay content. (c) next-flight's in-air assert recomputed the seeded STA at assert time (a minute roll between seed and assert broke it) — the value is pinned once. (d) dev-ui's E6 guard relaxed from >=4 to >=1 What's-New entries — a one-change release is legitimate; every per-line format check stands. Tests: 11 suites / 1190 asserts / 0 fail; sw v155.

**v1.33.1 (owner report, 2 fixes):** (light mode “unable to see” — onboarding + What's New) three dark-theme looks had leaked into daylight. Onboarding fields carried `.ui-input`'s dark border (white/10 — invisible on cream) and no surface → `html:not(.dark) #onboarding-view .ui-input` paints a white/60 field with a 20%-ink border (the selected gold-gradient state is untouched; dark unchanged). The watermark tagline + semver sat at `text-sia-navy/20` (1.4:1) → `/70` (~5.9:1), the divider hairline /20→/30, the name field's icon gray-400→gray-600 and its placeholder gray-600. The What's New bubble kept `--glass-bubble-bg` gold-tint glass, which over the black/60 scrim composites to dark olive under near-black light-mode text (3.4:1 body, 1.6:1 gold label) → `html:not(.dark) #whatsnew-backdrop .glass-bubble` is a near-opaque cream card on the dim scrim; its close button also joins the 48px standard (`p-2`→`p-3.5` — missed in the v1.33.0 sweep). (next flight “more accurate to real time” — a KUL turnaround that landed 11:45 still showed at 17:58) `upcomingRemember` now stores each flight's `sta` + `staYmd` (the date row its STA was printed on — echo rows carry the next day); `nfArrivalMs`/`nfFlightEnded` retire a flight at its LANDING (overnight: an STA printed on a later row is taken as-is, else STA<STD ⇒ +1 day; no arrival on record ⇒ the old date-granular rule — never guess a duration the roster never printed); `upcomingList` and the menu nudge inherit the fix; a leg between STD and STA reads “lands 11:45” instead of “departs”; `nextFlightExpirySweep` (60s interval + the instant the app returns to the screen, via visibilitychange) prunes storage and re-renders card + nudge — the 5:58pm unlock after an 11:45am landing clears the card with no reload. NOTE for the owner: re-attach the current roster once after updating so existing entries gain their arrival times — entries stored before v1.33.1 have no STA on record and keep the end-of-day rule. Tests: 11 suites / 1177 asserts / 0 fail; sw v154. Earnings-archive: two more date-pinned blocks (current/future month collapse; Completed/Upcoming) now run on the harness's frozen clock — the real date left September and they were reading October.

**v1.33.0 (owner-approved PM/UX review — 12 of 17 shipped; 8/11/16 skipped by ruling, 13 rain-checked):** (#1 contrast) unselected onboarding gender/rank buttons `text-gray-400`→`text-gray-600` (measured 2.26:1→~7:1 on cream) and `html:not(.dark) .ca-micro.text-gray-500 { color: #4b5563; }` lifts the 11px micro-label floor (4.6:1→~7:1, light theme only; dark untouched at 7.5:1); B/L/D meal strips `ca-micro`→`text-xs` in both render paths. (#2 rank-rate hard stop) `computeCardResults`: a PROFILE whose rank has no `baseRates` entry sets `snap.rateMissing` — no more silent $13.50 — sector loops skip, LMA still computes; single Calculate explains in-card + toast and refuses the overlay; Calculate-all counts `rateBlocked` (red note in the combined summary; a fully-blocked batch gets its own chat message, never the generic incomplete line); a missing PROFILE (impossible past onboarding) keeps the FS default so dev/test boots stay sane. (#3) undo toasts append an sr-only “— Undo available for N seconds.” line. (#4 targets) `.ca-arch-iconbtn` 38→48px; header/sheet close buttons `p-2`→`p-3.5`; scroll-bottom/roster/send `w-9 h-9`→`w-10 h-10`; chips +`min-h-[48px]`; archive delete `p-1.5`→`p-3`; all three switch rows restructured so the `<label>` wraps text+control — the whole row (text included) is the tap target. (#5) combined summaries group by month when the batch spans >1 month: gold-ruled header = month name + “N trips · $subtotal”, roster order preserved. (#7) `rosterResultsCsv` + `#btn-results-csv` in the results header (combined summaries only; the single summary hides it): sector rows (Month,Date,Flight,From,To,Type,Hours,Multiplier,Paxing,Direct US,Amount USD) + LMA row + TRIP TOTAL + GRAND TOTAL, UTF-8 BOM, plain numbers, via `archShareOrDownload` — the archive's own CSV export already existed and is untouched. (#9) `paintIfaTimeHints`: an empty block-time field explains itself — “Fetch fills this flight time from the schedule.” online, amber “Offline — type the flight time from your roster, or Fetch once connected.” offline; filled fields and hidden/collapsed blocks stay quiet; painted from `validateInputs` + `updateNetStatus`. (#10) bulk builds run `lucide.createIcons()` every 15 bindings so icons paint progressively (the end-of-build sweep still runs). (#12) flightless PDFs already explain themselves (“…doesn't look like a Crew Roster Report — I found no flight rows.”) — pinned with a test only, no What's-New entry. (#14) the empty chat input rotates three example asks every 4s (never while typing, focused, or hidden). (#15) `saveDevRates` stamps `crewAssist.ratesUpdatedAt`; the dev editor header shows “Rates last edited <ts>” (the net-status popover already carried “Rates last checked” — not duplicated). (#17) the archive month context now leads with the dollar MoM delta (“+$400.00 vs Apr 2026”, trending icon) with the YoY chip beside it; ctxLabel ‘Vs last year’→‘Context’; fallback “No <prev month> on record”. What's New: 12 entries, E6 format. Tests: 11 suites / 1171 asserts / 0 fail; sw v153. Harness: `boot(APP, { now })` installs a fixed page clock — earnings-archive's Sep-2026 anchor block runs frozen at 2026-09-20 and no longer breaks on real-date drift (its node-side anchor asserts read the same frozen instant).

**v1.32.1 (owner report, 4 fixes):** (E5) LMA breakdown figures match IFA size — the per-day cost chip and the B/L/D meal strip amounts step up from `ca-micro` to `text-sm` in BOTH render paths (calculate `dayRows` and the archive's `overviewDetailHtml`). (E6, owner-revised rule) What's New carries ONE CONCISE POINTER PER CHANGE (a release like v1.32.0 earns ~8 lines) — each line one sentence, <=12 words, still only the CURRENT release (v1.29.1 no-accumulate stands); dev-ui enforces the new format. (perf, owner report "Calculate all lags/hangs" on ~35-PDF batches) bulk-build mode: building 200+ cards ran document-wide passes per card — `lucide.createIcons`, `syncCalcUiAll`, FOUR `fitCalcPills` layout passes — plus a validation on every prefilled field (~213/card), i.e. O(cards²): minutes of frozen UI. Now `caBulkBuild` (counter, IIFE-scoped) gates every per-card document-wide pass; the LAST bind ends bulk mode and runs ONE sweep (icons once, one pill-fit, one UI sync, one validation per card via a dispatched `input` on each bubble); each card's own deferred 100ms validation runs even mid-batch (flag lifted around it — it is bubble-scoped) so cards arm right after their prefill; `rosterBuildCards(parsed, batch, silent, onDone)` reports completion and `rosterCalculateAll` is COMPLETION-DRIVEN (no more fixed 250ms guess) and computes in ~40ms slices with `setTimeout(0)` breathers. Measured on the 23-card real batch: createIcons 90+ calls -> 5. jsdom's nwsapi charges O(document) per scoped query so huge batches stay slow IN TESTS ONLY — Chrome scopes subtree queries, the phone cost is per-card constant. (paxing, owner report "some times could not be read" on 4 trips) the roster prints NO flight time for PU/TVL positioning sectors — the parser now marks `f.paxing = !!pos && !ft`, the trip gate exempts paxing sectors from the FT requirement, `rosterPrefillCard` pre-ticks the Paxing toggle (0.75x, SDP ignored), and the confirm bubble notes it, dated to the day and year (e.g. 'paxing (21 Oct 2024)'). The 4 real cases (SQ366 SIN-FCO Oct 2024, SQ828 SIN-PVG Apr 2025, SQ810 SIN-PKX Jun 2025, SQ298 CHC-SIN Feb 2026) now BUILD; FT lands from Fetch or hand entry — never estimated from STD/STA. Tests: 11 suites / 1112 asserts / 0 fail; sw v152.

**v1.32.0 (owner's third review — 8 of 10 shipped, M2 rain-checked at the 48h window, M4 audited clean):** (E1) every confirmed save — single summary or whole roster — can be UNDONE from its toast for TEN seconds (owner-picked window): the pre-write `loadArchive()` snapshot is held, Undo persists it back (new entries vanish, updates roll back), re-renders the archive if open, and re-arms the header save; `archToast(msg, undoFn, ms)` gained a duration arg and the 1200ms icon-rearm timer is cleared so it cannot fight the undo. (E2) the trash confirm names its scope — "Delete 6 shown entries?" when a search filters, "Delete all 312 entries?" when not — and `.ca-arch-headrule` divides the Import/Export pair from trash/close. (E3) `:focus-visible` paints a gold outline on every control except input/textarea/select (they keep their ring); `:focus-visible` never fires for thumb taps. (E4) chip order is allowance → earned → served → print (owner-specified): both headline actions on-screen at 360px with no scroll. (E5) money type ladder — micro 11 / body 14 / amount 17 / display 24 / hero 36 — with `.ca-amt` (tabular-nums) on every figure; gold-card total 34→36 (heroes unified), breakdown totals →17, review money 12→14, sticky bar 14. (E6) What's New house rule, enforced by a dev-ui assert: ONE sentence, plain words, ≤25 words, outcome not mechanism. (M1) surface tokens `--ca-surface-1` / `--ca-surface-2` / `--ca-hairline` (both themes) — all 35 `bg-black/5 dark:bg-white/5` pairs → `.ca-inset`, all 32 `border-black/5 dark:border-white/5` → `.ca-hairline`; identical rgba, zero visual change, one recipe per role for every future surface. (M3) the sheets keep their v1.31.0 modal promise: `caSheetFocusIn/Out` (stack-aware, document-capture keydown, top sheet only) moves focus into the sheet on open, wraps Tab/Shift+Tab at the boundaries, returns focus to the opener on close — wired into results (both open paths + close), settings, archive and the sub-sheet. LESSONS: (a) the first Tab-wrap condition was inverted for focus resting on a non-control (the sheet itself) — the jsdom trap test caught it before ship; (b) src-checks read comments — the token comment documenting the old recipe literally re-tripped the "no hand-mixed pairs" check; document migrations without the literal pattern.

**v1.31.2 (owner report):** the pinned totals bar clipped its text in half on device — `max-height: 44px` was a hand-guessed border-box cap and real font metrics (notably the OFFLINE FALLBACK face, which loads when Plus Jakarta Sans isn't cached) push the line box past it. Fix: cap raised to 96px (generous headroom — the max-height collapse trick only needs the cap to exceed content), `line-height: 1.2` pinned on the bar, `align-items: center` replaces baseline alignment (no cross-font metric games), horizontal padding 24px. LESSON: never hand-guess a max-height cap for text content — budget headroom or measure.

**v1.31.1 (owner device-pass fixes):** (1) the calc-interface segmented control never un-selected Default — the Default pill was MISSING its `id`, so `syncCalcUiPills()`'s `getElementById('calcui-pill-default')` returned null and skipped it; id added. (2) the new Block Time label follows its input in every mode (`.ifa-t-label` toggled together with `.ifa-time-input` in `syncCalcUiCard`). (3) `#ca-roster-sticky` moved OUT of the scroll content into the sheet itself (between the header row and `#results-content`) — flush under the header by construction, no padding gap; it now GROWS in place (max-height/padding transition, everything-moves) instead of fading, `hidden` for single summaries, populated + unhidden by `showRosterAllOverlay`, `content.onscroll` still toggles `.ca-on` at scrollTop>140. (4) all toasts (`archToast`) carry `border: 1.5px solid #d4af37`; dark mode inverts to a bright `#f6f1e8` pill with `#171d2b` text (owner: "#111 on dark barely read"); day mode keeps the dark pill; Undo uses `.ca-toast-undo` (bright gold on dark pill, ink gold on bright pill). (5) "Show all 12" now ANIMATES — the toggle flips `.ca-arch-insmore-hide` + rebuilds only the button innerHTML in place; the old toggle re-rendered the whole panel, which destroyed the wrapper and skipped the transition (an everything-moves violation, owner-caught). (6) owner bug report: SQ106 SIN-KUL + SQ105 KUL-SIN same day showed only ONE next-flight card row — `renderNextFlightCard` rendered `upcomingList()[0]` only; it now lists EVERY flight on the next flight's day (out-and-back = one duty), each row its own `.ca-nextflight-tap` (first row keeps the `#ca-nextflight-tap` id for compat), own Menus-saved badge, own fetch/open tap, header "N flights today/tomorrow" when >1. Owner's grammar QA guide delivered in chat (errors→modal, in-flow→inline panel, destructive→two-tap).

**v1.31.0 (owner-approved UX review — 11 of 15 items; 1/11/13/14 rain-checked):** (2) `#btn-results-action` is a labelled pill — icon + SAVE/SAVED micro text, `h-10 min-w-[88px]` so it NEVER changes shape between states (owner ruling); tour narration says "tap Save". (3+12) Save successes confirm via `archToast` (the global fixed toast), NOT appAlert — the review panel already carried the confirmation; failures keep the modal (grammar: informational outcomes → toast, errors + corrective guidance → modal, in-flow choices → inline panel, lossy chat actions → two-tap — locked). Also toast: dev-mode toggles, "Nothing to export yet" x3. (4) light-mode gold TEXT uses `--sia-gold-ink: #7d650f` (5.61:1 on white, 5.02:1 on black/5; dark keeps #c9a227) via `html:not(.dark) .text-sia-gold` + inline swaps; the always-dark toast Undo keeps bright gold explicitly. (5) chat-container is `role="log" aria-live="polite"`, typing indicator aria-hidden, four sheets are `role="dialog" aria-modal="true"`. (6) Settings has a "Learn the app → Replay" row (`btn-replay-tour` → closeSettings + startCaTour(true)); `finishCaTour(true)` ends a run cleanly. (7) chips `py-3.5` (~44px); `.ca-hit::after { inset:-5px }` invisible expansion on roster/send/settings/theme/reset/scroll/close buttons + `.ca-arch-iconbtn` + `.ca-arch-del`. (8) the IFA block-time input (the one placeholder-only field — LMA fields already had labels) gets a "Block Time" caption, placeholder now just "HH:MM". (9) Calculate-all summary: sticky `#ca-roster-sticky` totals bar (fades in at scrollTop>140 via `content.onscroll`), each trip header is a `.ca-trip-head` fold toggle (`.ca-tripsec`/`.ca-trip-body`/`.ca-trip-collapsed` — the archive group idiom, all expanded by default). (10) type floor: every `text-[9/9.5/10/11px]` (96 sites) is now the `.ca-micro` token (11px) — change the floor in ONE place. (15) insights are curated: top four per scope (month → streak, per-day, weekday, avg; else → top month, avg, streak, top flight), remaining eight in `.ca-arch-insmore` behind "Show all 12" (`archState.insightsAll`, reset on overlay open) — all twelve always render.

**v1.30.0 (owner ruling — the save review):** Tapping Save never writes straight away. `openSaveReviewPanel(content, payloads, onConfirm)` inserts a `#ca-save-review` panel above the summary: a counts line ("2 new · 1 updated · 1 no change") plus one `ca-save-revrow` per payload, tagged **New** (gold), **Updated** (amber, old amount struck through, "$old $new") or **No change** (muted — identical re-save, its own tag per owner ruling, never lumped with updates). **Confirm save** commits via the callback (the exact pre-existing save body), **Cancel** removes the panel + any station prompt and re-arms the header Save. `archiveSavePreview(payloads)` is the read-only sibling of `persistArchiveSaving` (same signature match). Both save paths are rewired: the single summary (station settled BEFORE the review so the preview reflects what will be filed — the station prompt's Save/Skip now opens the review) and Calculate all. GOTCHA: `setResultsAction(null)` clears the button's onclick — the review preserves and restores it, or a cancelled review / refused write leaves a dead-but-armed button (the onclick is kept across the hide). A refused write now re-arms via `setResultsAction('save', false)` (the old early-return left the button hidden after the review closed it). The tour's earnings chapter narrates the review between the save tap and the dialog (no new gate — gate numbering is asserted by tests). Stamps 1.30.0 / v147.

**v1.29.2 (owner spec — flight-based month progress):** `archMonthState(mk, entries)` replaces the old pace math (`archProjection` is GONE). A month's progress tracks its LAST FLIGHT — the latest `sectorDate` among its saved entries, plus the roster-remembered upcoming flights (`upcomingList()`) as the "more is coming" signal — never the last day of the calendar month. All flights flown -> **Completed** badge, no projected row, full solid bar, and the month-scope summary reads "Month total / Month complete", even with calendar days left. Flights still to be flown -> **In progress** badge + "Projected · N flights · $X" row reading the month's KNOWN schedule total (N = saved count, $ = month total), chart bar = solid FLOWN portion + striped un-flown remainder, tooltip "$flown → $total", summary "On track for $total · $unflown left". A future month (mk > current) with saved-but-unflown flights reads **Upcoming** (owner ruling — separate from In progress) with the same projection treatment. The all-time context's "proj. $X" chip uses the same state. YoY stays hidden for any in-progress month. Edge: a month that is in progress only via roster-remembered upcoming flights (nothing pre-saved) gets the badge but no projected row (nothing numeric to project) and a full solid bar.

**v1.29.1:** Month and Year scope follow the real calendar (`archScopeInfo` reads `todayLocalYMD()`, falling back to the newest entry's month when the current one has nothing saved, so the pill never shows a dead zero — owner ruling after the pill showed an early-saved future month); What's New carries ONLY the current release's note (never accumulate old entries — the sheet is "what changed in this version"); the armed discard button reads simply "Confirm" (no icon).

**v1.29.0:** three owner-approved upgrades. (1) **Tap-an-amount-to-correct:** every row's amount is a button (`data-amt`) — tap → inline `inputmode="decimal"` input, Enter/blur commits, Escape cancels, `archEditAmount` keeps the 6s undo toast; no more delete-and-redo for a typo. (2) **Broad search:** one haystack helper `archEntryMatches(e, q)` matches route, station display, month label + full month name, `sectorDate`, `flightType` and amount — used by BOTH `renderArch`'s list filter and `archFiltered` (scope/trash) so they can never disagree; "september", "2026-10", "777" and "layover" all find their entries. (3) **Save honesty:** `persistArchive` returns true/false — a refused write (quota/private mode) pops the honest alert "Couldn't save — your phone's storage is full or unavailable. The change wasn't saved." and both save acks (single + Calculate-all bulk) SKIP the "saved" claim and leave the save button armed for a retry instead of one-shotting. The tour store path stays silent.

### Backup nudge (v1.27.0; insights first-tap fix v1.27.1)

Earnings live only in `localStorage` — the archive now reminds the owner to export. `archBackupNudgeInfo(all)` (called from `renderArch`) shows an amber card at the top of the list when: **never backed up** (no `crewAssist.lastBackupAt`) and ≥2 months of entries, **or** last JSON export ≥21 days ago with ≥1 month of entries newer than that stamp. Snooze (×) writes `crewAssist.backupNudgeSnoozedAt` for 2 weeks; the card collapses away animated before the re-render (everything-moves). The Export button opens the same export sheet as the toolbar. `archDoExport('json')` stamps `crewAssist.lastBackupAt` (and clears the snooze) — CSV exports deliberately do NOT count as a backup. Tests in `earnings-archive.test.js`: never-backed-up card + copy, animated snooze, 2-day-fresh suppression, 30-day-stale card with month count, export stamps + clears, single-month quiet. **v1.27.1 in the same suite:** the insights panel must react to the FIRST tap with no leftover `hidden` class (the v1.27.0 regression), re-renders keep it open, and closing+reopening the earnings page resets the toggle fully (label, chevron, aria) — `showArchiveOverlay` now resets those explicitly.

### Next-flight awareness (v1.28.0; direct-open + launcher v1.28.2)

The roster import now persists upcoming flights (localStorage `crewAssist.upcoming`, grouped by monthKey) and the greeting uses them. All local — no server, no push.

- **Parser: positioning duties.** `RO_POS` = PU/PN/IPU/IPN/DU/DN/IDU/IDN + TVL. Verified against every real roster in `_inbox`: the pairing duty code sits on its own line (y~254) at the same x as the day column it positions, so the rotated-table x-clustering picks it up as `vals.pos` → `f.pos = true` on the flight. Timed TVL rows (e.g. PN · SQ298 CHC-SIN 1200→1735) otherwise parse as ordinary flights. Trip display is untouched — the tag only filters awareness. No-time TVL rows were already skipped by the no-times guard.
- **Store.** `upcomingRemember(parsed)` (called from `handleRosterFiles`, so the tour demo never writes it): keeps flights with a date + number, drops `pos`, dedupes fn|ymd, **replaces every month the import covers** (re-import fixes a changed roster), prunes past days at every read (`upcomingList()` = flat, sorted ymd+std).
- **Next-flight card.** `renderNextFlightCard()` rides the greeting's bot queue (`enqueueBot`), so it lands under the greeting bubbles. Shows SQ number, route, date, local departs time, relative label ("today / tomorrow / in N days" — dates only, no timezone math). Green **✓ Menus saved** badge when the `SQ<fn>:<ymd>:CABINS` cache exists. **Tap opens the menus directly (v1.28.2)** — the flight-verification card is no longer part of this flow and `renderFlightVerificationCard` lost its prefill second arg (existing callers unchanged).
- **Offline pre-save nudge.** `renderMenuNudge()`: flights within 48h (date-granular window `today..+2`) that are operating, un-cached and un-dismissed → one amber card. **v1.28.2 design (owner-locked):** plane icon inline-leading, text on top, rows below, ONE centered button (`#ca-menunudge-go` in a `flex justify-center` row), × absolute top-right. Single flight: "SQ nnn to XXX departs tomorrow — open its menus now…" + **[Open menus]** → the unified direct-open (fetches EVERY cabin, not just the guessed one). Multiple (e.g. 3 turnaround days): "You have N flights in the next 2 days" + one row per flight + **[Save all menus]**. The sweep (`saveMenusForFlightOffline`, now returns `{ ok, reason, cabins, saved }` and never throws; 101 → `reason:'unpublished'`) fetches getcabin + every cabin's menu via `fetchMenuForPrinter` (which caches), and calls `rememberRecentFlight` per flight so the prune-to-5 keeps them. **Sweep = save-only, no auto-open (owner's pick):** rows tick "N/M saved" emerald with chevrons as they complete; on full success the amber card transforms in place into a green **launcher** (`id` renamed to `ca-menulaunch-card` — the rename detaches it from the nudge lifecycle so re-renders can't reap it; emerald restyle via a `data-nfbox` hook, the button fades to `pointer-events:none`, `renderNextFlightCard()` runs once more for the badge) reading "Menus saved for N flights — tap one to open."; failed rows stay red on the amber card with no refresh, retry via the button (cached flights skip the refetch). Only ever runs from an explicit tap — never auto-fetches. × dismisses per fn|ymd (`crewAssist.nudgeDismissed`), animated collapse. Offline → no card; the existing `online` listener re-checks (`nextFlightChatActive()` guards appProfile + main view).
- **Unified direct-open (v1.28.2).** `openNextFlightMenus(fn, ymd, setBusy)` is the ONE path behind both the card tap and the single-flight nudge button: **cached** → `openNextFlightViewer(fn, ymd)` opens the viewer straight from cache (works offline / mid-flight); **not cached + online** → spinner ("Fetching every cabin for SQ nnn…") while `saveMenusForFlightOffline` caches the schedule + every cabin, then the viewer opens; **not cached + offline** → honest bubble "…and you're offline — connect once and tap again…" and no viewer; a `menuDateSide` guard tripping surfaces `appAlert(menuDateFetchError(side))`; unpublished → "not published yet" notice. `openNextFlightViewer` primes `currentMenuSearch` (flightNumber, date, cabins, aircraftType from the saved schedule — FCL label needs it) then calls `openMenuViewerForCabins`. NOTE: both functions live in the **menu-viewer script block** (next to `currentMenuSearch`) — top-level `let`s do not cross `<script>` blocks under jsdom's per-block evals (they do in real browsers), so a cross-block `let` reference passes on the phone and crashes the suite.
- **Tests** (`tests/next-flight.test.js`, 56 asserts — incl. the v1.28.4 fast-lane timing assert; `tour.test.js` pins the classic-pace exemption): parser tagging (PU/PN/DU/bare TVL vs clean), store prune/replace/dedupe/pos-exclusion, card render + copy + relative label, saved badge, the three tap paths (cached → viewer directly, offline-safe; online-unsaved → CABINS + EVERY cabin key cached then viewer; offline-unsaved → honest notice, no viewer), plane-icon/no-wifi-off/text-above-button/centered-button layout asserts, nudge appear/dismiss/consolidate/sweep (stubbed live API; asserts CABINS + per-cabin keys + recentFlights), sweep-end launcher transform (copy, inert button, tappable row → cached viewer), offline boot + online-event recovery, expired roster dormant. Harness notes: the v1.28.4 fast lane makes non-tour bubbles ~0.7–0.8s each (dots 150–250ms + 320ms anim + 250ms gap) — wait ≥3s or use the suite's `waitChatIdle` before clicking chat-guarded buttons (the old ≥5.5s waits still work, just generously); a fast-lane timing assert pins the card to <3.2s. Seeds must set `crewAssist.tourDone`/`tourOffered` or the first-run tour offer keeps the queue busy.

### Roster PDF import (v1.20.0; multi-month stitching v1.20.1; Calculate all v1.21.0; Flight Overview + tappable archive v1.22.0)

Paperclip `#btn-roster` (left of `#chat-input`) + hidden `#roster-file-input` (accept `application/pdf,.pdf`, multiple) + intent `/roster|upload|import/` all open the picker. pdf.js 3.11.174 lazy-loads from cdnjs on first use (`ensurePdfJs`; `workerSrc` set after load, `standardFontDataUrl` passed to `getDocument`); corrupt/password PDFs and non-roster PDFs produce clean bubbles via `handleRosterFiles`. Parsing is 100% on-device.

**Multi-month batches (v1.20.1):** `handleRosterFiles` parses each picked PDF, dedupes by `monthLabel` (duplicates get a skip bubble naming the file), sorts by month, and for 2+ unique months stitches the raw item lists with `rosterStitchItems` before one `rosterParse` — so a trip crossing a month boundary (out on the 29th, back on the 2nd) builds complete instead of being flagged open-ended in one file and mid-trip in the next. Stitching renumbers pages continuously (`pageOff += maxPage` per file) because rows and date anchors key on (page, x) and every PDF restarts at page 1. `renderRosterConfirm(parsed, fileName, displayMonth)` takes a third label arg ('June 2026 – July 2026'); without it the parsed month is shown, so single-month callers are unaffected.

**Flight Overview + tappable archive (v1.22.0):** every COP summary (single-trip and each trip section in Calculate all) opens with a plain-English paragraph in the assistant's voice — flight numbers, flight times, how IFA was derived (SDPs, buffers, bracket, rank rate), landing/departure date-times at each station, the LMA meal story, and the totals. `computeCardResults` now also returns a structured `detail` snapshot (type, sectors with fn/ft/SDP/mult/amount/dep/arr/dates/logic, bonus, stations with in/out times and per-day meals, meal money split, totals, rank rate) — frozen at calculate time so later rate edits never rewrite a saved summary; the snapshot also rides on `archivePayload.detail` and survives JSON import (`archNormalize` passes it through). `flightOverviewText(detail)` is the ONE generator used by both live summaries and the archive reopen, so they can never disagree; `overviewBlock()` wraps it with the sparkles label, `overviewDetailHtml()` rebuilds the IFA/LMA breakdown from the snapshot. Owner-locked formats: dates read `Sat 5th Sep 1727H` (`ovDay`/`ovDT`/`ovTime`), SDP stays a term, rank rate shows two decimals, bonus/full-day counts spell small numbers, overnight turnarounds (sector-2 departs another day) are described as such, and the Direct US line only ever names SIN–US–SIN. Honest fallbacks: unknown regions, shuttle grounds, incomplete stations and paxing sectors each get their own sentence. `rosterPrefillCard` also stamps `data-dep/arr-airport` on each IFA sector block (feeds the overview, trip bars and the archive station display for turnarounds). Archive rows are now tappable (chevron hint) → `openArchEntry` reopens the whole summary in the archive's bottom sub-sheet (gold total, Flight Overview, both breakdowns, saved-at footer); the X still deletes straight away with the undo toast and never opens the sheet. Pre-v1.22.0 entries say so honestly ("Saved before detailed summaries"). The sub-sheet itself now animates (fade + slide, `openArchSub`/`closeArchSub` with a shade class hook — never querySelector an escaped Tailwind class like `bg-black/50`, it throws). Nepal was missing from `countryToRegion` (the airports list had KTM under a South Asia comment but the runtime map never did) — added `NP: "South Asia"`, so KTM layovers now earn LMA instead of skipping.

**Calculate all (v1.21.0):** a third footer button (gold-outlined, under Build/Discard) is the automation path: one tap builds the cards (if not built yet) and calculates every trip, then shows ONE combined summary — gold grand-total header ("Grand Total — N trips"), one section per trip (route, dates, T/A or Layover badge, trip total, the card's own IFA/LMA breakdowns, the honest Turnaround-LMA note). `renderRosterConfirm` keeps a `batch = { ids, built }` closure; `rosterBuildCards(parsed, batch, silent)` records each `{ id, trip }` as cards bind, so Calculate all targets exactly this bubble's cards. `rosterCalculateAll` waits ~250ms for the bind/prefill timeouts, skips cards whose Calculate is still grey (reported as skipped in the overlay — never half-calculated), folds each calculated card like a manual Calculate, and calls `showRosterAllOverlay`. The compute core lives in `computeCardResults(id, mode)` (split out of `executeCalculation`, which is now a thin wrapper) — there is no second math path; the combined total is the sum of the per-card results. The combined Save files every trip as its own archive entry in one tap (same entries as saving each summary by hand; roster cards carry their stations so the single-summary station prompt doesn't apply; one-shot tick identical to a single summary). Spend semantics: Build spends only Build+Discard — Calculate all stays available so the crew can hand-check some cards first; Calculate all and Discard spend the whole footer. **Discard is two-tap (v1.29.0, owner design):** the first tap arms a red `ca-twotap-armed` confirm state ("Tap again to discard", 5s disarm, `armTwoTap`/`disarmTwoTap` helpers) and spends NOTHING; the second tap discards. The chat's clear-chat button (#btn-reset) uses the same helper — an accidental tap must not wipe a session with built, unsaved cards. All other destructive actions were already guarded (clear-all + dev-rates reset via appConfirm, archive deletes via undo) and are unchanged. `appProfile.rank` reads are guarded (`(appProfile && baseRates[...]) || 13.5`) — the profile-less state used to crash any Calculate.

**Live FileList trap (v1.20.1 fix):** in real browsers `input.value = ''` empties `e.target.files` **in place** — store the list, clear the value, and the stored list's `length` reads 0, so the handler no-ops (exactly the owner's "nothing happens" report). Always snapshot first: `Array.from(e.target.files || [])` *before* clearing. jsdom never trips this when tests call `handleRosterFiles` directly, so the regression test simulates the liveness: a `files` getter whose `length` reads whether the value has been cleared yet.

**How the report is built (reverse-engineered from six real months):** the page is rotated 90° — in raw pdf.js item coordinates **x = row position, y = column position** (22pt row pitch). The generator splits a column's text runs mid-table (same visual column, slightly different y), so: fn/sector values match globally (their shapes are unique); date anchors come from the dominant-y cluster of date-shaped items (excludes the `From --` header dates); STD/STA/FT values go to their nearest header anchor ('STD' / 'STA' / 'Flight'+'Time') within ±16, never 'Rpt'. Rows cluster by (page, x) tol 3; a row's date is the latest anchor at-or-above it, carried across page breaks.

**Echo rows:** overnight/midnight flights appear twice (operating row + landing-day row) and the echo never repeats STD/STA/FT — it only complements them. Merge rule: same flight+sector, immediate next flight row, ≤2-day gap, complementary times. A repeated operation later in the month carries its own complete times and stays a separate flight (Aug 2026's SQ 37 LAX-SIN merges across THREE rows — op + two echoes).

**Trip rules:** chain sectors until arrival at SIN; same-day back-to-back SIN turnarounds merge into one 4-sector trip; Layover iff any intermediate ground >6h (same threshold as `suggestShuttle`), else Turnaround; LMA stations = consecutive stops, in = inbound STA + its row date (same-row STA < STD rolls +1), out = outbound STD. After-midnight departures carry the STD row's date (flight date, not duty date). 2-sector layovers touching a US airport (`isUsAirport`) pre-tick Direct US. Mid-trip starts, month-end open trips and >4-sector loops are flagged in the bubble, not built. Non-flying days = date anchors with no flight rows (no duty-code breakdown — the duty column splits and legend pages make it unreliable).

**Prefill path:** `renderCalculatorCard(mode, onReady)` takes an optional callback fired after `bindCalculatorEvents` (roster build is its only caller). `rosterPrefillCard` clicks the flight-type / sector-count pills first (they rebuild the scaffolding), then writes every value through the calendar-commit path (value → `syncDateBtn` → `input` → `change`), dates in 1..N order so each cascade guess is overwritten by the next real date. LMA stations are added with the `__caLmaQuietAdd` fetch idiom and marked `data-arr/dep-state` done. Turnaround cards skip LMA (that section is hidden for them anyway).

**Privacy:** real rosters live in `docs/_reference_rosters/` as permanently committed reference fixtures — **owner order 2026-10-07** (the old protocol kept them in an untracked `_inbox/` and purged them after sign-off; the owner overruled it so a fresh clone runs the full suite). The synthetic rosters in `tests/roster-pdf.test.js` still cover the parser geometry on their own.

### Pre-history (v1.0.0 → v1.5.8 — owner-supplied 2026-10-01)

Everything before the v1.5.9 sign-off predates this handover file. The owner supplied that history in chat on 2026-10-01; it is now the oldest 25 entries of `APP_CHANGELOG` in `index.html` (v1.0.0 → v1.5.8) — **`index.html` is the source of truth for it**; this section is a map, not a copy. Shape of the run: **v1.0.0** the PWA scaffold (starry-sky canvas, SIA palette, gold paper-plane logo, light/dark toggles, offline install) → **v1.1.x** onboarding (name/gender/rank), the settings bottom sheet, Feedback & Support with the Telegram button, hidden Developer Mode behind ten logo taps → **v1.2.x** the chat-first home screen (quick-action chips, keyword parser, time-aware greeting) → **v1.3.x** COP/IFA/LMA chat forms with auto-formatting time inputs, the local IATA airport database, SIA business rules (IFA/LMA engines, cascading layover dates, multi-station routing) and the glassmorphic summary with day-by-day B/L/D → **v1.4.0–1.4.29** the Vercel Edge proxy + flight-verification chat flow, the full-screen inflight-menu sheet with the Route Hero and Meals/Drinks/Snacks/Amenities tabs, the shared navy-and-gold button system, safe HTML insertion + mobile scroll staging, and the printer/viewer pair (signed off v1.4.24) → **v1.5.x** the What’s New overlay, per-sector Fetch, per-station Shuttle, the CrewAssist calendar replacing the system date picker, opaque light-theme surfaces and LMA timezone parsing — ending at the **v1.5.9** sign-off baseline this file picks up from. Pointer-level detail for every one of these versions is in `APP_CHANGELOG`, release dates included: v1.0.0 = 4 Sept 2026 (inception) → the v1.5.0–v1.5.4 run = 14 Sept → the v1.5.9 sign-off = 15 Sept 2026 (owner-supplied, hotfix 4).

## Product dead ends — do not retry

- **Android share-target roster import — shipped in v1.27.0, REMOVED in v1.27.1 at the owner's direction.** The owner prefers the paperclip (`#btn-roster` → `#roster-file-input`) as the only roster entry point. Do NOT re-introduce `share_target`/`file_handlers` in manifest.json, the SW POST/multipart stash, or a boot-time pickup — the paperclip flow is the spec.
- Prefixing SQ relative images with `/assets/` 404ed thumbs.
- Nested parent `url` / `path` / `src` scans assigned junk. `data-src` / `revealMenuImages` left thumbs without `src`.
- Trusting HTTP status for IFE photos fails (200 HTML).
- html2canvas for compact invert/header (1.4.19–1.4.21).
